<!-- inogile:context-version=3 -->
# Technical relationship map — cleaning-software @ main (vue-skeleton fork)

## Project type

laravel-be + inertia-fe (phase 2 tenancy complete: multi-tenant row-level scoping, tenant switcher, invitations)

## Stack snapshot

**Backend:** Laravel 13.7 (PHP 8.5) + Inertia 3.0.6 (Vue 3 + TS + Tailwind 4 + DaisyUI 5) · Spatie Data 4.22 / Permission 7.4.1 (teams=true, team_foreign_key=tenant_id) / Activitylog 5.0 (uuid morphs) / MediaLibrary 11.22 / QueryBuilder 7.2 / TypeScript-Transformer 3 · Sanctum 4.3.2 (Bearer API + uuidMorphs fix applied 2026-09-06) · Scribe 5.9 · league/flysystem-aws-s3-v3 ^3 (documents disk `s3`, unverified against a real bucket) · symfony/html-sanitizer ^8 (DocumentHtmlSanitizer) · Postgres 16 (compose) · PHPUnit 12 on Postgres `cleanmaster_admin_testing` (phpunit.xml force=true, init SQL docker/postgres/init-testing-db.sql) · test count: see CLAUDE.md · Docker: `docker compose exec app php artisan …`

**Mobile + AI (Phase 11):** laravel/ai ^0.7.2 (Anthropic provider, `AI_MODEL` default `claude-sonnet-5-5`, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` backend only; the AI task run is the one queued AI call, queue `ai` from `AI_TASK_RUN_QUEUE`, 2026-11-01) · laravel/mcp ^0.7 (`/mcp` web server with Bearer token works; the `cleanmaster` local stdio server is registered but answers `Unauthenticated.`) · kreait/laravel-firebase ^7.2 (FCM via `FirebasePushSender`, `FIREBASE_CREDENTIALS`) · qpdf binary (`QpdfMerger`, bulk invoice PDF).

**Frontend:** @inertiajs/vue3 ^3.0.3 + Vue ^3.5.33 + TS ^6 strict + Vite 8 + laravel-vite-plugin ^3 · NO Pinia · NO Ziggy (0 route() calls; all URLs string literals) · i18n = vue-i18n ^11 (legacy:false), messages bundled from resources/lang/{sk,en}/app.json + locale from Inertia prop (sk default; sk/en/uk) · Theme: DaisyUI 5 themes:false + [data-theme='app-theme'] OKLCH token block in resources/css/app.css; Instrument Sans via bunny.net · Icons @heroicons/vue · Dates date-fns (utils/date.ts) · @tiptap 3.23.x (starter-kit, link, image, table, text-align, text-style, highlight; the document editor under Components/Documents) · ESLint 10 flat (no ref ban yet), Prettier, Lefthook pre-commit + pre-push vue-tsc. FE test runner: vitest ^4 + happy-dom (`pnpm test`, pure TS specs, no @vue/test-utils).

## Domains

### tenancy — multi-tenant isolation, invitations, team management (Phase 2, 2026-09-06)

**Core:**
- App\Models\Tenant — UUIDv7; traits HasFactory, HasUuids, InteractsWithMedia, LogsActivity (logOnly owner_id,name,ico,dic,vat_number,is_vat_payer,vat_rate,iban,is_active,swift_bic,invoice_number_format,registration_info,representative_name,signing_place,signature_media_id,address_line,city,postal_code,country,contact_email,contact_phone,peppol_participant_id), SoftDeletes. Columns: owner_id FK → users (restrictOnDelete), name, ico (nullable), dic (nullable), vat_number (nullable), is_vat_payer (bool), vat_rate (decimal), iban (nullable), swift_bic (nullable), invoice_number_format, registration_info, address_line, city, postal_code, country, contact_email, contact_phone, representative_name (nullable, signatory printed on contract text; edited via InvoiceSettingsData / Settings->Invoicing), signing_place (varchar 100 nullable, 2026-11-03, printed on quotes), **signature_media_id** FK (nullable, bigint) → media (nullOnDelete), is_active, timestamps, soft_deletes. Relations: owner BelongsTo User, interface HasOne TenantInterface, memberships HasMany TenantMembership, members BelongsToMany User (via tenant_memberships, pivot is_active/joined_at), signatureMedia BelongsTo Media (signature_media_id). Methods: `registerMediaCollections(): void` → `$this->addMediaCollection('signature')->useDisk(config('invoicing.signature.disk', 'local'))` (NOT singleFile — superseded rows retained so issued invoices keep resolving), (`logo` collection removed 2026-11-05), `missingSupplierFields(): array` (list required fields still empty: name/address_line/city/postal_code/ico + dic/vat_number when is_vat_payer; returns [] when complete; FE contract for banner label keys), `hasCompleteSupplierProfile(): bool` (shortcut === [] guard).
- App\Models\TenantMembership — UUIDv7 pivot; traits HasFactory, HasUuids, LogsActivity (logOnly is_active,position,first_name,last_name,user_id,employee_type). Columns: user_id FK (NULLABLE since 2026-11-01 migration 2026_11_06_000100: NULL = employee without app login, deviates from the rule that every membership has a user; restrictOnDelete), tenant_id FK (restrictOnDelete), is_active (bool), joined_at (timestamp), first_name (nullable), last_name (nullable), phone (nullable), position (nullable), employee_type (nullable string, EmployeeTypeEnum), contact_email (nullable, only while user_id is NULL), company_name (nullable), timestamps. Unique (user_id, tenant_id); indices (tenant_id, is_active), (user_id). NOT BelongsToTenant (the pivot defines tenancy itself). Relations: user BelongsTo User, tenant BelongsTo Tenant.
- App\Models\TenantInterface — UUIDv7 settings per tenant (D1); traits HasUuids, LogsActivity (logOnly incl. recurring_default_state, default texts, default_quote_included_text). Columns: tenant_id FK unique (restrictOnDelete), color (TenantColorEnum nullable, integer in DB), recurring_default_state (RecurringDefaultStateEnum, default 'draft'; the old invoice_template column was dropped by 2026_11_03_000100), default_constant_symbol (varchar 50 nullable), default_payment_type (PaymentTypeEnum, default 'bank_transfer'), default_currency (CurrencyEnum, default 'EUR'), default_rounding_mode (RoundingModeEnum, default 'half_up'), invoice_number_format (varchar 255, default '{number}'), vat_rate (decimal 5,2, default 20.00), **default_header_text (text nullable, phase 10)**, **default_footer_text (text nullable, phase 10)**, default_quote_included_text (text nullable, max 2000, 2026-11-03), timestamps. Relation: tenant BelongsTo.
- App\Models\TenantInvitation — UUIDv7; traits BelongsToTenant, HasFactory, HasUuids, LogsActivity (logOnly email,role_name,status,expires_at,accepted_at, never token), SoftDeletes. Columns: tenant_id FK (restrictOnDelete), invited_by_user_id FK (nullOnDelete), email, role_name (100), token (64 unique), status (InvitationStatusEnum), expires_at, accepted_at (nullable), timestamps, soft_deletes. Indices (tenant_id, status); partial unique (tenant_id, email) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND status='pending'. Relations: invitedBy BelongsTo User, tenant BelongsTo (inherited scope). Methods: isAcceptable() (checks expiry + pending status), markAccepted().
- App\Services\RegistrationService (final readonly) — `createOwner(name, email, password, companyName, ico, ?TenantSupplierProfileData $supplier = null): User` → DB::transaction, User→Tenant (with supplier data if provided)→TenantInterface→TenantMembership→RoleTemplatesSeeder→Admin role assignment. `addTenant(User, AddTenantData, ?Tenant $copyFrom = null): Tenant` → bootstrapTenant with the supplier profile from the DTO. Invoice-defaults source = `$copyFrom ?? (copy_settings ? active tenant : null)`. Colour copied from the source only when `$copyFrom` is null (web path); with `$copyFrom` (legacy import) colour = data.colour only. `private bootstrapTenant(User, name, ico, color?, ?TenantSupplierProfileData $supplier = null): Tenant` — atomically seeds Tenant (supplier columns from DTO or defaults) + interface + membership + team-scoped role bundle.
- App\Services\InvitationAcceptService — `resolve(token): TenantInvitation` (withoutGlobalScope), `accept(invitation, AcceptInvitationData, skipPasswordCheck?): User` — transaction: abort if non-acceptable (410), hash-check existing user (or create+verify new user), setPermissionsTeamId(invitation.tenant_id), create/reactivate membership, assign role, markAccepted, return user. **Critical:** role lookup happens inside the invitation's tenant context, not session tenant.
- App\Services\RoleAssignmentGuard (final readonly) — `assertAssignable(User $actor, iterable<Role> $roles): void` — validates each role's permission set ⊆ actor's permissions; throws ValidationException if violated (privilege escalation guard for UserService::create/update).
- App\Http\Middleware\TenantContextMiddleware — app middleware (appended after LocaleMiddleware on web; **prepended before `SubstituteBindings` via `bootstrap/app.php` `prependToPriorityList`**, so route-model binding runs under the resolved tenant — security fix `6f08aa9`, absent on `main`). `handle` resolves tenantId per D5: X-Tenant-Id header (if uuid + active membership, else 403 `tenant_forbidden`) → session active_tenant_id → first active membership (**for a `mobile` token: first membership whose `TenantVisibility::mobileAccess` is true**). If resolved: `app()->instance('current_tenant_id', $id)`, `setPermissionsTeamId($id)`, session bind. **Phase 11:** `mobile` token in a resolved tenant without `access mobile app` → 403 `mobile_forbidden`. Unauth users pass through (no binding).
- App\Http\Middleware\RequireActiveTenant — route middleware (`tenant.required`). Authenticated + no bound tenant → web: `Auth::logout()`, invalidate session, redirect login with error flash; API: 403 JSON `no_active_tenant`. Logout route still reachable (not gated).
- App\Scopes\TenantScope — global scope on BelongsToTenant models. When `app()->bound('current_tenant_id')`: `where tenant_id = ?`; else unfiltered (D6: console/jobs/seeders run intentionally unbound). **Phase 11 exceptions to the BelongsToTenant invariant:** `Task` (own `TaskScope`, nullable `tenant_id`, see domain `tasks`), `Notification` (nullable `tenant_id` for system messages), `DeviceToken` (per person, no `tenant_id`, see domain `push`).
- App\Concerns\BelongsToTenant — trait: `bootBelongsToTenant()` adds TenantScope + creating hook fills tenant_id from container; `tenant(): BelongsTo<Tenant,$this>`.

**Satellites (BE):**
- DTOs: Tenants/{AddTenantData (#[TypeScript]: full supplier profile fields + colour + copy_settings), TenantListItemData, TenantSupplierProfileData, TenantSwitchData (#[TypeScript]: redirect_to relative path optional, regex validator rejects absolute/proto URLs)}, Invitations/{AcceptInvitationData, InvitationAcceptPageData}, Auth/MeData, PermissionGroupData (grouped permission list for forms). TenantSupplierProfileData (#[TypeScript]): full supplier identity input (address_line, city, postal_code, country, dic, vat_number, is_vat_payer, contact_email, contact_phone, iban, swift_bic; name not included, handled by Tenant directly); two constants IBAN_PATTERN and SWIFT_BIC_PATTERN (regex, reused by InvoiceSettingsData for single-source-of-truth); `toTenantAttributes()` returns dict for Tenant::create; used by RegistrationService::createOwner + CreateOwner command + AddTenantData::toSupplierProfile(). AddTenantData: name, ico (required), dic/vat_number/is_vat_payer/address_line/city/postal_code/country/contact_email/contact_phone/iban/swift_bic (optional), colour (nullable TenantColorEnum), copy_settings (bool); `toSupplierProfile()` method converts to TenantSupplierProfileData. TenantSwitchData: optional redirect_to param for POST /tenants/{id}/switch (used to redirect after switch instead of dashboard default).
- Enums: PermissionEnum (67 backed string cases, #[TypeScript]; 2026-11-01 `use assistant` + `run tasks with ai`; 2026-11-01 `manage employee_access`), TenantColorEnum (8 hex, #[TypeScript]), InvitationStatusEnum (pending/accepted/revoked/expired), InvitationAcceptStateEnum (4 states for FE: expired/wrong_user/existing_user/new_user), SupportedLanguage (now sk/en/uk with `getDefault()`, `getDisplayName()`, `isSupported()`).
- Factories: TenantFactory (forOwner), TenantMembershipFactory (withProfile, inactive states), TenantInvitationFactory (accepted, expired, revoked states), TenantInterfaceFactory.
- Seeders: PermissionSeeder (global catalogue from PermissionEnum), RoleTemplatesSeeder (per-tenant role bundles: Admin=all, Vedúca, Sekretárka, Účtovníčka, Interná upratovačka, Zákazník; `seedForTenant(Tenant)` idempotent, called post-bootstrap).
- Policies: TenantPolicy::create(User) = `users.is_platform_admin` (a new tenant has no roles yet, so a permission check would be circular; the flag replaces the former D4a no-policy exception, 2026-10-31); TenantPolicy::switchTo(User, Tenant) = active membership + active tenant.
- Notifications: InvitationCreated (ShouldQueue, afterCommit, #[Tries(3)], mail-only, token + tenant_name + role_name in body).
- Commands: CreateOwner (interactive prompts or flags: --name, --email, --password, --company, --ico + 11 optional supplier flags --address-line, --city, --postal-code, --country (default SK), --dic, --vat-number, --vat-payer (value-less), --contact-email, --contact-phone, --iban, --swift; validates via TenantSupplierProfileData::validateAndCreate before any DB write; calls RegistrationService::createOwner with built DTO).
- Routes: POST /tenants (`#[Authorize('create', Tenant::class)]` -> TenantPolicy::create, 403 unless platform admin; accepts AddTenantData), POST /tenants/{tenant}/switch (TenantPolicy, accepts TenantSwitchData with optional redirect_to param, redirects to $data->redirect_to ?? '/'), GET|POST /invitations/{token} (guest, throttle:invitation-accept on POST), GET /api/me (in the `/api` group, see Cross-cutting "Mobile API surface"; returns MeData with active tenant + permissions per team scope).
- Middleware: TenantContextMiddleware (app-level, D5 resolution), RequireActiveTenant (route `tenant.required`), plus bootstrap/app.php registers aliases.

**FE Satellites (apply via FE agent plan phase-2-tenancy-fe.md):**
- Composables: usePageProps (ComputedRef<SharedProps>), useAuthorization (allows(PermissionEnum)).
- Components: TenantColorDot, TenantSwitcher (dropdown), AddTenantModal, ColorSwatchPicker, Can (permission guard).
- Pages: Invitations/Accept (4 states: expired/wrong_user/existing_user/new_user), Users/Index/Form (tenant members only, updated filters/forms).
- Shared props: tenant{active,available}, tenantColors, can (camelCase keys per PermissionEnum::sharedKey).

**Flow:**
- **Create owner (bootstrap):** `php artisan app:create-owner` → prompts name/email/password/company/ico → RegistrationService::createOwner → User + Tenant + TenantInterface + TenantMembership + seeded roles + Admin role assigned → logs "created successfully". Bootstrap flow only.
- **Add tenant:** Platform admin only (`users.is_platform_admin`, others 403; shared prop `canCreateTenant` hides the TenantSwitcher entry + AddTenantModal trigger otherwise; `app:create-owner` bypasses the route) POST /tenants (AddTenantData: name, ico required; full supplier profile optional: dic, vat_number, is_vat_payer, address_line, city, postal_code, country=SK, contact_email, contact_phone, iban, swift_bic; colour optional, copy_settings bool) → RegistrationService::addTenant(user, data, ?copyFrom = null) → source = copyFrom ?? (copy_settings true ? active tenant : null), web path colour defaults to source colour if set else data.colour (copyFrom given: data.colour only) → bootstrapTenant(user, name, ico, colour, data.toSupplierProfile()) → new Tenant (owned by actor, populated with supplier fields from DTO) → TenantInterface → active membership (actor + Admin role) → if copy_settings: copyInvoiceDefaults(source, target) copies invoice_template/recurring_default_state/default_constant_symbol/default_payment_type/default_currency/default_rounding_mode from TenantInterface + invoice_number_format/vat_rate from Tenant (NOT supplier identity) → session switch to new tenant → TenantController::store: if tenant.hasCompleteSupplierProfile() → redirect dashboard with `tenant_created` flash, else → redirect to_route('settings.invoicing') with `tenant_created_complete_supplier` flash (D1: onboard actor to complete profile before invoicing). Gated by TenantPolicy::create. No leader invitation created.
- **Switch tenant:** User POST /tenants/{tenant}/switch → TenantPolicy::switchTo gates (membership + active) → session bind active_tenant_id → Inertia redirect dashboard → shared props refresh (tenant, can, navigation).
- **Accept invitation:** Guest or logged-in GET /invitations/{token} → resolves InvitationAcceptPageData (4 states) → InvitationAcceptForm if form-state; POST /invitations/{token} (AcceptInvitationData: password, name optional, throttle 5/min) → InvitationAcceptService::accept (existing: Hash::check; new: create+verify) → Auth::login → session bind inviting tenant → redirect dashboard + flash. Expired/accepted/revoked → 410. Unknown token → 404.
- **API /api/me:** GET (auth:sanctum + tenant.context) → MeData{userId, activeTenantId, permissions[]} (per resolved tenant team scope via PermissionRegistrar::setPermissionsTeamId).

**Depends on:** Spatie Permission (teams=true, team_foreign_key=tenant_id), Spatie Activitylog, mailer (InvitationCreated notification).

**Depended on by:** Every tenant-scoped model (identity, auth, audit-logs, media, localisation) inherits BelongsToTenant + TenantScope; all services call setPermissionsTeamId; controllers gate via TenantPolicy / model policies + app()-bound current_tenant_id; legacy-import (addTenant with `$copyFrom`, ToolContext::run per firm).

**If you change Core, check:**
- PermissionEnum consumers: PermissionSeeder, RoleTemplatesSeeder, PermissionGroupData, PermissionManager (FE), Inertia shared can keys, #[Authorize] routes, NavItem permissions.
- TenantColorEnum consumers: TenantInterface casting, AddTenantModal (FE), Inertia tenantColors prop, CSS --color-primary override (FE).
- TenantSupplierProfileData::IBAN_PATTERN / SWIFT_BIC_PATTERN regex constants: AddTenantData validation, InvoiceSettingsData validation (keep in sync).
- InvitationStatusEnum/InvitationAcceptStateEnum: InvitationAcceptPageData (FE mapping), InvitationController template, tests/Feature/Tenancy/InvitationAcceptTest.php.
- Invitation token route: regex constraint, throttle middleware, controller 410/404 guards.
- setPermissionsTeamId call sites: TenantContextMiddleware, RegistrationService, InvitationAcceptService, UserService, tests/TestCase::actingAsTenantUser, every job/command (D6 discipline).
- TenantScope unfiltered behaviour (D6): tests/Feature/Tenancy/TenantScopeIsolationTest.php assert; console commands + seeders bind explicit tenant context.

**Keywords (SK):** firma, tenancy, member, pozvánka, invitation, tenant switcher, multi-tenant, tenant isolation, shared-props, team scope, setPermissionsTeamId, tenant_id FK, BelongsToTenant, TenantScope, active membership, is_active.

### identity — users, roles, permissions, self-service profile

**Core:**
- App\Models\User — UUIDv7 via App\Concerns\HasUuids; traits HasRoles, HasApiTokens, LogsActivity (logOnly name,email,locale,is_active), Notifiable, CanResetPassword. Columns: name, email(unique), password, locale(2, default sk), is_active(bool, default true), is_platform_admin(bool, default false; NOT in #[Fillable], never in shared props; gates global document templates + POST /tenants; set via `app:grant-platform-admin {email}`, by `app:create-owner` when no platform admin exists yet, and by UserSeeder for admin@example.com), email_verified_at, timestamps. No SoftDeletes; global identity (no tenant_id). Relations: memberships HasMany TenantMembership, tenants BelongsToMany Tenant (via tenant_memberships, pivot is_active/joined_at), ownedTenants HasMany Tenant (owner_id). Methods: hasActiveMembership(?tenantId): bool (exists-query for "has at least one active membership").
- App\Models\Role extends Spatie Role — UUIDv7 via HasUuids + LogsActivity (logOnly name,guard_name,tenant_id) + scopeInTenant(Builder, string) (no global scope; app queries use inTenant() explicitly). Methods: isSystem(): bool (= name === RoleTemplatesSeeder::ADMIN_ROLE). Per-tenant.
- App\Models\Permission extends Spatie Permission — UUIDv7 via HasUuids. Global catalogue (not per-tenant); only roles are per-tenant.
- Both registered config/permission.php (teams=true, team_foreign_key=tenant_id, uuid team columns).
- App\Services\UserService (final readonly, ctor DatabaseManager, PermissionRegistrar, RoleAssignmentGuard): `create(CreateUserData, User $actor): User` — transaction, setPermissionsTeamId(current_tenant_id), look up or create User by email, membership create/reactivate (is_active from DTO), `$guard->assertAssignable($actor, roles)`, syncRoles in tenant scope, return fresh User. `update(User, UpdateUserData, User $actor): User` — name/email/locale on User, is_active on membership, roles guard + syncRoles in tenant scope. `delete(User): void` — syncRoles([]), syncPermissions([]), delete tenant membership (User row never hard-deleted in phase 2).
- App\Services\RoleService: `const SYSTEM_ROLES = [RoleTemplatesSeeder::ADMIN_ROLE]` · `create(name, permissions): Role` (check uniqueness inTenant + not in SYSTEM_ROLES) · `update(Role, name, permissions)` · `delete(Role)` (block SYSTEM_ROLES + Spatie guard) · `getPermissionsGrouped(): list<PermissionGroupData>` (from PermissionEnum cases grouped by group(), each item has id/name/label per PermissionItemData).
- App\Services\ProfileService::changePassword(User, string): void — transaction: read the caller's current MobileSession (`currentFor`), update `password`, `MobileSessionService::revokeAllFor(user, PasswordChanged, except: current)`. Web passes no bearer token, so every phone is revoked. Then `event(new PasswordChanged($user))` (ShouldDispatchAfterCommit) → LogAuthenticationActivity::handlePasswordChanged logs `password_changed`.
- App\Services\ProfileService::updateProfile(User, UpdateProfileData): User — name/email/locale on User, session('locale') + app()->setLocale(). Locale sync via app()->setLocale() (user.locale consumer on login via LocaleMiddleware).
- App\Enums\PermissionEnum (#[TypeScript], backed string) — 68 cases (2026-11-03 added `revert audit logs` in group `audit_logs`, migration 2026_11_10_000200, Admin only; 2026-11-01 added `manage employee_access` in group `employees`, migration 2026_11_06_000200, Admin only; 2026-11-01 added `use assistant` in group `ai` (migration 2026_11_05_000100; gates the web chat bubble, the mobile assistant endpoint, the assistant company list and tools) and `run tasks with ai` in group `tasks`, granted to Admin and Vedúca by migration 2026_11_04_000400 and RoleTemplatesSeeder; Phase 11 added `access mobile app` in group `mobile`, five `tasks` cases incl. breadth `view all tasks`, and `view expenses` / `manage expenses` in group `expenses`), grouped into 15 groups (employees, roles, audit_logs, api_docs, media, billing; phases 3+ add clients, objects, quotes, contracts, schedule, invoices, recurring_invoices, notifications, document_templates). 2026-10-31: `*_contract_templates` renamed to `view|create|edit|delete document_templates` (migration 2026_10_31_000400 renames the permission rows in place, keeping role attachments); new in the employees group: `view employee_personal_data`, `view employee_documents`, `manage employee_documents`. Methods: label(), group(), groupLabel(), sharedKey() (Str::camel($value)), values(). Key list in Phase 2 plan §3.1.

**Satellites (FE):**
- Composables: usePageProps() (ComputedRef<SharedProps>), useAuthorization() (allows(PermissionEnum)).
- Components: Can (permission guard), PermissionManager (translated, groups: PermissionGroupData[]).
- Pages/Users/Index.vue — props {users: Paginator<UserListItemData>; filters?: Record<string,unknown>; filterOptions:{roles: RoleListItemData[]}} · QueryBuilder member-only filter is_active (on membership pivot) · Filters search/role/is_active (membership-scoped) · DataTable · Edit/DELETE gated `allows('edit employees')` / `allows('delete employees')`, create gated `allows('create employees')`. **Phase 2 change:** users are now active+inactive **members of the active tenant** (not globally all users); `is_active` filter label → `membership_active` (semantic = membership status, not User.is_active).
- Pages/Users/Form.vue — props {user?: UserListItemData; roles: RoleListItemData[]} · useForm(post|put, /users[/{id}], {name,email,password,password_confirmation,is_active,roles: string[]}) · Create: password required only if new email (DTO Rule::requiredIf); existing email → link (password ignored). ToggleInput field="is_active" label="membership_active". Roles: `Rule::exists('roles','name')->where('tenant_id', current_tenant_id)` + subset escalation guard → error on roles field. CreateUserData has `email` unique-check removed (link-existing flow); UpdateUserData validates email unique except self.
- Pages/Roles/Index.vue — props {roles: Paginator<RoleDetailData>} · Per-tenant roles (RoleController QueryBuilder::for(Role::inTenant($tenantId))) · canDeleteRow=!row.is_system · CREATE/EDIT/DELETE gated `allows('create roles')` etc. System role badge → `system_role`.
- Pages/Roles/Form.vue — props {role?: RoleDetailData; permissions: PermissionGroupData[]} · useForm(post|put, /roles[/{id}], {name,permissions: string[]}) · PermissionManager now receives grouped data structure (no client-side grouping; render by group + group checkbox + items) · system role: name disabled + alert-warning · Admin blocks delete.
- Pages/Profile/Show.vue — no page props, reads usePageProps().value.auth.user (ComputedRef, never destructure) · two useForm('put') forms (profile name/email/locale, password change) · locale select from shared languages (now includes uk).
- AppLayout language dropdown → <a href="/language/{locale}"> (full reload) → LocaleMiddleware resolves: user.locale → session → cookie → Accept-Language → sk. Languages list now data-driven from shared languages (includes uk).

**DTOs in:**
- CreateUserData (#[Required] name/email, ?string password [Rule::requiredIf new email], is_active=true, roles=[]), UpdateUserData (name/email/locale, is_active, roles=[]).
- CreateRoleData (#[Required, Max(100)] name, roles=[]), UpdateRoleData (name, permissions=[]).
- UpdateProfileData (name, email [unique except self], locale), ChangePasswordData (current_password [Hash::check], password [confirmed]).
- PermissionGroupData (#[TypeScript]: group, group_label, permissions: PermissionItemData[]) — PermissionItemData (id, name: PermissionEnum, label).

**DTOs out:**
- UserListItemData (id, name, email, roles: string[], is_active from membership in active tenant), UserAutocompleteItemData, RoleListItemData (id, name, counts, is_system), RoleDetailData (extends RoleListItemData, + permissions: string[]). Dead: UserIndexFilterData, RoleIndexFilterData.

**Policies:**
- UserPolicy (#[Authorize] gates): viewAny → `can('view employees')`, view → `can('view employees')` + record-level isMemberOf(current_tenant), update → `can('edit employees')` + isMemberOf, delete → `can('delete employees')` + `can('manage employee_access')` + isMemberOf + not self + not tenant owner; create → `can('create employees')` + `can('manage employee_access')` (Users module creates logins, so it needs the access permission; 2026-11-01). UserService update(deactivate)/delete also call `RegistrationService::revokePendingInvitations`. Users list only shows memberships with a user (no-access employees live in Employees only).
- RolePolicy: viewAny/view → `can('view roles')` + `tenant_id === current_tenant_id`, create/update → `can('create|edit roles')` + tenant check, delete → `can('delete roles')` + tenant check + not SYSTEM_ROLES.
- Helper User::isMemberOf(tenantId): bool (any status); delete asserts User::isNotOwnerOfTenant(tenantId).

**Factories & Seeders:**
- UserFactory states unverified(), inactive(). TenantMembershipFactory states withProfile(), inactive(). UserSeeder creates admin@example.com/password via RegistrationService::createOwner (D3: canonical path, now owns "Demo Cleaning s.r.o."). No 5 faker users (spec chose "no demo accounts").

**Flow:**
- GET /users → UserController@index (#[Authorize('viewAny', PermissionEnum::ViewEmployees->value)]) → QueryBuilder::for(User::whereHas('memberships', fn ($q) => $q->where('tenant_id', current_tenant_id)->with(...)) + AllowedFilter filters (search/role/is_active membership-scoped) → ->through(UserListItemData::fromModel) → Inertia render Users/Index {users, filters, filterOptions{roles: RoleListItemData[]}}.
- POST /users (+Precognition) → @store(CreateUserData, $actor) → UserService::create($data, $actor) → User (new or existing email) + TenantMembership (create/reactivate) + role assignment (setPermissionsTeamId + assertAssignable guards) + return. PUT /users/{user} → @update(UpdateUserData, User, $actor) → UserService::update. DELETE /users/{user} → UserService::delete (membership removed, roles revoked, User row kept).
- GET /users/autocomplete?q= → UserController@autocomplete → whereHas('memberships', current_tenant) + ilike search → UserAutocompleteItemData[]; private const AUTOCOMPLETE_MIN_CHARS=2.
- GET /roles → RoleController@index (#[Authorize(PermissionEnum::ViewRoles->value)]) → QueryBuilder::for(Role::inTenant(current_tenant_id)) → Inertia render Roles/Index {roles: Paginator<RoleDetailData>}. POST /roles → @store(CreateRoleData) → RoleService::create → Roles/Form {role?: RoleDetailData, permissions: PermissionGroupData[]}.
- GET /profile → Inertia render Profile/Show (reads usePageProps().value.auth.user). PUT /profile → ProfileService::updateProfile. PUT /profile/password → Hash::check in controller (web + API), then ProfileService::changePassword (web: all mobile sessions revoked; API: all except the calling phone).
- API mirror (Sanctum): GET /api/users → same member-only query; GET|PUT|DELETE /api/users/{user}, GET|PUT /api/profile, PUT /api/profile/password → same services, JSON UserListItemData (paginated). **Phase 11:** these routes sit in the `/api` group that requires `ability:mobile` (a web or MCP token gets 403).

**Depends on:** tenancy (TenantMembership, tenant_id FK, setPermissionsTeamId discipline), auth layer (#[Authorize], Spatie can() per team scope), App\Utils\AllowedFilter, Activitylog, i18n (PermissionEnum labels).

**Depended on by:** auth (User hasActiveMembership check at login), audit-logs (causer_type=User subject_type=User/Role), media (temporary_uploads.user_id), navigation (NavigationRegistry::canAccess checks can(permission)), tenancy (role templates, user invitations), Inertia shared can + auth.user + languages.

**If you change Core, check:**
- PermissionEnum changes: PermissionSeeder (findOrCreate per case), RoleTemplatesSeeder (role permission bundles), PermissionGroupData structure, PermissionManager (FE groups), HandleInertiaRequests shared can map (useAuthorization().allows(permission)), #[NavItem(permission: PermissionEnum::*->value)], policies can() checks, tests/Support/CreatesUsers.php userWithPermission().
- User model changes (columns, relations): app/Models/User.php (Fillable, relations, hasActiveMembership), UserListItemData (roles, is_active reads membership), UserFactory, tests/Feature/Auth (login guards is_active + hasActiveMembership), TenantMembership foreign key.
- Role model changes (per-tenant scoping): config/permission.php teams/team_foreign_key, RolePolicy (inTenant guards), RoleController/RoleService (inTenant queries), Role.php (isSystem, inTenant scope, logOnly fields).
- User.is_active enforcement: AuthController login/Api/AuthController login guards + RequireActiveTenant middleware (D4).
- Membership soft-revocation vs hard-delete: UserService::delete vs TenantMembership soft-delete strategy (currently hard-delete membership, never hard-delete User).

**Keywords:** user, tenant member, role (per-tenant), permission (global catalogue, per-team assignment), admin, is_active flag (User state), membership.is_active (tenancy state), locale, syncRoles (per-team), setPermissionsTeamId, autocomplete (tenant-scoped), profile, change password, PermissionEnum, hasActiveMembership, RoleAssignmentGuard (escalation prevention).

### auth — session login, password reset, Sanctum tokens, auth audit

**Core:**
- No model/service; controllers call Auth::attempt + multi-guard checks. Web: AuthController::showLogin/login(LoginData, Request)/logout. API: Api\AuthController::login(LoginData, Request)/logout (Sanctum Bearer). Password reset: PasswordResetLinkController::create|store(PasswordResetLinkData), NewPasswordController::create|store(NewPasswordData). Middleware guest/auth/auth:sanctum.
- **Phase 11 API tokens:** `POST /api/auth/login` (group: HandlePrecognitiveRequests + locale.api + throttle:mobile-login) issues a `MobileTokenPairData` through `MobileSessionService::issue` only when `TenantPermissionSnapshot::hasMobileAccess($user)` (permission `access mobile app` in some active tenant); otherwise `Auth::logout()` + 422 on `email` with `mobile_forbidden`. `POST /api/auth/refresh` (locale.api + throttle:mobile-token) → `MobileSessionService::refresh` → 200 pair or 401 `app.session_invalid`. `POST /api/auth/revoke` (locale.api + throttle:mobile-token) → `revokeByRefreshToken` → 204. `POST /api/auth/logout` (auth:sanctum, locale.api) — no ability gate; a session behind a mobile token is revoked (reason `logout`), an MCP token is deleted as before. MCP tokens are a separate kind: `ProfileMcpTokenController` → `McpTokenService::create` issues ability `['mcp']`, expiry 180 days, max 10 per user; `findOwnMcpToken` resolves only own tokens whose abilities are exactly `['mcp']` (a `mobile` token is not found there).
- **Login guards (D4):** Auth::attempt(['email' => …, 'password' => …, 'is_active' => true], remember) so inactive users fail indistinguishably from bad credentials + audit fires Failed event; then `if (! $user->hasActiveMembership()) { Auth::logout(); throw ValidationException::withMessages(['email' => [__('app.no_active_tenant')]]); }` (indistinguishable from no membership, deliberate per D4). API login same guards. Failure indistinguishable from bad credentials (failure still fires Failed event for audit).
- App\Listeners\LogAuthenticationActivity (auto-discovered, no AppServiceProvider wiring) — handleLogin|handleLogout|handleFailed|handlePasswordReset|handlePasswordResetLinkSent|handleLockout|handlePasswordChanged → activity()->log('login'|'logout'|'login_failed'|'password_reset'|'password_reset_link_sent'|'login_lockout'|'password_changed'). Every row: properties ip (get_client_ip()), user_agent, email (string or null). Causer = user where known. `login_failed` and `login_lockout` have no causer (not visible in the audit viewer, DB only). `password_reset_link_sent` logs existing emails only. `login_lockout` deduped by Cache::add: one row per IP and one per email+IP per 60 s; email capped at 255 chars. `password_changed` comes from `App\Events\PasswordChanged` (ShouldDispatchAfterCommit) dispatched by ProfileService::changePassword (web + API).
- **Login and reset rate limits (2026-11-04, named limiters in AppServiceProvider::boot):** `web-login` on POST /login = 10/min per IP + 5/min per lowercased email+IP; `password-email` on POST /forgot-password = 5/min per IP; `password-reset` on POST /reset-password = 5/min per IP. Attached in routes/web.php next to HandlePrecognitiveRequests. A Precognition request goes to its own `precognition:ip:` bucket (60/min) and does not consume the real buckets. A throttled web request → ValidationException on `email` with `app.auth_throttled` (:seconds), shown under the field; `web-login` dispatches `Lockout`. `mobile-login` (POST /api/auth/login) uses the same two login limits, dispatches `Lockout` and keeps the JSON 429. Array `email` input is ignored for the key (no 500). Keys use get_client_ip() = `$request->ip()`, resolved through the trusted-proxy list (see Cross-cutting "Client IP and trusted proxies"; unset `TRUSTED_PROXIES` = every request keyed on the proxy IP, see Gotchas "Trusted proxies").
- RequireActiveTenant middleware (route `tenant.required`) ensures every authenticated HTTP request has a bound tenant via TenantContextMiddleware (D4). Mid-session membership deactivation → next request logs out web user / returns 403 API (transparent to user).
- Session invalidation on logout via `session()->invalidate()` + `regenerateToken()` (CSRF).

**Satellites (FE):**
- Pages/Auth/Login.vue — props {canResetPassword}; useForm('post','/login',{email,password,remember}), onFinish reset('password') · FormProvider + new Components/Auth/{AuthShell, AuthHero, AuthTextField, AuthPasswordField, AuthCheckboxField, AuthSubmitButton, AuthLanguageSwitcher}.
- Pages/Auth/ForgotPassword.vue — {status?}; POST /forgot-password · same Auth components + FormProvider.
- Pages/Auth/ResetPassword.vue — {token,email}; POST /reset-password · same Auth components + FormProvider.
- Layout: AuthShell wraps all three pages (brown/amber hero left 60 % desktop, white form panel, language switcher below divider).
- Components/BrandMark.vue — removed in the KEGOS redesign (logo is a static asset; firm colour is a dot only, `useTenantTheme` removed).
- AppLayout.logout() → router.post('/logout') (AppLayout.vue:284).

**DTOs:**
- LoginData (email, password, remember=false; `remember` ignored by the API), PasswordResetLinkData, NewPasswordData, AuthTokenData {token, refresh_token, user: UserListItemData}, RefreshTokenData {refresh_token} (required, max 255; body of /refresh and /revoke), MobileTokenPairData {token, refresh_token} (no user payload, no expires_in).

**Flow:**
- GET /login → Auth/Login {canResetPassword}. POST /login (+Precognition) → LoginData → Auth::attempt → 302 dashboard | ValidationException on email __('app.invalid_credentials') | throttled: ValidationException on email __('app.auth_throttled') (see Core rate limits). POST /logout → login. GET /forgot-password → Auth/ForgotPassword {status}; POST → back()->with('status'). GET /reset-password/{token} → Auth/ResetPassword {email, token}; POST /reset-password → Password::reset → login. POST /api/auth/login → JSON AuthTokenData; POST /api/auth/refresh → MobileTokenPairData | 401; POST /api/auth/revoke → 204; POST /api/auth/logout → 204.

**Depends on:** identity (User.is_active, hasActiveMembership), tenancy (RequireActiveTenant middleware, TenantContextMiddleware), audit-logs (listener), localisation (LocaleMiddleware reads user.locale).

**Depended on by:** every authenticated route (behind auth/auth:sanctum); RequireActiveTenant gates HTTP; HandleInertiaRequests (auth.user prop).

**If you change Core, check:**
- Login guards: AuthController.php login method (is_active + hasActiveMembership + ValidationException), Api/AuthController.php (same), tests/Feature/Auth/AuthControllerTest.php (guards), tests/Feature/Tenancy/RequireActiveTenantTest.php (mid-session loss).
- Api/AuthController.php (login, refresh, revoke, logout), routes/api.php throttle groups, AppServiceProvider.php limiters `mobile-login` / `mobile-token`, tests/Feature/Auth/MobileSessionTest.php, tests/Feature/Api/AuthApiControllerTest.php, tests/Feature/Api/TokenAbilityTest.php (MCP logout).
- app/Data/AuthTokenData.php, app/Listeners/LogAuthenticationActivity.php, app/Events/PasswordChanged.php (ip → get_client_ip()), app/Support/helpers.php get_client_ip().
- Client IP chain: bootstrap/app.php `trustProxies(headers: X_FORWARDED_FOR | X_FORWARDED_PROTO)` (never add `at:` — it overrides config), config/trustedproxy.php (`proxies` always an array, unset = `[]`), tests/Feature/Http/TrustedProxiesTest.php (11 tests).
- FE login page consumes `canResetPassword` prop (remains auth-page-only, not shared).
- Rate limiters: AppServiceProvider::boot `web-login` / `password-email` / `password-reset` / `mobile-login` and helpers `loginLimits`, `precognitionLimit`, `throttledWithValidationError`; routes/web.php `throttle:web-login|password-email|password-reset`; routes/api.php `throttle:mobile-login`; tests/Feature/Auth/AuthActivityLogTest.php, AuthControllerTest.php, PasswordResetLinkControllerTest.php, NewPasswordControllerTest.php.

**Keywords:** login, logout, refresh token, revoke, mobile session, sanctum, bearer token, password reset, forgot password, remember, guest, session regenerate, login_failed, login_lockout, password_changed, password_reset_link_sent, throttle, web-login, password-email, password-reset, auth_throttled, is_active guard, hasActiveMembership, tenant-required.

### mobile-session — mobile login sessions, refresh rotation, revocation, prune (2026-11-01, stay logged in)

**Core:**
- App\Models\MobileSession — UUIDv7 (HasUuids), **per person**: `user_id` FK → users (restrictOnDelete); no tenant_id (rule-6 exception). `personal_access_token_id` FK nullable unique → personal_access_tokens (nullOnDelete; empties when Sanctum prunes the access token). `refresh_hash` char(64), `previous_refresh_hash` char(64) nullable, `rotated_at`, `rotation_confirmed_at`, `expires_at` (sliding). Both hashes hidden. No Policy, no SoftDeletes, no LogsActivity. Migration 2026_11_08_000100_create_mobile_sessions_table.
- App\Services\MobileSessionService (final readonly; DatabaseManager, TenantPermissionSnapshot, DeviceTokenService) — `issue(User): MobileTokenPairData`; `refresh(string): ?MobileTokenPairData` (null = refused, controller answers 401); `revokeByRefreshToken(string): void` (locks the row, accepts current or previous secret); `revoke(MobileSession, MobileSessionRevokeReasonEnum)`; `revokeAllFor(User, reason, ?except)`; `revokeAllIfNoActiveMembership(User)` (also removes device rows without a session); `currentFor(User): ?MobileSession` (via `currentAccessToken()`); `mobileAccessDenial(User): ?string` (`app.account_inactive` | `app.no_active_tenant` | `app.mobile_forbidden`, null = allowed); `pruneExpired(): int`.
- Token format: refresh token `<session uuid>.<secret>`. First secret `Str::random(64)`; next secret = `hash_hmac('sha256', "{session_id}|{presented_secret}", app.key)`, so parallel refreshes get the same next token. Only sha256 stored. Access token = Sanctum PAT named `mobile`, abilities `['mobile']`, expiry now + `mobile_auth.access_token_minutes` (60). `sanctum.expiration` stays null (it would also cut MCP tokens to 60 min). Rotation deletes the previous PAT.
- Refresh decision order (session row `lockForUpdate`): malformed or unknown session id → refuse, nothing killed. Secret equal to neither current nor an acceptable previous → kill `reuse_detected` (warning log `mobile_session.reuse_detected`). `expires_at` past → kill `expired`. `mobileAccessDenial` non-null → kill `access_lost`. Equal to current → rotate (`previous_refresh_hash` = current, `rotated_at` = now). Equal to previous and acceptable → reissue (same next token; `rotated_at` and `previous_refresh_hash` unchanged). Previous is acceptable when `rotation_confirmed_at` is null or `rotated_at` is within `reuse_leeway_seconds` (60). Every success: `rotation_confirmed_at` = null, `expires_at` = now + `refresh_idle_days` (365).
- App\Listeners\ConfirmMobileSessionRotation — handles Sanctum `TokenAuthenticated`; when the access token's `last_used_at` is null (first use), sets `rotation_confirmed_at` on the session holding that PAT. Auto-discovered (no explicit registration).
- App\Enums\MobileSessionRevokeReasonEnum — logout, password_changed, password_reset, access_lost, reuse_detected, expired, membership_lost. Audit: `activity()->causedBy($user)->withProperties(['ip', 'session_id', 'reason'])->log('mobile_session_revoked')`, one row per revoked session; no row for a routine refresh or the prune.
- config/mobile_auth.php: `access_token_minutes` 60, `refresh_idle_days` 365, `reuse_leeway_seconds` 60.

**Satellites:**
- Api\AuthController — login issues the pair; refresh, revoke and logout as in auth; Scribe attributes on all four.
- DTOs: RefreshTokenData, MobileTokenPairData, AuthTokenData.refresh_token.
- App\Console\Commands\PruneMobileSessions (`cleanup:mobile-sessions`) → `pruneExpired()`: bulk delete of sessions past `expires_at` plus their PATs; device rows follow by FK. routes/console.php: 12:00 Slovak time via `$atSlovakHour`.
- Revocation callers: ProfileService::changePassword (except current), NewPasswordController::store (`PasswordReset`, all), EmployeeService::deactivate and EmployeeAccessService::revoke (`revokeAllIfNoActiveMembership`), UserService remove and membership deactivation (`revokeAllIfNoActiveMembership`).
- Push: DeviceTokenService rows follow the session through `mobile_session_id` (see push).

**Flow:**
- Login: POST /api/auth/login → `mobileAccessDenial` (422 on `email`, no session created) → `issue` → AuthTokenData {token, refresh_token, user}.
- Refresh: POST /api/auth/refresh {refresh_token} → `refresh()` → 200 MobileTokenPairData, or 401 {message: `app.session_invalid`}. Malformed body → 422. Throttle → 429, never 401.
- Revoke: POST /api/auth/revoke {refresh_token} → 204 for any well-formed body, including unknown or malformed tokens.
- Password: PUT /api/profile/password keeps the calling phone (its session found by `currentFor`), revokes the others; PUT /profile/password (web) and POST /reset-password revoke all.
- Lifetime: refresh past 365 days idle → kill `expired` on the next refresh; the prune removes the row later, but expiry is enforced at read.
- Migration 2026_11_08_000100: deletes `personal_access_tokens` with abilities `["mobile"]` and `expires_at` null, deletes all `device_tokens`, drops `device_tokens.personal_access_token_id`, adds `device_tokens.mobile_session_id` (cascade). Forward-only in practice: `down()` drops the table and restores the old column, but the deleted tokens and device rows are not restored.

**Depends on:** auth (login, password reset, Api\AuthController), identity (User.is_active, password), tenancy (hasActiveMembership, TenantPermissionSnapshot::hasMobileAccess), push (DeviceTokenService), Sanctum (PersonalAccessToken, TokenAuthenticated event).

**Depended on by:** Api\AuthController, Api\DeviceController (`currentFor`), ProfileService, NewPasswordController, EmployeeService, EmployeeAccessService, UserService.

**If you change Core, check:**
- Callers of `revokeAllFor` / `revokeAllIfNoActiveMembership` / `changePassword` (grep `app/`, list above).
- tests/Feature/Auth/MobileSessionTest.php, tests/Feature/Api/AuthApiControllerTest.php, tests/Feature/Push/DeviceRegistrationTest.php, tests/Feature/Push/DeviceCleanupTest.php, tests/Feature/Services/ProfileServiceTest.php.
- ConfirmMobileSessionRotation: changing how the access token is looked up breaks rotation confirmation (a reuse after a prune would then kill the session).

**Keywords:** mobile session, refresh token, rotation, reuse detection, revoke, logout, password changed, prune, cleanup:mobile-sessions, access token 60 min, sliding 365 days.

### audit-logs — Activitylog viewer + audit-log revert (viewer Phase 2; revert 2026-11-03)

**Core:**
- `App\Models\Activity` extends Spatie `Activity` (`final class`). PK = bigint `id()` (not UUID). Morph subject/causer (uuid), `tenant_id` (uuid nullable), `batch_uuid` (uuid nullable, indexed, pre-existing 2026_04_30_200000). **Revert columns 2026_11_10_000100:** `reverted_at` (timestampTz nullable, cast datetime), `revert_batch_uuid` (uuid nullable, indexed), no FK. booted() creating hook: `batch_uuid ??= ActivityBatch::current()`; `tenant_id ??= current_tenant_id()` unless the non-persisted `keepNullTenant` flag is set (global task rows). Methods: `scopeVisibleInTenant`, `isVisibleInTenant`, `causerUser()`.
- `App\Support\ActivityBatch` (static `run(Closure): mixed`, `current(): ?string`). Outermost `run` mints a uuid7 and resets it in `finally`; nested `run` reuses the outer uuid. Static property, not Context, so it never reaches queued jobs. Entry points: `ClientService::create|update|delete`, `ObjectService::update`, and the revert writes in `ActivityRevertService::revertLocked`. Every other writer leaves `batch_uuid` NULL; its rows revert one by one.
- `App\Services\ActivityRevertService` (final readonly, ctor DatabaseManager), the single allowlist. Private consts `UPDATE_FIELDS` (per model), `REMOVABLE` (created / restored → delete), `RESTORABLE` (deleted → restore; restored CleaningObject → delete). Public: `describeMany(Collection, User): array<int, ActivityRevertData>` (list quick check, bulk subject eager load, 2 link queries), `describe(Activity, User): ActivityRevertData` (full check: conflict, later changes, batch members), `subjectLabels(Collection): array<int, ?string>`, `changes(Activity): list<ActivityRevertChangeData>`, `revert(Activity, User): void` (ValidationException on `status` with the reason label; AuthorizationException when the record ability fails; `UniqueConstraintViolationException` → `unique_conflict`).
- Auth events (2026-11-04): LogAuthenticationActivity writes login, logout, login_failed, password_reset, password_reset_link_sent, login_lockout, password_changed. login_failed and login_lockout have no causer and are not returned by Activity::visibleInTenant (DB only).
- Contact rows (2026-11-04): subject `App\Models\Contact`, audit type label "Contact", parent resolved via `contactable`.
- Allowlist (`UPDATE_FIELDS`): Client = type, name, ico, dic, vat_number, is_vat_payer, street, city, postal_code, country, peppol_participant_id. Contact (polymorphic, `contactable` = Client or CleaningObject) = name, position, email, phone (`is_primary` added only for whole-batch reverts, see Gotchas). CleaningObject = type, name, address fields, access_code, key_box_code, key_count, special_instructions, area_sqm, floor, billing_* (client_id and is_active excluded). Task = title, description, priority, due_at, remind_at. Expense = date, amount, currency, vat_amount, supplier_name, category. Tenant = company fields, vat fields, iban, swift_bic, registration_info, representative_name, signing_place, address fields, contact fields, peppol_participant_id. TenantInterface = color, recurring_default_state, default_constant_symbol / payment_type / currency / rounding_mode, default_header_text / default_footer_text, default_quote_included_text, invoice_mail_subject / invoice_mail_body. Invoice, Quote, Contract = empty list (every `updated` row → `lifecycle_field`).
- Record ability (`allowed()`): Tenant / TenantInterface → `manage billing settings`. Contact → `update` on its `contactable` parent (Client or CleaningObject), every kind. Other models → `update` for update rows, `delete` for created / restored / deleted rows. Route gate: `ActivityPolicy::revert` = `revert audit logs` AND `activity.tenant_id === current_tenant_id()`.
- Remove path (`remove()`): Client → ClientService::delete, Task → TaskService::delete, Expense → ExpenseService::delete, Invoice / Quote / Contract → their service delete (422 `not_draft` surfaces here); other models `->delete()`. Services resolved via `app()` inside `remove()`.
- Restore path (`restore()`): single-row batch of Contact sets `is_primary` = no other active primary; Quote and Contract also restore their trashed `document()` (`withTrashed()`, D9).

**Satellites (BE):**
- Enums (#[TypeScript]): `ActivityRevertStatusEnum` (available, unavailable, reverted); `ActivityRevertBlockReasonEnum` (17 cases; `label()` → `__('app.audit_revert_reason_<case>')`).
- DTOs (#[TypeScript]): `ActivityRevertData` (status, reason, changes_count, batch_size, blocking_activity_id, reverted_at, reverted_by_name, reverted_by_activity_id, reverts_activity_id); `ActivityRevertChangeData` (field, label, old, new, current, conflict, masked). `ActivityLogListItemData` += `subject_label`, `revert`. `ActivityLogDetailData` += `subject_label`, `revert` (full check), `changes`. Raw `attribute_changes` and `properties` stay in the detail DTO.
- Migrations (additive, forward-only in practice): `2026_11_10_000100_add_revert_columns_to_activity_log_table` (2 columns, 2 indexes); `2026_11_10_000200_add_revert_audit_logs_permission` (permission row, granted to Admin; down deletes the row).
- Lang (sk/en/uk `app.json`): `app.audit_log_reverted`, 17 `app.audit_revert_reason_*`. Field labels: `validation.attributes.<field>`, fallback raw field.
- `reverts_activity_id` / `reverted_by_activity_id` are computed links (same subject first, else lowest id in the batch), not stored.
- Policy: `ActivityPolicy` viewAny / view (view also checks visibleInTenant); `revert` (see Record ability).
- FE: Pages/AuditLogs/Index.vue (revert column via `ActivityRevertStatus`; undo icon button in `#buttons` with `@click.stop`; ConfirmDeleteModal via `useActivityRevertConfirm`; row link kept), Pages/AuditLogs/Show.vue (subject label, `ActivityChangesDiff` before/after table, `ActivityRevertCard`, raw JSON in a collapsed `<details>`), Components/AuditLogs/{ActivityRevertStatus, ActivityChangesDiff, ActivityRevertCard}.vue, Composables/useActivityRevertConfirm.ts (wraps useDeleteConfirm, POST `/audit-logs/{id}/revert`), utils/auditLog.ts (formatAuditValue, revertConfirmKey, revertSubjectLabel).

**Flow:**
- GET /audit-logs → `AuditLogController@index` (`#[Authorize('viewAny', Activity::class)]`) → QueryBuilder over `Activity::visibleInTenant(current_tenant_id)->with('causer')` + AllowedFilter search / subject_type / created_at + sorts → `describeMany` + `subjectLabels` → `->through(ActivityLogListItemData::fromModel)` → Inertia `AuditLogs/Index`.
- GET /audit-logs/{activity} → `show` (`#[Authorize('view', 'activity')]`) → `describe` + `changes()` → `ActivityLogDetailData` → `AuditLogs/Show`.
- POST /audit-logs/{activity}/revert → `AuditLogController@revert` (`#[Authorize('revert', 'activity')]`) → `ActivityRevertService::revert` → `DB::transaction`: one `lockForUpdate` query over the batch (same `batch_uuid` and tenant, ordered by id; or the single row); original already reverted → 422 `already_reverted`; full verdict over all members (subjects locked too); `ActivityBatch::run` applies pending members newest first (update → `forceFill(old)->save()`; created / restored → domain delete; deleted → `restore()`; Document satellite rows skipped), then sets `reverted_at` + `revert_batch_uuid` on the originals. Success → `back()` with flash `app.audit_log_reverted`.
- Read-side subject: `attachSubjects` loads each subject through its own model query (global scopes on, `withTrashed` for SoftDeletes); the subject is nulled unless `activity.tenant_id === current_tenant_id()` and the subject belongs to the tenant (→ `subject_missing`). Morph aliases `client`, `cleaning_object`, `tenant_membership` resolve via `Relation::morphMap` (AppServiceProvider).

**Depends on:** tenancy (tenant_id, `current_tenant_id()`, belongsToTenant guard), identity (causer User; `view audit logs` + `revert audit logs`), clients + objects (batch entry points, domain delete / restore), tasks, expenses, invoices, quotes, contracts (domain delete services; Quote / Contract document restore), documents (Document satellite rows).

**Depended on by:** ClientService, ObjectService (write batches into the activity log). AuditLogController is the only caller of ActivityRevertService.

**If you change Core, check:**
- `ActivityRevertService::UPDATE_FIELDS` / `REMOVABLE` / `RESTORABLE` / `remove()` match / `allowed()` move together. The revert never changes a `logOnly` set.
- Callers of `ActivityBatch::run`: `grep -rn "ActivityBatch::run" app` (ClientService x3, ObjectService x1, revert x1). A new batched write path must wrap or its rows revert alone.
- `AuditLogController` (index / show / revert) is the only route consumer: `routes/web.php` lines 104-106.
- `ActivityLogListItemData::fromModel` and `ActivityLogDetailData::fromModel` callers: AuditLogController index and show only.
- `ActivityRevertBlockReasonEnum` cases → `app.audit_revert_reason_*` keys in all three lang files + `generated.d.ts` (`php artisan typescript:transform`).

**Keywords:** activity log, audit, revert, redo, batch_uuid, login_failed, login_lockout (no causer, DB only), password_changed, revert_batch_uuid, reverted_at, ActivityBatch, conflict, has_later_changes, batch_member_blocked, causer, subject, LogsActivity, attribute_changes, reverts_activity_id.

### media — MediaLibrary viewer + temporary uploads

**Core:**
- App\Models\Media extends Spatie\MediaLibrary\MediaCollections\Models\Media (D1-new) — `final class`, booted() creating hook: for `model_type` = Task it copies the owner's `tenant_id` (NULL for personal tasks) and throws RuntimeException('media.owner_missing') when the owner is not found; for other owners `tenant_id ??= app('current_tenant_id')` (throws RuntimeException('media.tenant_context_missing') when unbound). Columns: morph model_id (uuid), **tenant_id (uuid, NOT NULL except Task owners since migration 2026_11_02_000200; DB CHECK `media_tenant_required_unless_task`: NULL allowed only when model_type = App\Models\Task)** + index, disk public per config/media-library.php, 10 MB max. Methods: `scopeInTenant(Builder, $tenantId): Builder` (tenant_id = ?). Config media-library.php media_model → `App\Models\Media`.
- App\Models\TemporaryUpload — UUID PK, #[Fillable(['session_id','user_id','tenant_id'])], implements HasMedia, collection default; traits HasFactory, BelongsToTenant. FKs: user_id → users (nullOnDelete), **tenant_id → tenants (restrictOnDelete, new)**. TenantScope applied. user(): BelongsTo.
- App\Services\TemporaryUploadService (final readonly, DB::transaction): `store(UploadedFile, ?User, string $sessionId): Media` (posts to /uploads, current_tenant_id bound required) · `moveToModel(HasMedia $model, string $collection, string $uuid, ?User $user, string $sessionId): Media` (tenant + ownership checks; callers: QuoteService create/update, InvoiceSettingsService::update for Tenant `signature`) · `delete(string $uuid, ?User, string $sessionId): void` · `purgeOlderThan(int $hours=24): int` (runs unbound in console, sees all tenants intentionally).
- App\Services\MediaService (final readonly): `index(MediaIndexFilterData): LengthAwarePaginator<Media>` (QueryBuilder Media::inTenant) · `show(Media): MediaDetailData` (policy gate).
- App\Services\MediaUrlResolver::resolve(?string $modelType, string|int|null $modelId): array{label,url} — reads config/media-urls.php (no tenant_id reference today, consumers MediaListItemData pass resolved URL).

**Satellites (FE):**
- Pages/Media/Index.vue — {media: Paginator<MediaListItemData>; filters?} · DataTable · formatBytes.
- Pages/Media/Show.vue — {media: MediaDetailData} · image preview.
- FileUploadInput.vue (form component) — posts to /uploads (hard-coded :41) → 201 {uuid,name,file_name,mime_type,size,url}; DELETE /uploads/{uuid} → 204; v-model uuid|uuid[]. RichTextEditorInput reuses endpoint for inline images.

**DTOs:**
- MediaListItemData::fromModel (app(MediaUrlResolver::class)), MediaDetailData, MediaIndexFilterData (#[MapInputName('filter.*')]), StoreTemporaryUploadData (file|max:10240).

**Policies:**
- MediaPolicy (viewAny|view → `can('view media')`) — viewAny via enum, view(User, Media) via enum + `media->tenant_id === current_tenant_id`. Registered explicitly in AuthServiceProvider::$policies.
- TemporaryUploadPolicy (create|delete → `can('upload files')`) — class-level gate; ownership + tenant checks in service via OwnedTemporaryMedia rule (validates uuid + user_id + tenant_id match).
- App\Rules\OwnedTemporaryMedia (validates form field holds uuid of a TemporaryUpload owned by actor in current tenant).

**Commands:**
- App\Console\Commands\PurgeTemporaryUploadsCommand (app:purge-temporary-uploads {--hours=24}) daily in routes/console.php (runs unbound, purges all tenants).

**Flow:**
- POST /uploads (multipart file, inside tenant.required) → #[Authorize('upload files')] → TemporaryUploadService::store → 201 JSON {uuid,name,file_name,mime_type,size,url}. DELETE /uploads/{uuid} → 204. Consumer contract: form DTO holds uuid validated by OwnedTemporaryMedia → service calls moveToModel($owner,'collection',$uuid) (tenant + ownership checks inline).
- GET /media → MediaController@index (#[Authorize('view media')]) → QueryBuilder::for(Media::inTenant(current_tenant_id)) + AllowedFilter filters → Inertia render Media/Index {media: Paginator<MediaListItemData>, filters}.
- GET /media/{media} → MediaController@show (Model binding with tenant scope implied, #[Authorize('view media')]) → MediaPolicy::view (tenant_id check) → Inertia render Media/Show {media: MediaDetailData}.

**Depends on:** tenancy (Media.tenant_id, TemporaryUpload tenant FK + TenantScope, inTenant scope, Tenant now HasMedia owner), identity (user_id FKs), auth, AllowedFilter, config/media-urls.php.

**Media collections in use (verified registerMediaCollections/addMediaCollection grep, 2026-10-31):** TemporaryUpload `default` (public MEDIA_DISK, 24h purge) · Document `file` + `pdf` (both singleFile, disk config('documents.disk') = env DOCUMENTS_DISK, default local; replaces Quote `document`, Quote is no longer HasMedia) · Tenant `signature` (multi-row, config('invoicing.signature.disk')) · Invoice `efa_xml` (written by EInvoiceService::submit via addMediaFromString, disk = invoicing.signature.disk) · Task `attachments` (2026-11-01, multi-row, not singleFile, disk config('tasks.attachments.disk') = env DOCUMENTS_DISK; the Task owner implements HasMedia, see Cross-cutting MediaLibrary). Client / CleaningObject / TenantMembership / Contract / DocumentTemplate have NO media collection of their own: employee files, quote files and the signed-contract PDF snapshot all hang off a `Document` (morph `documentable`). Invoice PDFs and unsigned-contract / quote PDFs are NOT stored (rendered per request). MediaService::index and MediaPolicy::view exclude `model_type` Document and Task (documents alias to FQCN; ID scans must not show up for holders of `view media`; Task media would bypass `Task::isVisibleTo`), so quote files and task attachments also left this viewer.

**Depended on by:** documents (Document `file`/`pdf` collections; EmployeeDocumentService streams them), Quote file upload (moveToModel onto the Quote's Document), Tenant signature (Invoice signature snapshots reference media id), Invoice efa_xml, FileUploadInput (FE form component), task attachments (Task `attachments`, written directly by TaskAttachmentService, no staging), any future stored attachments (use moveToModel + OwnedTemporaryMedia + private DOCUMENTS_DISK).

**If you change Core, check:**
- config/media-urls.php: **User::class => users.edit (param user), Tenant::class => ['name' => 'settings.invoicing']** (no 'param'; MediaUrlResolver builds `route($name, $params)` with optional param). Quote and Invoice owners are NOT mapped (media viewer shows no owner link for them) — add an entry when a new media owner (e.g. Contract) is introduced.
- TemporaryUploadService: store (app('current_tenant_id') required), moveToModel (OwnedTemporaryMedia validation, tenant + ownership checks), purgeOlderThan (unbound console, all tenants).
- OwnedTemporaryMedia rule: validates uuid + user_id + tenant_id match current context.
- MediaPolicy: tenant_id === current_tenant_id guard on view.
- Tenant::registerMediaCollections(): adds 'signature' collection with config('invoicing.signature.disk') (NOT singleFile — superseded rows retained). 2026-11-03 to 2026-11-05 it also had a 'logo' collection (singleFile); removed 2026-11-05 with the firm logo feature. Existing `logo` media rows stay in place, nothing reads them.
- FileUploadInput.vue (FE): posts to /uploads (hard-coded, inside tenant.required group), deletes to /uploads/{uuid}.
- Media::creating Task branch (owner lookup, `media.owner_missing`) and migration 2026_11_02_000200 CHECK: a personal task's file must stay tenant NULL. Tests: tests/Feature/Tasks/TaskAttachmentApiTest.php, tests/Feature/Media/MediaControllerTest.php.

**Keywords:** media, upload, temporary upload, HasMedia (Spatie), collection, moveToModel, OwnedTemporaryMedia, purge, tenant_id scope, media-urls, FileUploadInput, task attachment, media_tenant_required_unless_task, media.owner_missing.

### api-me — Sanctum API self-capability endpoint

**Core:**
- App\Http\Controllers\Api\MeController (invokable, `GET /api/me`, Sanctum auth) — #[Group('Auth')], #[Authenticated], #[Endpoint('Me', ...)], #[ResponseFromSpatieData(MeData::class, User::class)]. Returns current user's active tenant + permissions.
- App\Data\Auth\MeData (#[TypeScript]) — **Phase 11:** userId, name, email, locale (SupportedLanguage), activeTenantId: string (non-null: route behind tenant.required), permissions: PermissionEnum[] (derived from $user->getAllPermissions() filtered to active tenant team scope via PermissionRegistrar::setPermissionsTeamId before query; FE converts to union for type-safe checks), companies: list<MobileCompanyData> (tenant_id, name, color, mobile_access, permissions per company, built from `TenantPermissionSnapshot::for($user)` in MeController).
- Middleware: `/api` group order `auth:sanctum` → `locale.api` → `user.active` → `ability:mobile` → `tenant.context` → `tenant.required` (routes/api.php; Phase 11 — previously `['auth:sanctum', 'tenant.context', 'tenant.required']`).
- No policy gate (returns caller's own capabilities, documented in controller).

**Flow:**
- GET /api/me (Sanctum Bearer) — resolves user via Sanctum, TenantContextMiddleware (D5: X-Tenant-Id header precedence → session → first active membership), setPermissionsTeamId(resolved), getAllPermissions()->pluck('name') → map to PermissionEnum cases, return MeData. On foreign tenant header → 403 `tenant_forbidden`; on zero memberships → 403 `no_active_tenant`.

**Depends on:** tenancy (active tenant resolution), identity (user permissions per team).

**Depended on by:** mobile app (repo `cleaning-software-app`; consumer of MeData.companies and permissions — consumer code not verified in this repo).

**Keywords:** api, sanctum, bearer, me, capabilities, tenant context, permissions per team.

### tasks — firm and global tasks, assignment, reminders, attachments, comments and AI runs (Phase 11, 2026-11-01)

**Core:**
- App\Models\Task — UUIDv7 (HasUuids), LogsActivity, SoftDeletes, `#[ScopedBy(TaskScope::class)]`, **HasMedia** (collection `attachments`, see the Attachments bullet). **Not** BelongsToTenant: `tenant_id` nullable; NULL = global task owned by `created_by_user_id`. Columns (migration 2026_11_01_000200): tenant_id FK nullable → tenants (restrictOnDelete), created_by_user_id FK → users (restrictOnDelete), assigned_membership_id FK nullable → tenant_memberships (restrictOnDelete), status (TaskStatusEnum open/in_progress/waiting/nice_to_have/done, default open; `cancelled` retired 2026-11-06, see Gotchas), priority, due_at, remind_at, reminded_at, completed_at, source (TaskSourceEnum manual/voice/assistant/mcp), taskable_type + taskable_id (polymorphic, no FK). Indexes (tenant_id, status, due_at), (tenant_id, assigned_membership_id), (taskable_type, taskable_id).
- App\Scopes\TaskScope (fail-closed): bound tenant's rows OR the authenticated user's own global rows (`tenant_id IS NULL AND created_by_user_id = auth id`); neither bound → `1 = 0`.
- `Task::unscopedForSystem()` — the single scope bypass (`withoutGlobalScope(TaskScope::class)`). Callers: `TaskService::findForSystem`, `TaskService::sendReminder`, `RemindTasks`. Listeners and the confirm path load via `findForSystem`.
- Task::scopeVisibleTo(User) / isVisibleTo(User): global rows only for their creator; firm rows need `view tasks`; `view all tasks` widens to every firm row; otherwise created-by-me or assigned to my active membership. `isGlobal()` = `tenant_id === null`.
- App\Services\TaskService (final) — paginate, listAcrossCompanies, board, boardAcrossCompanies (2026-11-06), formContext, detail, assertMayCreate, create (optional `status`, 2026-11-06), update, changeStatus (2026-11-06, the single status write), complete and reopen (both delegate to changeStatus), assign, delete, findForSystem, unassignForMembership(membership, onlyOpen = true), sendReminder, remindAtFor (private, update rule below).
- **Reminder slots (2026-11-04):** App\Enums\TaskReminderSlotEnum (#[TypeScript], string-backed: morning 07, noon 12, evening 19; `TIMEZONE` Europe/Bratislava) is the only owner of slot hours and rounding. `nextAtOrAfter(instant)` = UTC result, minute truncated, first slot at or after it (rounds UP); `instantOn(Y-m-d)` = slot instant on a local date; `dateAndSlotFor(instant)` = [local Y-m-d, slot] of that same result; `firstAfter(instant)` = strictly after the minute (default picker slot).
- App\Data\Tasks\TaskUpsertData write rule: `remind_on` + `remind_slot` are required together and `remind_at` is prohibited with a slot (`withValidator`, Spatie skips defaulted properties). `remindAtUtc()`: slot instant when the pair is set; else `nextAtOrAfter(remind_at)`; else null. MCP `create_task` / `update_task` schema descriptions say the value is rounded up to 07:00, 12:00 or 19:00 Europe/Bratislava; no new argument.
- TaskService::update keeps the stored `remind_at` (private `remindAtFor`) when the requested minute equals it, or when `remind_slot` is set and `[remind_on, remind_slot] === dateAndSlotFor(stored)`. Any other change stores the new value and resets `reminded_at` (only when the stored minute changes). Title-only edits of a legacy off-slot row therefore neither shift nor re-arm it.
- Web FE (2026-11-04): `resources/js/utils/taskReminder.ts` (pure: `initialReminderState`, `buildReminderPayload`, `findReminderSlot`, `formatSlotTime`); `Components/Tasks/TaskForm.vue` toggle + DateInput + RadioGroup (sends `{remind_on: null, remind_slot: null}` when off, `{remind_at: stored}` when unchanged, else the pair); `Pages/Tasks/Show.vue` shows "date, Slot (H:mm)".
- App\Policies\TaskPolicy — viewAny, viewAnyAcrossCompanies, view, create(?tenantId), assignIn, update, delete (second layer for global ownership), deleteAttachment(User, Task, Media) + mayDeleteAttachment (global: creator only; firm: task visible AND (uploader OR `delete tasks`)). Adding attachments uses `update`; listing and download use `view`. 2026-11-06: static `changeAllowed(bool $personalTask, bool $isCreator, bool $holdsPermission)` is the one rule for who may change a task (personal = creator only, firm = permission); `mayChange` and the list `can_update` both call it.
- Enums: TaskStatusEnum, TaskPriorityEnum (low/normal/high), TaskSourceEnum (manual/voice/assistant/mcp), TaskIndexViewEnum (table/board, 2026-11-06).
- **Statuses and board (2026-11-06, Core rule):** `TaskStatusEnum` = open "Na spracovanie", in_progress "Rozpracovaná", waiting "Čaká", nice_to_have "Nice to have", done "Hotová". `isActive()` (everything but done), `static activeValues()` and `static boardOrder()` are the single owner of "unfinished" and of the column order; `Task::isOpen()` was renamed `Task::isActive()` (`status->isActive()`), so "open" means only the status. Every "unfinished" check uses `whereIn('status', TaskStatusEnum::activeValues())` or `isActive()`: reminders (RemindTasks, sendReminder claim), NotifyTaskDue, AI run start and auto-close, answers (TaskCommentService, TaskRunLookup::can_answer), unassignForMembership (`onlyOpen`), complete (422 `task_not_open`) and reopen (422 `task_not_closed`, target `open`). Partial index `tasks_pending_reminder_idx` (`remind_at WHERE reminded_at IS NULL AND status IN` the four active values) is rebuilt by migration `2026_11_13_000500_task_board_statuses`, which also maps any `cancelled` row (soft-deleted included) to done with `completed_at`. The status write is `changeStatus`: same status = no write; done sets `completed_at = now()`; others clear it; `reminded_at` untouched. No event, no notification.
- **Board read model and list fields (2026-11-06):** `TaskBoardData {columns}` of `TaskBoardColumnData {status, total, tasks, has_more}`, one column per `boardOrder()` status. Ignores `filter[status]`, `sort`, `page`, `per_page`. Limit `config('tasks.board.column_limit')` = 50. Filters shared with the list (one private builder `filtered()`): `filter[status]` comma list (`whereIn`), `filter[assignee]=me|none|<membership uuid>` (`me` = actor's active memberships, works across companies), `filter[due]=overdue|today|week|none` (local days in `TaskReminderSlotEnum::TIMEZONE`; `week` = today + 6 days; `overdue` = active and `due_at < now()`), `sort=priority` (CASE rank, then created_at desc, id). `TaskListItemData` (list and board only) gains `comments_count` (human comments, `author_kind = user`, `TaskCommentScope` removed inside the subquery only), `attachments_count` (collection `attachments`), `can_update` (from per-tenant rights map + `TaskPolicy::changeAllowed`, no per-row gate queries), `is_active`; `TaskDetailData` gains `is_active`. These counts never go on `hydrate()` / `detail()`.
- **Create into a column (2026-11-06):** `TaskUpsertData.status` is optional, create only, `In(activeValues)`; `done` → 422 on `status`. Update ignores it.
- **Comments and AI runs (2026-11-01):**
  - App\Models\TaskComment — UUIDv7, SoftDeletes, LogsActivity (`body` is never logged; personal-task comments use `keepNullTenant`), `#[ScopedBy(TaskCommentScope::class)]`. **Not** BelongsToTenant (rule-6 exception): `tenant_id` nullable, set by TaskCommentService from the task only (NULL on a personal task). `user_id` NOT NULL = accountable person (the writer, or the run starter for `author_kind = ai`). `author_kind` user|ai, `task_run_id` (ai only; CHECK `(author_kind = 'ai') = (task_run_id IS NOT NULL)`), `answers_comment_id` self-FK. Constants `MAX_BODY_CHARS` 2000, `MAX_PER_TASK` 200. No `$touches` on Task: a comment changes no Task attribute.
  - App\Scopes\TaskCommentScope (fail-closed): bound tenant's rows OR `tenant_id IS NULL AND user_id = auth id`; neither bound → `1 = 0`.
  - App\Models\TaskRun — UUIDv7, BelongsToTenant (`tenant_id` NOT NULL = the company the run acts in), no LogsActivity, no SoftDeletes. Columns: task_id, started_by_user_id, status (TaskRunStatusEnum), closed_task, actions (jsonb, display snapshot of AssistantActionData), started_at, finished_at. Partial unique index `task_runs_one_active_per_task_idx` on `task_id WHERE status IN ('queued','running')`. `isStale()`: queued and older than `ai.task_run.stale_minutes` (15) from created_at; running and older than `ai.task_run.running_stale_seconds` (120) from started_at.
  - Task::comments() HasMany. Task::aiRuns() and latestAiRun() drop TenantScope on the relation only (the parent Task already passed TaskScope; a personal task's run can belong to a company other than the bound one). Task::unscopedForSystem() gets no new caller.
  - App\Services\TaskCommentService — add (tenant copied from the task, never from input), addForRun, delete, findForSystem, accountableUserId.
  - App\Services\TaskRunService (final) — start: company required on a personal task; `startAiRun` gate; task unfinished (`isActive()`, 2026-11-06); expireIfStale on the latest run; the starter's live pending actions on this task → 422 `status`; `LaravelAiTaskRunner::isAvailable()` false → 422 `ai`; inserts a queued TaskRun and dispatches RunTaskWithAi afterCommit; unique violation → 422 `status`. execute(runId): atomic claim queued → running, runs under `ToolContext::run(starter, run tenant)`, no DB transaction around the model call. markFailed, expireIfStale (re-checked on the locked row), failStale (sweep, limit 200).
  - App\Services\Ai\TaskRunFinisher — finish: maps the outcome to a status by the server's table (blocked, failed, could_not_finish, needs_input, awaiting_confirmation, done); writes exactly one AI comment per terminal run; closes the task through TaskService::complete only when outcome done, no write refused by a cap or the time budget, no live pending action linked to this task, task still unfinished (`isActive()`), starter has `update`. endWithNotice(runId, status, noticeKey, onlyIfStale) writes the fixed-text comment.
  - App\Services\Ai\LaravelAiTaskRunner (implements App\Contracts\Ai\RunsTasks; AssistantTurn pinned to the run's company; TaskRunContextBuilder, TaskRunRequest, TaskRunResult). App\Ai\Tools\ReportTaskOutcomeTool — run-only, records the outcome (done|question|cannot), not in CleanMasterServer::TOOLS. AssistantToolbox::forTaskRun(turn) = toolClasses() minus EXCLUDED_FROM_TASK_RUN (ListCompaniesTool, CompanyOverviewTool, CompleteTaskTool, UpdateTaskTool, AssignTaskTool) plus ReportTaskOutcomeTool.
  - App\Services\TaskThreadService — for(Task, User) and runStatus(Task, User): the single read side. O4 audience through TaskRunLookup::visibleTo → TaskPolicy::viewAiOutput (firm task: starter or `view all tasks`; personal task: creator). Also feeds TaskListItemData.ai_run_status and the TaskController::show `thread` prop.
  - App\Jobs\RunTaskWithAi — `Tries(1)`, `Timeout(85)`, `FailOnTimeout`, no `#[Queue]`: queue name from `config('ai.task_run.queue')` at dispatch (env `AI_TASK_RUN_QUEUE`, default `ai`). `failed()` → TaskRunService::markFailed. The only queued AI call.
  - Command App\Console\Commands\ExpireTaskRuns (`cleanup:task-runs`, daily 12:00 Europe/Bratislava, routes/console.php) → failStale. No 5-minute sweep.
  - Events TaskCommented (after commit) and TaskRunFinished (after commit). Listeners NotifyTaskCommented (creator, assignee, accountable user of the answered question, minus the author; returns early on global tasks) and NotifyTaskRunFinished (starter only; skipped when the starter is inactive or, on a firm task, lost membership). Notifications TaskCommentedNotification and TaskRunFinishedNotification extend TaskNotification (url `route('tasks.show', id)`, no comment body, status text fixed per run status).
  - Enums: TaskCommentAuthorEnum (user|ai), TaskRunStatusEnum (queued, running, done, needs_input, awaiting_confirmation, could_not_finish, blocked, failed), TaskRunOutcomeEnum (done|question|cannot).
  - Permission `run tasks with ai` (PermissionEnum::RunTasksWithAi, group `tasks`): Admin and Vedúca. Existing tenants by migration 2026_11_04_000400, new tenants by RoleTemplatesSeeder.
- Events: TaskAssigned, TaskDue (both `ShouldDispatchAfterCommit`). Listeners NotifyTaskAssigned / NotifyTaskDue → TaskAssignedNotification / TaskDueNotification (extend TaskNotification → BaseTenantNotification, queued).
- **Attachments (2026-11-01, merged):** App\Services\TaskAttachmentService (final readonly): `list(Task, User, string $downloadRoute): TaskAttachmentListData` (oldest first, capped at `max_count`, not paginated; `can_add` = `update` policy); `add(Task, UploadedFile, User): Media` (DB transaction, `lockForUpdate` on the task row, count check → 422 on `file`, `usingName(cleanName)`, `usingFileName('{uuid}.{ext}')`, custom properties `uploaded_by_user_id` + `uploaded_by_name`, canonical `mime_type` saved after the Media Library write); `find(Task, uuid)` (`firstOrFail` on the task's `attachments` collection); `delete(Task, Media, User)` (`Gate::forUser` `deleteAttachment`, hard delete in a transaction, audit); `stream(Media)` (inline for jpeg/png/webp/pdf, `nosniff`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`); `static cleanName(string)` (path tail, invisible characters, Cc, trim Z, 120 code points keeping a 2–11 character extension). App\Rules\TaskAttachmentFile: `static resolve(UploadedFile): ?array{mime, extension}` is the only owner of the type table (config/tasks.php `attachments.types`, zip and OLE fallbacks by client extension); `detectedMimes()` feeds `Task::registerMediaCollections` `acceptsMimeTypes`. Collection not singleFile, disk `config('tasks.attachments.disk')`.

**Satellites:**
- DTOs in: TaskUpsertData, TaskAssignData, TaskParseData. Out: TaskListItemData, TaskDetailData, TaskFormContextData, TaskDraftData, TaskCanData, TaskSubjectData, TaskMemberOptionData.
