Reminder slots (2026-11-04): in `TaskUpsertData` `remind_on` (Y-m-d) + `remind_slot`; out `TaskReminderSlotData` {slot, hour} (`forAllSlots()` → `TaskFormContextData.reminder_slots`), `TaskFormContextData.default_remind_on` + `default_remind_slot`, `TaskDetailData.remind_on` + `remind_slot` (derived from stored `remind_at` via `dateAndSlotFor`, null when `remind_at` is null; `remind_at` stays for display). Attachments: out TaskAttachmentData {id (media uuid), name, mime_type, size, previewable, uploaded_by_name, created_at, download_url, can_delete}, TaskAttachmentListData {attachments, can_add, constraints}, TaskAttachmentConstraintsData (`fromConfig()`); in TaskAttachmentUploadData (`file`, messages map to `app.task_attachment_too_large`). `TaskDetailData` gains `attachments_count: int`, passed in as an argument.
- Command: App\Console\Commands\RemindTasks (`app:remind-tasks`, registered at 07:00, 12:00, 19:00 Europe/Bratislava: one `Schedule` entry per `TaskReminderSlotEnum` case in routes/console.php, withoutOverlapping): unfinished tasks (`status IN TaskStatusEnum::activeValues()`, 2026-11-06) with `remind_at <= now` and `reminded_at IS NULL` → `sendReminder` (claims the row with `UPDATE ... reminded_at`, dispatches TaskDue only when the claim succeeds).
- Controllers: web TaskController (`/tasks`); API Api\TaskController + Api\TaskParseController (`/api/tasks`, see assistant); attachments: web App\Http\Controllers\TaskAttachmentController (redirects) and Api\TaskAttachmentController (JSON, Scribe `#[ResponseFromSpatieData]` on list and upload).
- MCP tools: list_tasks, get_task, create_task, update_task, complete_task, assign_task, delete_global_task (excluded from the assistant toolbox). `get_task` is unchanged: no thread, comment or run data reaches MCP or the assistant (TaskDetailData unchanged).
- Comments and runs DTOs: out TaskThreadData, TaskCommentData, TaskRunData, TaskRunStatusData, TaskQuestionData, TaskRunCompanyData; in TaskCommentStoreData (`body`, `answers_comment_id`), TaskRunStartData (`company_id`, required on a personal task). Controllers: web TaskCommentController (thread, store, destroy) and TaskRunController (status, store); API Api\TaskCommentController (thread, store 201, destroy) and Api\TaskRunController (status, store 202), Scribe `#[ResponseFromSpatieData]`.
- FE comments and AI run (web): Pages/Tasks/Show.vue (prop `thread`; left column order Description, TaskAiCard, TaskAttachmentsCard, TaskCommentsCard), Components/Tasks/{TaskAiCard, TaskCommentsCard, TaskCommentItem, TaskCommentForm, TaskAiRunBadge}.vue, Composables/useTaskAiRun.ts (polls GET /tasks/{task}/thread every 3 s through `getJson` while `latest_run.is_active`; at run end one partial `router.reload` with `['thread','flash']`, or `['task','thread','flash']` when `closed_task`), utils/taskAi.ts (pure rules). Pages/Tasks/Index.vue shows TaskAiRunBadge for queued, running, needs_input, awaiting_confirmation. Comment text is interpolated, never `v-html`.
- FE attachments (web, Inertia): Pages/Tasks/Show.vue (prop `attachments: TaskAttachmentListData`; `useTaskAttachmentUpload`; disables Complete, Reopen, Edit and Delete while uploading; `router.on('before')` asks before leaving during an upload), Components/Tasks/TaskAttachmentsCard.vue (queue rows, image grid, file rows, delete via `useDeleteConfirm`), TaskAttachmentPreviewModal.vue (`<dialog>`), Components/Forms/FilePickerButton.vue, Composables/useTaskAttachmentUpload.ts (one `router.post` per file, `forceFormData`, `only: ['attachments']`, `preserveState`), utils/taskAttachments.ts (accept list, rejection, camera names). The mobile client is in repo `cleaning-software-app`, not in this repo.

**Flow:**
- Create: TaskUpsertData → TaskPolicy::create (tenant or global) → TaskService::create (created_by = actor; `assigned_membership_id` prohibited when tenant_id null — DTO rule) → LogsActivity.
- Assign: TaskAssignData → membership must belong to the task's tenant → TaskService::assign → TaskAssigned (after commit) unless the actor assigns to self.
- Status change (2026-11-06): web `POST /tasks/{task}/status` (`tasks.status`, flash `task_status_changed`, redirect back) or API `POST /api/tasks/{task}/status` (returns TaskDetailData) → TaskStatusChangeData {status} → `#[Authorize('update', 'task')]` → TaskService::changeStatus (same status = no write; `done` sets completed_at, any other status clears it; reminded_at untouched; no event, no notification). complete/reopen keep their 422 guards and delegate to changeStatus.
- Board read (2026-11-06): web `GET /tasks?view=table|board` (session `tasks.index_view` remembers the last valid view; only the requested shape is computed: paginator or `board`) and API `GET /api/tasks/board?scope=` → TaskService::board / boardAcrossCompanies (same visibility bases as the list, same filters except `filter[status]`, sort and paging) → one grouped count + one query per status capped at `tasks.board.column_limit` (50), `limit + 1` gives `has_more`. Order inside active columns: priority rank, `due_at` nulls last, `created_at` desc; Hotová: `completed_at` desc nulls last, `updated_at` desc.
- Reminder set (2026-11-04): web TaskForm and mobile picker send `remind_on` + `remind_slot` → TaskUpsertData::remindAtUtc stores the slot instant (UTC). `remind_at` from MCP, the assistant or an older mobile build is rounded UP to the next slot. Edit keeps the stored value per the update rule above.
- Reminder fire: `app:remind-tasks` at a slot → TaskService::sendReminder → TaskDue (after commit) → NotifyTaskDue → TaskDueNotification. A row due between slots fires at the next slot run.
- Membership removal: EmployeeService (deactivate) and UserService (`unassignForMembership(onlyOpen: true)` on deactivate, `onlyOpen: false` on delete) release assignments so the restrict FK does not block. 2026-11-01: `EmployeeAccessService::revoke` calls `unassignForMembership` (unfinished tasks only, i.e. `TaskStatusEnum::activeValues()`; done tasks keep the assignee). 2026-11-06: the four unfinished statuses are all released. Task assignees must have `user_id` (TaskService::formContext, TaskAssignData, TaskUpsertData new-task rule, TaskParseService::members all `whereNotNull('user_id')`); a no-access employee cannot hold tasks.
- Comment: POST /tasks/{task}/comments → TaskCommentStoreData (body ≤ 2000 chars) → TaskPolicy::comment (task visible) → TaskCommentService::add (tenant from the task; `answers_comment_id` must be the current open question) → TaskCommented after commit. A plain comment or an answer never creates a run.
- AI run: POST /tasks/{task}/ai-runs → TaskRunStartData → TaskRunService::start (gates and 422 rules above) → queued TaskRun + RunTaskWithAi (afterCommit, queue `ai`) → worker: execute (claim) → ToolContext::run(starter) → LaravelAiTaskRunner::run → TaskRunFinisher::finish (one AI comment, status, optional close) → TaskRunFinished after commit → NotifyTaskRunFinished (starter only). Continuing ("Pokračovať") is a second POST ai-runs, not an answer.
- Read: GET /tasks/{task}/thread and GET /tasks/{task}/ai-run-status → TaskThreadService (O4: AI comments, latest_run, question and pending actions only for the audience). A stale run is ended on read (expireIfStale) and on start.
- Routes web: GET /tasks (`?view=table|board`, 2026-11-06), GET|DELETE /tasks/{task}, POST /tasks/{task}/complete|reopen|status (`tasks.status`, 2026-11-06); with the write permission group: POST /tasks, PUT|PATCH /tasks/{task}, POST /tasks/{task}/assign; attachments: GET|POST /tasks/{task}/attachments (POST in the `task-attachments` throttle), GET|DELETE /tasks/{task}/attachments/{attachment}; comments and runs: GET /tasks/{task}/thread, POST /tasks/{task}/comments (throttle `task-comments` 30/min), DELETE /tasks/{task}/comments/{comment}, GET /tasks/{task}/ai-run-status, POST /tasks/{task}/ai-runs (throttle `task-ai-runs` 10/min). API under `/api` (ability:mobile): GET|POST /tasks, GET /tasks/board (`scope=all` for across companies, 2026-11-06), GET /tasks/form-context, GET|PUT|PATCH|DELETE /tasks/{task}, POST /tasks/{task}/complete|reopen|status|assign, POST /tasks/parse, GET|POST /tasks/{task}/attachments, GET|DELETE /tasks/{task}/attachments/{attachment} (`{attachment}` = media uuid), and the same thread, comment and run routes under `api.` names.

**Attachment flow:** POST → `TaskAttachmentUploadData` (`file`: required, file, max 10240 kB, `TaskAttachmentFile`) → controller `#[Authorize('update', 'task')]` → `TaskAttachmentService::add` (row lock, count ≤ 20, store) → `Media::creating` takes the owner's tenant_id → audit `attachment_added`. DELETE → `#[Authorize('view', 'task')]` → `find` (404 for another task or a soft-deleted task, since the binding excludes trashed tasks) → `deleteAttachment` → hard delete → audit `attachment_deleted`. Task soft delete keeps the files on disk; no purge job.

**Depends on:** identity (`view|create|edit|delete tasks`, `view all tasks`), tenancy (tenant_id NOT scoped by BelongsToTenant; memberships FK), notifications (TaskAssigned/TaskDue channels), audit (LogsActivity; Activity keepNullTenant), pending-actions (`delete_task` prepare; global tasks refused by ActionConfirmationService::describe), media (Task `attachments`: tenant NULL rule, DOCUMENTS_DISK), rate limiters `task-attachments` 60/min, `task-comments` 30/min, `task-ai-runs` 10/min (AppServiceProvider, per user); queue `ai` and the AI task run (Queue section, Cross-cutting "Sync AI calls"); assistant tool layer (`AssistantToolbox::forTaskRun`, `McpToolAdapter` company pin); pending-actions (channel `task_run`, `task_run_id`); notifications (`task.commented`, `task.ai_run`); prompt guard (`PromptGuard::scanValues`, nonce block).

**Depended on by:** web Pages/Tasks/Index (view switch, board) and Show (status menu), API GET /api/tasks/board and POST /api/tasks/{task}/status (mobile `TasksApiService`), ListTasksTool (`assignee`, `due`, status enum), EmployeeService, UserService (unassign), ActionConfirmationService (`delete_task` via findForSystem), NotifyTaskAssigned, NotifyTaskDue, RemindTasks, MCP task tools, AssistantTurn (write map for create/update/complete/assign), TaskService::detail (sole caller of `TaskDetailData::fromModel`; web, API and MCP `get_task` responses), TaskController::show (`attachments` and `thread` props), TaskListItemData (`ai_run_status`), TaskRunFinisher (auto-close via TaskService::complete), NotifyTaskCommented, NotifyTaskRunFinished, ExpireTaskRuns, PendingAction rows with channel `task_run` (ActionConfirmationService::ttlMinutes, 1440).

**If you change Core, check:**
- `Task::unscopedForSystem()` callers: TaskService (findForSystem, sendReminder), RemindTasks, DeleteGlobalTaskTool via findForSystem. Any new bypass is a review finding.
- TaskScope / scopeVisibleTo / isVisibleTo: tests/Unit/Scopes/TaskScopeTest.php, tests/Feature/Tasks/TaskScopingTest.php, tests/Feature/Tasks/GlobalTaskTest.php.
- TaskSourceEnum consumers: CreateTaskTool (`mcp`), assistant path (`assistant`), TaskParseService.
- AssistantTurn::WRITES keys (create_task, update_task, complete_task, assign_task) — tool names must match.
- TaskStatusEnum / `status` values (2026-11-06): every unfinished check uses `activeValues()` / `isActive()`. `TaskStatusEnum::Open` under app/ is only the create default (TaskService::create) and the reopen target (TaskService::reopen). Consumers: TaskService (changeStatus, board, filters, unassign, sendReminder), TaskRunService, TaskRunLookup, TaskCommentService, TaskRunFinisher, NotifyTaskDue, RemindTasks, ListTasksTool (enum from `cases()`), FE `utils/enums.ts` `TASK_STATUSES` / `TaskStatusBadge`, mobile `taskStatusRules`. A new status value needs: the enum case, the partial index predicate (new migration), the lang keys in sk/en/uk, and the FE/mobile tone maps. The column is varchar(16) with no DB CHECK.
- `TaskService::changeStatus` and `TaskService::board`: tests tests/Feature/Tasks/TaskStatusChangeTest.php, TaskBoardTest.php, TaskListFiltersTest.php, TaskStatusMigrationTest.php; reminders / runs / comments / unassign tests (TaskReminderTest, TaskRunStartTest, TaskRunExecutionTest, TaskCommentTest, EmployeeAccessTest) assert the four unfinished statuses.
- `TaskDetailData::fromModel($task, $can, $attachmentsCount)`: sole caller TaskService::detail, which counts the `attachments` collection. Never `loadCount`: a loaded `*_count` attribute changes the `getAttributes()` fingerprint and cancels a pending `delete_task`.
- TaskAttachmentService / TaskAttachmentFile / config/tasks.php: callers are the web and API attachment controllers only. Media hook and migration CHECK: see the media domain. Tests: tests/Feature/Tasks/TaskAttachmentApiTest.php, tests/Feature/Tasks/TaskAttachmentWebTest.php, tests/Unit/Services/TaskAttachmentNameTest.php, tests/Feature/Mcp/McpToolsReadTest.php (only `attachments_count`), tests/Feature/Mcp/ActionConfirmationTest.php (count does not cancel `delete_task`).
- TaskCommentScope / TaskCommentService / TaskPolicy::viewAiOutput / TaskRunLookup: tests/Unit/Scopes/TaskCommentScopeTest.php, tests/Feature/Tasks/TaskCommentTest.php, tests/Feature/Tasks/TaskThreadVisibilityTest.php. A `tenant_id` taken from input is a review finding.
- TaskRunFinisher mapping table, TaskRunService, RunTaskWithAi: tests/Feature/Tasks/TaskRunStartTest.php, TaskRunExecutionTest.php, TaskRunLifecycleTest.php, TaskRunGuardTest.php, TaskRunGlobalTaskTest.php, TaskRunNotificationTest.php, tests/Feature/Ai/LaravelAiTaskRunnerTest.php.
- Never `loadCount` on task paths for comments or runs (fingerprint of a pending `delete_task`). Auto-close changes `status` and cancels such a pending action at confirm, by design.
- AssistantToolbox::EXCLUDED_FROM_TASK_RUN: a run must not change existing tasks, so it may not gain complete/update/assign or company-wide tools.

- TaskReminderSlotEnum / TaskUpsertData::remindAtUtc / TaskService::remindAtFor consumers: TaskUpsertData, TaskService (formContext, create, update), TaskDetailData, TaskFormContextData, TaskReminderSlotData, routes/console.php (schedule loop), FE `utils/taskReminder.ts`, `TaskForm.vue`, `Pages/Tasks/Show.vue`. Tests: tests/Unit/Enums/TaskReminderSlotEnumTest.php, tests/Feature/Console/ScheduleTest.php, tests/Feature/Tasks/TaskCrudTest.php. Slot hours 7/12/19 live only in the enum on the BE (the mobile side is in the cross-repo push line, TODO verify there).

**Keywords (SK):** nástenka, board, Nástenka, Na spracovanie, Rozpracovaná, Čaká, Nice to have, Hotová, changeStatus, activeValues, isActive, boardOrder, column_limit, filter[assignee], filter[due], TaskIndexViewEnum, tasks.index_view, úloha, task, globálna úloha, global task, priorita, pripomienka, reminder, remind_at, remind_on, remind_slot, TaskReminderSlotEnum, nextAtOrAfter, Ráno, Obed, Večer, assign, priradiť, TaskScope, unscopedForSystem, created_by_user_id, view all tasks, príloha, attachment, attachments_count, TaskAttachmentService, cleanName, komentár, comment, TaskComment, TaskCommentScope, Nech to spraví AI, AI run, TaskRun, TaskRunService, TaskRunFinisher, RunTaskWithAi, run tasks with ai, could_not_finish, awaiting_confirmation, odpoveď, Pokračovať.

### expenses — expense ledger, receipts, bank statement (camt.053) import, AI proposals (Phase 11, 2026-11-01)

**Core:**
- App\Models\Expense — UUIDv7, BelongsToTenant (tenant_id NOT NULL, restrictOnDelete), InteractsWithMedia (receipt), LogsActivity, SoftDeletes. Columns (migration 2026_11_01_000500): created_by_user_id FK → users (restrictOnDelete), status (ExpenseStatusEnum draft/confirmed, default draft), category (ExpenseCategoryEnum, 12 values), source (ExpenseSourceEnum manual/receipt/bank_statement/mcp; migration 2026_11_08_000100 rewrote `bank_csv` to `bank_statement`), date, amount, currency, supplier_name, external_hash (partial unique per tenant `WHERE external_hash IS NOT NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL` — bank statement dedupe).
- App\Services\ExpenseService (final) — paginate, listAcrossCompanies, formContext, detail, assertMayCreate, create/update (take sessionId for receipt staging), confirm (draft → confirmed, ExpenseConfirmData), delete, attachReceipt, analyze, analysisContext. `analyze` returns an ExpenseDraftData proposal and persists nothing.
- App\Services\ExpenseImportService — `preview(UploadedFile, User $actor): ExpenseImportPreviewData` and `import(ExpenseImportData, User $actor): ExpenseImportResultData`. Limits `expenses.statement` (config/expenses.php): max_size_kb 5120, max_rows 2000, max_new_rows 100. Key per row = `sha256('camt053|IBAN|NtryRef')` (`CamtStatementParser`) stored in `expenses.external_hash`. Row states (ExpenseImportRowStateEnum): new, already_imported, possible_match (same amount, ±3 days, unticked), previously_deleted (unticked, can be ticked). Account check (ExpenseImportAccountStatusEnum): matched, other_company (offer to switch firm), unknown_account, company_iban_missing (firm fills `tenants.iban` first). Credits, reversals, non-booked and zero-amount entries are skipped; imports create drafts only (`source = bank_statement`). Persist runs in a transaction with inserts in key order, retries on unique violation (MAX_ATTEMPTS 3). AI categorisation via `AnalyzesExpenses::categorize` in batches of `ai.categorize_batch` (50).
- App\Services\Expenses\{CamtStatementParser, CamtStatement, StatementRow} — camt.053 XML parsing (counterparty text cleaned by App\Support\SafeText). Removed: BankStatementParser, ParsedStatement, StatementExtraction, BankCsvImportData, BankCsvMappingData, `AnalyzesExpenses::mapBankCsv`, `tenant_interfaces.bank_csv_mapping`, config `ai.csv_preview_max_rows`, `/api/expenses/import*`.
- AI port: `AnalyzesExpenses` contract → `LaravelAiExpenseAnalyzer` (bound in AppServiceProvider).
- App\Policies\ExpensePolicy — `view expenses` / `manage expenses`.
- Enums: ExpenseStatusEnum, ExpenseCategoryEnum, ExpenseSourceEnum.

**Satellites:**
- DTOs in: ExpenseUpsertData, ExpenseConfirmData, ExpenseAnalyzeData, ExpenseReceiptUploadData (mime list from `config/expenses.php` `receipt.allowed_mimes`, max 10240 KB default), ExpenseImportData, ExpenseImportRowInputData, ExpenseImportUploadData. Out: ExpenseListItemData, ExpenseDetailData, ExpenseFormContextData, ExpenseDraftData, ExpenseProposalData, ExpenseCategoryProposalData, ExpenseImportPreviewData, ExpenseImportRowData, ExpenseImportAccountCheckData, ExpenseImportResultData, ExpenseRowData.
- Controllers: web ExpenseController (`/expenses`, import page, receipt, analyze, POST `/expenses/import/preview`, POST `/expenses/import`); API Api\ExpenseController (`/api/expenses`, no import routes). FE: Pages/Expenses/Import.vue, ExpenseImportTable, composable useExpenseImport.
- Storage: receipts are Media on `config('expenses.disk')` = env `DOCUMENTS_DISK` (default local).
- Throttle `ai-analyze` (20/min) on analyze and import preview (both web and API).
- MCP tools: list_expenses, create_expense, update_expense, preview_bank_expenses (`#[IsReadOnly]`), import_bank_expenses (`#[IsDestructive(false)]`); both bank tools are in `AssistantToolbox::EXCLUDED`. Delete is prepare-only (`delete_expense` via pending-actions).

**Flow:**
- Receipt/text analyze: ExpenseAnalyzeData → AnalyzesExpenses (sync AI call, see Cross-cutting "Sync AI calls") → ExpenseDraftData proposal → user saves via store (draft) → confirm → confirmed.
- Bank statement, web (file path, byte-exact): upload camt.053 XML → preview (account check, row states, AI-categorised rows) → user ticks rows → import (new keys only, drafts) → ExpenseImportResultData.
- Bank statement, MCP (rows path, 2026-11-01, no upload): Claude reads the statement and sends debit rows → `preview_bank_expenses` / `import_bank_expenses` (Gate `import` on Expense, `manage expenses`) → `App\Services\Expenses\BankExpenseRowsService::preview|import(ExpenseStatementRowsData, User)` → builds a `CamtStatement` and calls `ExpenseImportService::inspect(..., categorize: false)` and `import()`, so key (`camt053|IBAN|NtryRef`), row states, partial unique index, retry and `source = bank_statement` are the web ones and the channels dedupe against each other. States are recomputed inside import; a row is created when `new` or listed in `include_references`; `exclude_references` wins; a listed reference not among the rows is an error and nothing is created. `possible_match` and `previously_deleted` are never created unless listed. No previewed-first check is enforced (stateless server; only the instructions ask for it). Rows capped at `expenses.statement.max_new_rows` (100) per call, counting all sent rows. Input DTOs `ExpenseStatementRowsData` / `ExpenseStatementRowData` (reference charset `^[A-Za-z0-9._:/+\-]+$` max 100, so a value the parser's `Str::trim` would change is refused; amount string with 2 decimals; direction DBIT; currency EUR; counterparty max 200, note max 500). Output DTOs `ExpenseStatementPreviewData` (counts, `amount_total`, rows with `existing_differs`, `text_withheld`), `ExpenseStatementPreviewRowData`, `ExpenseStatementImportResultData` (created/already_imported/excluded, `text_withheld` references, `security_notice`); neither tool echoes counterparty or note. Guard: row `counterparty`/`note` are excluded from the argument scan via `scannedArguments` and scanned in the service instead; a hit nulls both texts, the row still imports, the reference goes to `text_withheld`, `promptGuard->record` writes source `bank_statement_row`, and `restrictMcp` is NOT called (a payer must not be able to lock imports). A flagged `reference`, reference list or unknown row key still arms the lock. Audit via `auditedArguments`: only `company_id`, `rows_count`, `include_count`, `exclude_count`. `/api` has no import route. TODO verify: lang keys `expense_bank_rows_*` names and the exact error paths in the final test run.
- Verified 2026-11-01 (local stack, browser): real statement imported twice, first 6 created with 14 credits skipped, replayed POST and second preview created 0; backend 2297 passed, 1 skipped; vitest 408.
- Open risks (unverified): (1) `NtryRef` staying the same across different exports is NOT verified; importing the same file twice proves only that the same file dedupes. If the bank changes it, rows show as `possible_match`; at the first real overlapping export the overlap must preview as `already_imported`, if it shows `possible_match` stop and redesign the key. (2) If a bank reuses a `NtryRef` for different payments, a genuinely new payment previews as `already_imported`, cannot be ticked and shows no warning. (3) Format verified against one bank's export only (no CSV fallback); `tenants.iban` has no uniqueness rule; an unticked new row reappears ticked next time; a refund does not change the expense; an expense entered by hand after the import gets no duplicate warning; concurrent import is protected by the unique index with inserts in key order, and a repeated third unique violation would return a 500.
- MCP/assistant creates expenses with `source = mcp` (CreateExpenseTool) and drafts stay `draft` until confirmed.

**Depends on:** tenancy (BelongsToTenant), identity (`view|manage expenses`), media (receipt storage), AI (AnalyzesExpenses binding, `ai.*` config), audit (LogsActivity).

**Depended on by:** Api\ExpenseController, ExpenseController (web), PreviewBankExpensesTool / ImportBankExpensesTool (via BankExpenseRowsService), CreateExpenseTool / UpdateExpenseTool / ListExpensesTool, ActionConfirmationService (`delete_expense`), AssistantTurn (create/update expense writes).

**If you change Core, check:**
- ExpenseStatusEnum / ExpenseSourceEnum consumers: ExpenseService::confirm, ExpenseImportService, ExpenseDetailData, FE ExpenseStatusBadge.
- external_hash partial unique index (migration 2026_11_01_000500): ExpenseImportService dedupe, CamtStatementParser key, tests/Feature/Expenses/BankStatementImportTest.php, tests/Unit/Services/CamtStatementParserTest.php.
- ExpenseReceiptUploadData mime/size list ↔ config/expenses.php ↔ FE FileInput (`resources/js/Components/Forms/FileInput.vue`).

**Keywords (SK):** výdavok, expense, účtenka, receipt, bankový výpis, camt.053, XML import, NtryRef, kategória, draft, confirm, potvrdiť, external_hash, AI analýza, Expense ledger.

### pending-actions — two-step confirmation of irreversible actions (Phase 11, 2026-11-01)

**Core:**
- App\Models\PendingAction — UUIDv7, tenant_id FK (restrictOnDelete), user_id FK → users (restrictOnDelete), subject morph (subject_type + uuid subject_id, no FK), action (PendingActionEnum), payload (jsonb), payload_hash, token_hash (char 64, unique, nullable), subject_hash, preview, subject_label, customer, total, email, channel (ActionChannelEnum mcp/assistant/task_run), `task_run_id` FK nullable → task_runs (restrictOnDelete, indexed; set on `task_run` rows by McpToolAdapter::linkPreparedActionsToRun from the run's turn), status (PendingActionStatusEnum pending/executed/cancelled/expired), expires_at, executed_at, confirmed_via (ConfirmationChannelEnum chat/app). Indexes (user_id, status, expires_at), (subject_type, subject_id).
- App\Services\Ai\ActionConfirmationService (final readonly):
  - `prepare(User, tenantId, PendingActionEnum, Model $subject, payload, ActionChannelEnum)` → PendingActionPreparedData. Checks subject class + tenant, `Gate::forUser($user)->authorize($action->ability(), $subject)` inside ToolContext, preconditions (cancellable / editable / signable), describes the action, stores payload_hash + subject_hash. Token (random 40 chars, sha256 stored) only for channel `mcp`.
  - `resolveToken(User, token)` — sha256 lookup; must belong to the user, be pending and unexpired.
  - `execute(User, PendingAction, ConfirmationChannelEnum)` — transaction + `lockForUpdate`; assertExecutable (owner, pending, not expired, channel in allowed list, mobile token needs `access mobile app`); subject re-resolved (Task via findForSystem, tenant-checked); subject fingerprint mismatch or deleted subject → `PendingActionSubjectChangedException`, row cancelled; Gate re-authorised; payload_hash mismatch → 422 `status`; then status Executed + perform().
  - `cancel(User, PendingAction)`, `pendingFor(User, ?tenantIds)`, `expireStale()`.
- App\Enums\PendingActionEnum (9 cases): issue_invoice, send_invoice, send_quote, sign_contract, cancel_invoice, delete_invoice_draft, delete_quote, delete_task, delete_expense. Methods: subjectClass(), ability() (issue / send / sign / cancel / delete).
- Config `config/ai.php`:
  - `confirm_channels` per action: issue_invoice, send_invoice, send_quote = chat + app; sign_contract, cancel_invoice, delete_invoice_draft, delete_quote, delete_task, delete_expense = app only. Missing key → app only.
  - Rows prepared on the assistant or task_run channel are app-only regardless of config (PendingAction::allowedConfirmationChannels returns App for every non-MCP channel). The task_run channel has no token; an AI task run never confirms.
  - TTL by channel (ActionConfirmationService::ttlMinutes): `ai.pending_action_ttl_minutes` = 10 for mcp and assistant rows; `ai.task_run.pending_action_ttl_minutes` = 1440 for task_run rows (the fingerprint still cancels on a changed subject). Not the MCP token lifetime (180 days, auth auth-domain).
- Policy: App\Policies\PendingActionPolicy — viewAny, view, confirm, cancel (owner-scoped).

**Satellites:**
- Exception: App\Exceptions\PendingActionSubjectChangedException.
- DTOs: PendingActionData (out; `confirm_in_app_only` flag), PendingActionPreparedData (MCP only, see Gotchas), ActionResultData (out after confirm).
- Controllers: web PendingActionController (`/pending-actions`, POST confirm/cancel, execute with `ConfirmationChannelEnum::App`); API Api\PendingActionController (`/api/pending-actions`, same).
- MCP: `prepare_action` (PrepareActionTool) returns the preview + one-time token; `confirm_action` (ConfirmActionTool) executes with `ConfirmationChannelEnum::Chat`.
- Command: `cleanup:pending-actions` (ExpirePendingActions, hourly) → `expireStale()`.
- Audit: each prepare / confirm / cancel / error writes a ToolAudit record (`ToolAudit::record`).

**Flow:**
1. Caller (MCP client or assistant) calls `prepare_action` → ActionConfirmationService::prepare → PendingAction (pending, preview with the company name, TTL 10 min; 24 h on the task_run channel). Assistant receives the row as `pending_action`. An AI task run's prepared rows are listed to their owner in thread `pending_actions` with a link to /pending-actions; the task page never confirms them.
2. Confirmation: MCP `confirm_action` with token (chat allowed only for issue/send actions); or app via web/API POST confirm (app channel, all actions).
3. Execution runs inside a transaction and re-checks fingerprint, payload hash, owner, state and permission. Stale subject cancels the row.

**Depends on:** tenancy (ToolContext binds tenant + team), identity (`access mobile app`, per-action Policy abilities), invoices / quotes / contracts / tasks / expenses services (perform()), audit (ToolAudit).

**Depended on by:** PrepareActionTool, ConfirmActionTool, PendingActionController (web + API), AssistantTurn (pending id), ExpirePendingActions, PendingActionData (FE Components/Ai/PendingActionRow.vue, Components/Assistant/AssistantPendingAction.vue (web chat card) and Pages/PendingActions/Index.vue, both via Composables/usePendingActionDecision.ts, Pages/PendingActions/Index.vue), TaskRunFinisher::hasPendingActions (a live action linked to a run of the task blocks auto-close), TaskRunService::start and TaskThreadService::can_start_run (the starter's live actions on the task block a new run), McpToolAdapter (stamps `task_run_id`).

**If you change Core, check:**
- PendingActionEnum::subjectClass() / ability() ↔ Policies (InvoicePolicy, QuotePolicy, ContractPolicy, TaskPolicy, ExpensePolicy) — a missing ability is an authorisation bug at prepare and at execute.
- ActionConfirmationService::perform() match arms ↔ PendingActionEnum cases (LogicException on mismatch).
- `config/ai.php` confirm_channels keys ↔ PendingActionEnum values.
- fingerprint(): attributes + items (+ quote client name) + text document of contracts and quotes (decrypted body, title, frame flags, uploaded file uuid). Adding a column to Invoice/Quote/Contract changes the fingerprint automatically — check that existing pending rows are not expected to survive a deploy.
- tests/Feature/Mcp/ActionConfirmationTest.php (hash, expiry, channel, subject change).

**Keywords (SK):** potvrdenie, confirm_action, prepare_action, dvojkrok, two-step, pending action, token, expirácia, odoslať faktúru, vystaviť faktúru, storno, zmazať.

### push — device tokens and FCM notifications (Phase 11, 2026-11-01)

**Core:**
- App\Models\DeviceToken — UUIDv7, **per person**: user_id FK → users (restrictOnDelete); no tenant_id. mobile_session_id FK nullable → mobile_sessions (cascadeOnDelete: ending the session drops the device row; replaces personal_access_token_id, migration 2026_11_08_000100). token string(255) unique, platform (DevicePlatformEnum android/ios), app_version (32, nullable), last_seen_at. Migration 2026_11_01_000400.
- App\Services\DeviceTokenService — register(User, DeviceRegisterData, ?string $mobileSessionId = null): in a transaction deletes this session's row holding a different FCM token, then upserts on `token`; unregister(User, token), removeAllFor(User), removeTokens(array), hasTokens(User), tokensFor(User). The no-membership cleanup now lives in MobileSessionService::revokeAllIfNoActiveMembership.
- App\Notifications\Channels\FcmChannel — invoked from BaseTenantNotification::via when `pushEnabledFor(user)`. Sends only if the user has tokens AND `TenantPermissionSnapshot::hasMobileAccess($user, notification tenant)`. Builds PushMessage (title, body, route, tenantId, notificationId, badge from NotificationService::badgeFor). Exceptions are swallowed on purpose (a retry after partial multicast failure would re-send).
- App\Contracts\SendsPush → App\Services\Push\FirebasePushSender (AppServiceProvider binding). Multicast; returns unusable tokens (SenderIdMismatch deliberately excluded — wrong project credentials must not wipe tokens). **Skips quietly** with log `push.skipped` when `config('firebase.projects.{default}.credentials')` is empty.
- Logs (2026-11-04): `FirebasePushSender` writes `push.send.rejected` (warning: count + error class and message of each non-token failure, e.g. APNs or auth; no tokens); `FcmChannel` writes `push.token.pruned` (warning: user_id, count) after `removeTokens`. Pruned tokens are otherwise silent.
- App\Services\Push\PushRoute — allowlist of mobile routes: `/tasks|invoices|quotes|expenses/{uuid}` and `/pending-actions`; anything else → `/notifications`.

**Satellites:**
- DTOs: DeviceRegisterData (token ≤ 255, app_version ≤ 32), DeviceUnregisterData, PushMessage, PushRoute (static).
- Controller: Api\DeviceController — POST /api/devices and DELETE /api/devices, inside `/api` (ability:mobile) and `throttle:devices` (10/min per user).
- Config: config/firebase.php (default project env `FIREBASE_PROJECT`, default `app`; credentials env `FIREBASE_CREDENTIALS`).
- Mobile side (not in this repo): FCM token on both platforms via `@capacitor-firebase/messaging` — TODO verify in `cleaning-software-app`.
- Cross-repo (2026-11-04, `cleaning-software-app`, not re-verified here): `TaskReminderField.vue` + `taskReminderRules.ts` send `remind_on` + `remind_slot` + `remind_at: null`; the push tap path does not change.

**Flow:**
- Register: mobile app → POST /api/devices (token, platform, app_version) → DeviceTokenService::register, linked to the caller's current MobileSession (`currentFor`; null for a raw-token test client).
- Send: domain event → NotifyX listener → notification queued (BaseTenantNotification, Tries 3) → channels: TenantDatabaseChannel, optional mail, FcmChannel → FirebasePushSender → invalid tokens removed.
- Cleanup: EmployeeService (deactivate), EmployeeAccessService::revoke and UserService (membership removed) call `MobileSessionService::revokeAllIfNoActiveMembership(user)`. Logout, revoke, refresh kill, password change, reset and prune remove the device row through `mobile_session_id`.

**Depends on:** notifications (BaseTenantNotification, NotificationService::badgeFor), tenancy (TenantPermissionSnapshot::hasMobileAccess), mobile-session (mobile_sessions FK, `currentFor`), config firebase (credentials), kreait/laravel-firebase.

**Depended on by:** FcmChannel, EmployeeService, UserService, Api\DeviceController, MobileSessionService. Task comment and AI run pushes (`task.commented`, `task.ai_run`) reach FcmChannel through TaskNotification → BaseTenantNotification; their `url()` is `route('tasks.show', id)`, so the push route is `/tasks/{uuid}` with no query or fragment (unverified on device: FCM delivery not tested).

**If you change Core, check:**
- PushRoute pattern ↔ mobile router paths (cross-repo; changing a route breaks deep links from pushes).
- DeviceToken per-person rule ↔ Rule 6 exception (Cross-cutting "Global task and per-person exceptions").
- tests/Feature/Push/{DeviceRegistrationTest,DeviceCleanupTest,PushDeliveryTest,FirebasePushSenderTest}.php.

**Keywords (SK):** push, FCM, device token, zariadenie, notifikácia, APNs, Firebase, PushRoute, deep link, badge, skipped.

### assistant — in-app AI assistant over the MCP tool layer (Phase 11, 2026-11-01)

**Core:**
- POST /api/assistant/messages (Api\AssistantController, `throttle:assistant` 30/min per user). Gate `chatIn` on `Tenant` (TenantPolicy::chatIn) with `company_id`: 403 `mobile_forbidden` when the company is not one the token may act in (no mobile access for mobile tokens); 403 `assistant_forbidden` when the user lacks `use assistant` in that company.
- Web chat (2026-11-01): POST /assistant/messages (routes/web.php, name `assistant.messages`, group `auth` + `tenant.required`, `throttle:assistant`) → the same Api\AssistantController::store and the same `chatIn` gate. CSRF via X-XSRF-TOKEN (`utils/http.ts` postJson). FE: Components/Assistant/{AssistantLauncher,AssistantPanel,AssistantMessage,AssistantActionLinks,AssistantPendingAction}.vue, Composables/useAssistantChat.ts (module singleton, in-memory conversation, epoch drops replies after a company change or reset), Composables/usePendingActionDecision.ts (shared with Pages/PendingActions/Index.vue). Shared prop `assistantAvailable` = `AiClientOptions::isConfigured()` (config read only; the bubble stays visible, composer disabled without a key). The chat card's Confirm/Cancel is an in-app confirmation through web POST /pending-actions/{id}/confirm (ConfirmationChannelEnum::App), not the chat channel. AssistantRequestData: company_id, messages (1..20, each ≤ 4000 chars, total ≤ 12000; last one from user).
- App\Services\AssistantService::reply → `ChatsWithTools` contract → App\Services\Ai\LaravelAiAssistant (AppServiceProvider binding). AiUnavailableException → 422 `ai`.
- LaravelAiAssistant: builds App\Ai\Agents\AssistantAgent (`#[MaxSteps(6)]` + `maxSteps()` from `ai.assistant.max_steps`; `#[MaxTokens(2048)]`), toolbox from App\Ai\Tools\AssistantToolbox::make(turn), history normalised (must start with user, roles alternate; client-sent "assistant" messages are not trusted as proof of effects), system instructions with the company list from `TenantPermissionSnapshot::forRequest`.
- App\Services\Ai\AssistantTurn — constructor `(?float $budgetSeconds = null, ActionChannelEnum $channel = Assistant, ?string $pinnedCompanyId = null, ?string $taskRunId = null)`: the AI task run reuses it with channel `task_run`, a pinned company and its run id (`taskRunId` drives `linkPreparedActionsToRun`); `McpToolAdapter` refuses any `company_id` other than the pin, on reads too. The assistant chat itself keeps the pin null. 45 s turn budget (`ai.assistant.turn_seconds`); records write effects (created/updated ids on allowlisted paths) and pending action ids in call order (first returned as `pending_action`; more than one → extra "open pending actions" link to /pending-actions).
- App\Ai\Tools\McpToolAdapter — each tool call checks the turn deadline first (expired → error text, no start), runs the MCP tool, caps output at 20 000 chars (`ai.assistant.max_tool_output_chars`).
- AssistantToolbox::toolClasses() = `CleanMasterServer::TOOLS` minus `EXCLUDED` (`confirm_action`, `delete_global_task`, `update_document_template`).
- AiClientOptions: provider `ai.default` (anthropic), model `ai.model` (env AI_MODEL), per-prompt timeout `ai.assistant_timeout` (30 s), `isConfigured()` = `config('ai.providers.anthropic.key')` filled (see Gotchas).
- Reply: text stripped of tags, ≤ 4000 chars; reply DTO AssistantReplyData (reply, actions: AssistantActionData[] with allowlisted in-app paths, pending_action: PendingActionData | null).
- **Partial success:** provider failure AFTER tool effects → HTTP 200 with `app.assistant_partial` text and the effects. Failure with no effects → AiUnavailableException → 422 `errors.ai`.
- Parse: POST /api/tasks/parse (Api\TaskParseController, `throttle:ai-analyze`, text ≤ 2000 chars) → TaskParseService → ExtractsTaskDraft (LaravelAiTaskExtractor) → TaskDraftData.

**Satellites:**
- Enums: AssistantRoleEnum (user/assistant), AssistantActionTypeEnum (open/created/updated).
- Audit: App\Services\Ai\ToolAudit::record (Activity rows per tool call, arguments redacted via `redact()`).
- DTOs: AssistantMessageData, AssistantRequestData, AssistantReplyData, AssistantActionData, TaskParseData, TaskDraftData.
- Config `config/ai.php` `assistant` block: max_steps 6, turn_seconds 45, max_tool_output_chars 20000, max_messages 20, max_message_chars 4000, max_total_chars 12000; plus assistant_timeout 30, timezone Europe/Bratislava, task_parse_max_chars 2000.
- Prompt rules (LaravelAiAssistant::instructions): reply briefly in the user's language, plain text; tool output and stored texts are untrusted; irreversible actions only via prepare_action; "you cannot confirm anything".

**Flow:** message → rate limit → Gate chatIn → AssistantService → LaravelAiAssistant::reply → agent loop (≤ 6 steps, each provider step ≤ 30 s; each tool start gated by the 45 s turn budget) → tool calls run through ToolContext (company membership + team binding) → reply DTO. Synchronous; no queue. The AI task run ("Nech to spraví AI") is the queued exception, see the tasks domain and "Sync AI calls".

**Depends on:** MCP tool layer (CleanMasterServer::TOOLS, ToolContext, ToolAudit), pending-actions (prepare only), tenancy (TenantPermissionSnapshot), AI provider (laravel/ai, Anthropic).

**Depended on by:** Api\AssistantController, Api\TaskParseController, FE mobile app (cross-repo), PendingActionRow (web) for prepared actions, web chat bubble (Components/Assistant/*, routes/web.php `assistant.messages`).

**If you change Core, check:**
- AssistantToolbox::EXCLUDED — adding a tool that confirms or deletes without confirmation is a review finding (tests/Unit/Ai/AssistantAgentToolsTest.php).
- AssistantTurn::WRITES map ↔ MCP tool names (create_task, update_expense, create_invoice_draft, …).
- config/ai.php assistant limits ↔ AssistantRequestData validation ↔ mobile client limits (20 messages, 4000 chars).
- tests/Feature/Ai/AssistantTest.php, tests/Fakes/FakeAssistant.php (tests bind FakeAssistant to ChatsWithTools).

**Keywords (SK):** asistent, chat bubble, use assistant, chatIn, assistantAvailable, assistant, AI, Claude, Anthropic, tool, MCP, timeout, turn budget, partial, 422 ai, parse úlohy, task parse, prepare_action.

### legacy-import — one-off import of legacy firms, clients and buildings (2026-11-05)

**Core:**
- `Database\Seeders\LegacyImportSeeder` (production path, 2026-11-05; run as `db:seed --class=LegacyImportSeeder`). Decrypts `database/seeders/data/legacy-import.enc` (`Encrypter` aes-256-gcm, key `config('legacy_import.key')` = env `LEGACY_IMPORT_KEY`, base64 of 32 bytes; path `config('legacy_import.path')`), owner = owner of the settings-source firm (first imported firm with a signature, else first; must be active and an active member), runs `LegacyImportService::import` with `config('legacy_import.dry_run')`, prints one count line (no names), throws after commit when a media copy failed. Prints `auto_issue_firm` count (`Firms with auto-issue default`).
- `App\Console\Commands\ImportLegacyData` (`app:import-legacy`; local JSON only, 2026-11-05; options `--owner=<email>`, `--disk=local`, `--path=import/legacy-cleaning-system.json`, `--copy-settings-from=<IČO or tenant UUID>`, `--dry-run`, `--details`). Thin adapter: owner must be an active user; settings source must exist and the owner must be an active member; reads the JSON with `Storage::disk($disk)->get($path)`; sets the auth user to the owner; prints the report. Returns FAILURE on RuntimeException, AuthorizationException or QueryException (no write survives).
- `App\Services\LegacyImport\LegacyImportService` (final readonly) — `import(array $payload, User $owner, ?Tenant $settingsSource, bool $dryRun): LegacyImportReport`. One `DatabaseManager` transaction for all DB writes. Dry-run rolls back and calls `PermissionRegistrar::forgetCachedPermissions()`. Media copy runs after commit, outside the transaction.
- `App\Services\LegacyImport\LegacyRecordMapper` (final readonly, no DB access) — `firmInput`, `supplierProfile`, `clientInput`, `objects`, `clientPairs`, `legacyId`, `text`. Constants `IMPORTED_FIRM_IDS = [2, 3, 23, 25, 100]` (5 active legacy firms; other firms are not imported), `DEFAULT_CITY = 'Bratislava'`.
- `App\Services\LegacyImport\LegacyImportReport` — `add(entity, legacyId, firm, action, name, warnings)`, `rows()`, `count(entity, action, ?firm)`, `firms()`. Entities `firm|client|object|media`; actions `created|existing|merged|skipped|copied|would_copy|failed`.

**Flow (one run):**
1. Shape check: keys `firms`, `clients`, `firm_clients_2026`, `monthly_templates` must be arrays, else RuntimeException before any write.
2. Open transaction. Per imported firm: match by IČO (`replace(ico, ' ', '') = ?`, soft-deleted excluded by default) else `RegistrationService::addTenant($owner, AddTenantData, $settingsSource)`. A matched firm requires `hasActiveMembership` for the owner, else the run fails.
3. Inside `ToolContext::run($owner, $tenant->id, …)`: `InvoiceSettingsService::fillBlanks`; manual objects from `manual_recurring` (no stand merging); postal rule `correctPostalCode` for existing objects (fill blank / correct a value equal to the client's; see Gotchas); clients (`ClientService::create(ClientUpsertData)` or match by IČO, then by name, both `withTrashed`); objects (`ObjectService::create($data, $owner)` or match by name inside the firm, `withTrashed`). An object whose client was soft-deleted after an earlier import is reported `skipped`, not restored.
4. Every record passes `validateAndCreate` (AddTenantData / ClientUpsertData / ObjectUpsertData) inside the tenant context. A ValidationException becomes a RuntimeException naming the legacy record (`firm #n`, `client #n`, `template #n`) and rolls back everything.
5. `--dry-run`: report `would_copy` for media, rollback. Real run: commit, then `copyMissingMedia` per firm inside `ToolContext::run`. A media failure is reported `failed`, the command exits FAILURE, DB stays committed; a re-run finishes the media (idempotent).

**Mapping rules (register wins):**
- Firm and client: name, IČO, street, city and PSČ come from the RPO `register` block when present, else legacy normalised. Firm `country` SK, `copy_settings` false, `is_vat_payer` false, `vat_number` null, `swift_bic` null; IBAN spaces stripped and uppercased.
- Client: one per (firm, legacy client) pair from the union of `monthly_templates` and `firm_clients_2026`. `type` = corporate when IČO filled, else private. Legacy client IBAN dropped (clients have no IBAN column).
- Object: from `monthly_templates` and `manual_recurring` (manual rows never merge). Container stand names (`… kont. stojisko`, case-insensitive) merge into the object with the base name in the same firm and client (`merged` in report); a standalone container stand → `common_areas`; a name with `č.` → `residential_building`; else `office`. Street = base name with `č.` → space, spaces collapsed; city `Bratislava` (`DEFAULT_CITY`), PSČ null; header lines never set PSČ or city (they hold the property manager's address, 2026-11-05). `billing_name` = header1 (else header4), plus a following lowercase header2 continuation; "V zastúpení …" lines ignored. Other `billing_*` stay null, so `CustomerParty` uses the object address.
- Recurring (2026-11-05, `LegacyRecurringInvoiceImporter`, one per monthly template and per manual row): on the object (match `lower(trim(name))`, soft-deleted included); client = object's client; type monthly; `frequency` = payload `frequency` when present, else monthly for templates (a manual row must carry it); `billed_month` previous; `day_of_month` 1; `due_days` 30; `auto_issue` and `auto_send` false. Items = legacy item names, quantity (default 1), legacy price, VAT 0, discount 0. `header_text` = `above` (right-trimmed) + `services` on a second line unless `above` already contains it (case-insensitive). `{mesiac/rok}` kept verbatim. Skip reasons: `object missing`, `object deleted`, `no last_billed_month`, `no items`, `backfill over cap` (more than 3 billed months due).
- Backfill (2026-11-05): due billed months = `last_billed_month + k·interval` while that month's run date (1st of the next month) ≤ today; one draft per billed month via `generateInvoiceFromTemplate($ri, $month)`; then `start_date` = run date of the next billed month.

**Idempotency and safety:**
- Create-only. A matched firm keeps its name, IČO and filled fields (only blanks are filled). Soft-deleted matches count as existing and are never recreated.
- Recurring invoices (2026-11-05): matched by object + name (case-insensitive, trimmed, soft-deleted included). Match → `existing`, no backfill; a second run creates 0 recurring invoices and 0 drafts. A deleted or missing object → `skipped`, never recreated.
- Activity rows of created clients and objects have causer = owner (guard user set by the command) and `tenant_id` = the firm (through `ToolContext`).
- No events, jobs or mail dispatched. Output by default holds counts and warnings with legacy ids only; `--details` adds names (local use only).
- Imported since 2026-11-05: recurring templates with their items (not historical invoices), drafts for the billed months since `last_billed_month` (max 3 months back), the firm's 2026 numbering (raise only) and the number format `{YYYY}{XXXX}` (only from the default). Nothing issued or mailed. Not imported: historical invoices, expenses, contacts, users other than the owner, other legacy firms.

**Depends on:** tenancy (RegistrationService::addTenant, Tenant, TenantMembership, ToolContext::run, PermissionRegistrar), clients (ClientService::create, ClientUpsertData), objects (ObjectService::create, ObjectUpsertData, ObjectTypeEnum::ResidentialBuilding), settings-invoicing (InvoiceSettingsService::fillBlanks + copyMissingMedia, TenantSupplierProfileData), audit (Activitylog causer via guard user), recurring-invoices (RecurringInvoiceService::create, RecurringInvoiceUpsertData, `generateInvoiceFromTemplate($ri, $billedMonth)`), invoices (InvoiceNumberService::continueSeries), settings-invoicing (InvoiceSettingsService::adoptNumberFormat, Tenant/TenantInterface `recurring_default_state`), objects (ObjectService::updatePostalCode).

**Depended on by:** none at runtime (one-off operator command). Operator how-to: CLAUDE.md "Working with this repo".

**If you change Core, check:**
- Legacy JSON keys: LegacyImportService::REQUIRED_KEYS and LegacyRecordMapper field reads (`register`, `ico`, `dic`, `header1`..`header4`, `monthly_templates`, `firm_clients_2026`, `client_ic_dph`, `kont. stojisko` name suffix), 2026-11-05 optional keys `manual_recurring`, `last_invoice_number_2026`, `monthly_templates[*].last_billed_month`, `object_postal_codes` (shapes in Layer contracts "legacy payload ↔ importer").
- Item unit (2026-11-06): every recurring item gets `unit` `mes.` (`LegacyRecurringInvoiceImporter::LEGACY_UNIT`; the payload has no unit, it was null before). UBL maps `mes.` to `MON` (`config/efa.php`).
- Recurring + numbering: `LegacyRecurringInvoiceImporter` (`MAX_BACKFILL_MONTHS` 3, `DUE_DAYS` 30, `LEGACY_NUMBER_FORMAT` `{YYYY}{XXXX}`), `RecurringBilledMonthEnum::Previous` run-date rule, `InvoiceNumberService::continueSeries` (raises only), `InvoiceSettingsService::adoptNumberFormat` (writes only while the format equals `config('invoicing.default_number_format')`), `ObjectService::updatePostalCode` (the rule lives in `LegacyImportService::correctPostalCode`).
- Matching keys: IČO normalisation in LegacyImportService::firm and ImportLegacyData::settingsSource (`replace(ico, ' ', '')`); client name match `lower(trim())`; object name match `lower(trim())`.
- Validation stays in the DTOs (AddTenantData, ClientUpsertData, ObjectUpsertData, TenantSupplierProfileData). Do not repeat DTO rules here.
- Report action names: ImportLegacyData::printReport table columns and `reportMediaFailures`.
- Report keys: `recurring` created|existing|skipped, `draft` created, `postal_code` filled|corrected|invalid, `numbering` raised|kept, `number_format` set|kept, `auto_issue_firm` issued_default (read by the seeder and ImportLegacyData).
- Tests: tests/Feature/Console/ImportLegacyDataCommandTest.php, tests/Feature/Console/ImportLegacyRecurringTest.php, tests/Feature/Invoices/InvoiceNumberContinueSeriesTest.php (invented fixtures, fake local and s3 disks), tests/Unit/Services/LegacyRecordMapperTest.php.

**Keywords (SK):** import, legacy, starý systém, migrácia, register, RPO, bytový dom, kontajnerové stojisko, dry-run, idempotentný, firmy, klienti, objekty, opakované faktúry, doplnenie draftov, číslovanie faktúr, PSČ objektu, šifrovaný import.

## Cross-cutting

### auth layer (3 tiers, RBAC-full profile)
**Route gates:**
- Middleware: auth / auth:sanctum / guest (Laravel built-in).
- Per-route `tenant.required` (RequireActiveTenant middleware) — authenticates AND has bound tenant, else web logout + redirect / API 403. Routes: all app/* except logout (logout reachable to clear session).
- Per-route `permission:*` (PermissionMiddleware) — used ONLY for /docs* non-model route (config/scribe.php, routes/web.php:37).

**Per-action:**
- #[Authorize($ability, $modelOrParam)] attribute on every controller method (no route-level gates except permission: middleware). Ability = PermissionEnum::*->value (e.g., 'create clients', 'view audit logs'). Model = route-bound model or null (gate on PermissionEnum only, no model).
- Policies: AuthServiceProvider::$policies maps model → Policy. Every policy method (viewAny, view, create, update, delete) calls $user->can(PermissionEnum::*->value) [+ per-record tenant_id check where applicable]. Policies never use $user->hasRole (CLAUDE.md mandate: capabilities only, no role checks in UI/BE).

**Shared Inertia can map:**
- HandleInertiaRequests::share builds can: `collect(PermissionEnum::cases())->mapWithKeys(fn ($p) => [$p->sharedKey() => $user->can($p->value)])` (derived from the 56-case enum, not hand-maintained list).
- FE: useAuthorization().allows(PermissionEnum) → reads page.props.can[permissionKey(value)] === true (permissionKey mirrors Str::camel).
- <Can permission="create clients"> guard with optional #fallback slot.
- **Documented exception to the single permission axis:** `users.is_platform_admin` (boolean column, not in #[Fillable], never shared as a prop) gates global document templates (DocumentTemplatePolicy) and `POST /tenants` (TenantPolicy::create). Reason: a firm Admin role cannot gate cross-firm data, and `POST /tenants` would otherwise let any logged-in user create a firm and become its Admin. The shared prop `canCreateTenant` (= `can('create', Tenant::class)`) is the only FE exposure.
- Per-tenant team scope: Spatie Permission teams=true, team_foreign_key=tenant_id. Every role/permission check calls setPermissionsTeamId(current_tenant_id) first (TenantContextMiddleware, services, tests).

### Cross-tenant multi-read pattern (Dashboard only, Phase 9)
Dashboard is the ONLY cross-tenant read surface (all companies user is a member of, filtered by permission in each tenant). Pattern:
- **Permission snapshot (ADR-D3):** DashboardService::visibilityFor(User) loops through user's active memberships, calls `setPermissionsTeamId(tenantId)` + `$user->unsetRelation('roles')->unsetRelation('permissions')` per tenant to capture current permissions in each company (no relation cache carryover). Finally block restores original team_id + cache clear (critical: HandleInertiaRequests shared props evaluators run after, must read correct active-tenant permissions).
- **Query pattern:** DashboardMetricsQuery + DashboardAlertsQuery never touch TenantScope global scope; they receive pre-filtered id lists from visibility snapshots. Queries: `Model::query()->withoutGlobalScope(TenantScope::class)->whereIn('tenant_id', $ids)->...` (never `withoutGlobalScopes()`; keeps SoftDeletes). Result: row-level isolation preserved (no query can see data outside allowed tenant set), multi-tenant aggregation happens at query level (Postgres FILTER syntax for grouped counts).
- **No user param in queries:** Metrics/alerts queries are stateless aggregators receiving company ids only (no User passed). Consequence: system actions (cron, queued jobs) call services without User; user actions pass actor for row filtering. Dashboard service owns authorization; queries own aggregation.

### Client IP and trusted proxies (2026-11-04)
- **Reach:** `get_client_ip()` (app/Support/helpers.php) = `request()->ip()`. Consumers: LogAuthenticationActivity (every auth row `ip`), PasswordChanged, mobile-session revoke audit, and every per-IP limiter (`web-login`, `password-email`, `password-reset`, `mobile-login`, `mobile-token`, `invitation-accept`).
- **Trust set:** bootstrap/app.php `trustProxies(headers: X_FORWARDED_FOR | X_FORWARDED_PROTO)`; proxies from env `TRUSTED_PROXIES` via config/trustedproxy.php (comma CIDRs or `PRIVATE_SUBNETS`, trimmed, empties dropped). Host, Port, Prefix, AWS_ELB and Forwarded headers are NOT trusted.
- **Fail-closed:** unset or blank `TRUSTED_PROXIES` = `[]` = trust nobody. This overrides the Laravel Cloud `*` fallback (framework TrustProxies trusts `*` with the full header set when `laravel_cloud()` is true and `proxies` is null; verified in the app container vendor, framework v13.39.0). Under `*` Symfony returned the leftmost, client-controlled `X-Forwarded-For`, so per-IP limits and audit IPs were spoofable.
- **Not special-cased:** `TRUSTED_PROXIES=*` is passed through and Symfony rejects it as an invalid CIDR, so the app fails closed. Never set it; `.env.example` says so.
- **Logging:** AppServiceProvider::boot `RequestHandled` listener writes `proxy.headers` (remote_addr, x_forwarded_for/proto/host, x_real_ip, cf_connecting_ip, true_client_ip, forwarded, resolved_ip, scheme) only when `TRUSTED_PROXIES_LOG=true` (default false). Diagnostic only; turn it off after rollout.
- **Host is not pinned:** no `trustHosts` and no `URL::forceRootUrl` in the repo. The reset-link host follows the request Host header; the fix removed X-Forwarded-Host trust, not Host trust. TODO verify on Cloud whether the edge overwrites Host.
- Rollout and Cloud steps: CLAUDE.md "Laravel Cloud" bullet.

### Activitylog reach
Batch writes (2026-11-03): `ActivityBatch::run` wraps `ClientService::create|update|delete`, `ObjectService::update` and the revert writes; every activity of one action shares `batch_uuid`. Other writers leave it NULL. Revert columns `reverted_at` + `revert_batch_uuid` are written only by `ActivityRevertService`. The revert writes are ordinary LogsActivity rows, so a revert is itself revertable (redo). Subject read is tenant-guarded (see audit-logs).
User, Role (logOnlyDirty, dontLogEmptyChanges), LogAuthenticationActivity (login, logout, login_failed, password_reset, password_reset_link_sent, login_lockout, password_changed; auto-discovered). Morph columns uuid. Phase 3+: enabled on business models (Client, Object, Quote, Invoice, RecurringInvoice, DocumentTemplate, Document, Contract, ScheduledJob, TenantMembership, WorkBreakdown, InvoicePayment — all SoftDeletes domain models except WorkBreakdownTask which cascades).

### MediaLibrary
Collections (reconciled 2026-11-05; Tenant `logo` collection removed 2026-11-05, existing rows left in place and unread): TemporaryUpload::default (public disk, 24h purge), Document::file + Document::pdf (private `documents.disk`, singleFile; quote uploads, employee ID scans / files, signed-contract PDF snapshot), Tenant::signature (private, multi-row so issued invoices keep their snapshot), Invoice::efa_xml (private, written by EInvoiceService::submit). Clients, objects, DocumentTemplate have no stored files; Invoice and unsigned Contract/Quote PDFs are generated per request and returned as bytes. Employee files do NOT go through /uploads staging (staged files sit on the public disk up to 24h): multipart straight to EmployeeDocumentController. **Phase 5:** Quote document-kind quotes upload PDF/image via temporary upload contract (FE posts to /uploads, gets uuid, form sends document_uuid, DTO validates OwnedTemporaryMedia + TemporaryMediaConstraints, service moves via moveToModel + re-validates ownership/tenant; Media::move honours collection useDisk so file physically moves from public temp to private DOCUMENTS_DISK). A future stored-document feature should reuse `Document` (new `DocumentTypeEnum` case + policy) rather than adding HasMedia to another owner; non-sensitive uploads use the staging + moveToModel contract. **Task attachments (2026-11-01): deviation from the advice above.** `Task::attachments` (private `config('tasks.attachments.disk')`, multi-row, not singleFile) uses `Task implements HasMedia`, not `Document`: Document is BelongsToTenant with NOT NULL `tenant_id` and needs two rows per file, so it cannot hold personal-task files. Uploads are direct multipart to the task controllers (no staging); the Task owner is the one HasMedia owner whose media rows may have NULL `tenant_id` (DB CHECK).

### AllowedFilter + DataTable query contract
App\Utils\AllowedFilter extends Spatie AllowedFilter: search([...cols]), contains, dynamic (→ SymbolOperatorFilter, operators != <= >= ~ < > = between:), relationExact(name, relation, column), callbackClean, callbackWithOperator. Validators uuid()/date()/isoDateTime()/integer()/numeric()/boolean() log-and-skip. ILIKE on pgsql. FE counterpart Composables/useSpatieTableQuery.ts + Components/DataTable/* (filter[x]=<opPrefix><value> URL contract). Operator prefixes (filterOperators.ts ↔ SymbolOperators::parse, keep in sync): ~: contains, !=:, <:, <=:, >:, >=:, between: (from,to), none = '='. Operator sets by FilterType: string/text ~ = !=; number = != < <= > >= between; boolean = (tri-state); date/datetime = < <= > >= between; enum/select/autocomplete = != (+multiple → comma). Sub-components TableFilters, TableFilter, TableSearch (400 ms debounce → filter[search]), TablePagination (emits page, perPage; 10/25/50/100).
2026-11-06 (tasks): `TaskService::filtered()` is the one filter builder for the task list and the board (board drops `filter[status]`, `sort`, `page`, `per_page` before building); `callbackClean('assignee' | 'due')` and a custom `AllowedSort::custom('priority')` (CASE rank). DataTable `TableColumn.filter` puts a header funnel (TableHeaderFilter, AnchoredPopover) on the column; the page's own toolbar is then off (`enable-filters=false`). Filter state for tasks lives in the URL only (no store).

### Precognition + Forms
HandlePrecognitiveRequests around every POST|PUT mutation (web + api). AppServiceProvider::register (:25-27) binds PrecognitiveDataValidatorResolver (honours Precognition-Validate-Only), short-circuits DTO resolution during precognitive requests. FE: useForm(method, url, data) (Inertia 3 method-bound, Precognition built-in) inside FormProvider → form.submit() → BE store|update(DTO) → 422 → form.errors Record<field,string> | 204 + redirect with flash → toast.

DTOs injected as controller params, #[TypeScript] on all → resources/js/types/generated.d.ts (App.Data.*, App.Enums.*) via TypeScriptTransformerServiceProvider (app/Data + app/Enums, GlobalNamespaceWriter). DataValidatorResolver singleton swapped — custom resolvers must extend PrecognitiveDataValidatorResolver.

### Navigation (BE-driven discovery, persistent + Link-based)
App\Navigation\NavItem attribute (repeatable, method-level: label, route, icon, permission, policyModel, group, order) discovered by NavigationRegistry via router reflection; visibility Gate::forUser()->allows(permission, policyModel) or $user->can (:79-89); groups NavigationRegistry::GROUPS (settings). Shared as navigation: NavigationItemData[], rendered Layouts/AppLayout.vue:215-275. **FE persistent layout:** `createInertiaApp({ layout: (name) => (name.startsWith('Auth/') || name.startsWith('Invitations/') ? undefined : AppLayout) })` in app.ts wraps every page in AppLayout except Auth/* and Invitations/* (sidebars, bell, switcher persist, no remount-on-navigate). Pages no longer import/wrap AppLayout. Internal nav links use Inertia `<Link :href>` (from `@inertiajs/vue3`, renders `<a>` but intercepts clicks → Inertia visit, no full reload). Mobile drawer closes on `router.on('navigate')` to prevent drawer-left-open-after-click UX. Logout button moved from sidebar user card to footer above language switcher (`router.post('/logout')` unchanged). **Icon map:** hardcoded ICONS (HomeIcon, UsersIcon, ShieldCheckIcon, ClipboardDocumentListIcon, PhotoIcon, EnvelopeIcon, UserCircleIcon, Cog6ToothIcon, BellIcon, BellAlertIcon, IdentificationIcon, CalendarDaysIcon; unknown → HomeIcon) — every new NavItem icon must be added. translateLabel strips app. prefix then t(). Active = page.url.startsWith(href). Settings group children (order 10/15/20/25/30/40/50/60): Profile/Users/InvoiceSettings/NotificationSettings/DocumentTemplates/Roles/AuditLogs/Media. **Keep full-page reload ONLY for:** `/language/{locale}` (LocaleMiddleware via Get), `*.pdf` links (target="_blank"), media download_url, `mailto:`, `tel:`, absolute http, form 409 errors. On port: add NavItems for clients, objects, quotes, contracts, invoices, recurring invoices + ensure all internal links converted to `<Link>`.

### Dashboard UX gotchas (Phase 9)

**Carbon::diffInDays signed arithmetic:** DashboardAlertsQuery uses `$dueDate->diffInDays(today())` (signed int). Overdue invoices: due_date < today → positive int (days late). Expiring/unassigned: due_date >= today → zero or negative int (clamped >= 0 for display, "0 days" = today, "-1 days" → "1 day left"). Sort predicate: overdue_invoice uses `-days` (descending by days late, oldest first); expiring uses `+days` (ascending, soonest first). Logic: diffInDays(today()) returns future dates as negative; alerts sort corrects sign per type urgency (more urgent = earlier in list).

**MorphTo::constrain receives Builder, not Collection.** ContractExpiring query includes `->with(['contractable' => morphWith([TenantMembership::class => ['user:id,name,email']])])` (Eloquent eager-load syntax for morphs). Inside DashboardAlertData::fromContract, `$contract->contractable->label()` works (model instance loaded). For soft-deleted CleaningObject (maybe employee left, object deleted), label() returns '' (acceptable fallback). Never assume morphTo relations exist; always provide defaults or skip the relation in the alert.

**Dashboard metric tiles are interactive buttons, not static divs.** MetricStat.vue is `<button type="button">` not `<div>`, because tile click may POST a switch first (cross-company navigation). `<button :disabled="switchingTo !== null">` gates while switch in flight. Children (label, value, caption) are not interactive themselves (no nested buttons). AlertRow.vue same pattern: `<button>` wraps all content, single click handler. ADR-F1 consequence: no middle-click/open-in-new-tab on tiles (trade-off for unified switch+navigate UX).

**Alerts sorted by type rank, then urgency (sign-dependent).** DashboardAlertsQuery sorts: `[$type->rank() ASC, urgency ASC]` where urgency = `[-days for overdue, +days for others, 0 for supplier]`. Result: overdue invoices (rank 1) come first, sorted most-days-late first; then supplier (rank 2) static; then contracts/quotes/unassigned (ranks 3–5) each sorted soonest-first. Capped alerts: e.g., 3 overdue of 50 total (15/type), rest hidden → AlertsPanel section shows "3 items +47 more" (UI feedback that alerts are truncated, not end of list).

### Queue (Laravel queue, database driver + queue compose service) — Phase 4

**Backend:**
- `QUEUE_CONNECTION=database` (dev + test); composed `queue` service in production.
- Queue table `jobs` pre-migrated.
- Notifications: `InvoiceIssued` implements `ShouldQueue`, `#[Tries(3)]` `#[Backoff([10,30,60])]` `#[Timeout(120)]`, `afterCommit()` in ctor (mail + PDF).
- Jobs: `GenerateRecurringInvoiceJob` (ShouldQueue, ShouldBeUnique, retries 3).
- Commands: `MarkOverdueInvoices` / `GenerateRecurringInvoices` scheduled daily via `routes/console.php`.
- Dev: `queue:work` runs in dedicated `queue` compose service (mirrors prod supervisor).
- Tests: `QUEUE_CONNECTION=sync` in phpunit.xml (jobs execute immediately).
- **AI task run (2026-11-01):** `RunTaskWithAi` on queue `ai` (`AI_TASK_RUN_QUEUE`, default `ai`, read in `config/ai.php` `task_run.queue`, set at dispatch). `Tries(1)`, `Timeout(85)`, `FailOnTimeout`. `config/queue.php` `retry_after` unchanged (`DB_QUEUE_RETRY_AFTER`, default 90).
- **Open risk, pre-existing, not fixed:** `InvoiceIssued`, `GenerateRecurringInvoiceJob` and `GenerateScheduledJobsJob` declare `#[Timeout(120)]`, above `retry_after` 90 and above the 90 s managed-queue limit on Laravel Cloud. Not changed by this pass.

**DevOps:**
- Compose: `queue` service (same image as `app`, php artisan queue:work). AI task run: `queue-ai` service, `php artisan queue:work --queue=${AI_TASK_RUN_QUEUE:-ai} --sleep=3 --tries=1 --timeout=85 --max-time=3600` (2026-11-01).
- Supervisor (prod): `[program:scheduler]` runs `php artisan schedule:work` (daily crons). `[program:queue-worker-ai]` (docker/supervisor/supervisord.conf, 2026-11-01): same queue, `--tries=1 --timeout=85`, numprocs 1. `AI_TASK_RUN_QUEUE` must be a real environment variable where supervisor runs, not only in Laravel's `.env` (supervisor's `sh -c` reads the process environment).
- Laravel Cloud: the queue named by `AI_TASK_RUN_QUEUE` must be the one the managed worker listens to. UNVERIFIED on Cloud; no deployment has been run.
- `.env.example`: `MAIL_MAILER=smtp`, `MAIL_HOST=mailpit`, `MAIL_PORT=1025` (dev Mailpit service).

### Cleaner actor scoping (final, Phase 7)

**Per-actor row visibility for Interná upratovačka role (no "all" permissions).**
- `ScheduledJob::scopeVisibleTo(Builder, User|TenantMembership $actor): Builder` — filters to jobs assigned to actor's current membership (WHERE assigned_membership_id = actor->current_membership_id). Returns empty for other roles' queries (they never call scopeVisibleTo; all-permission holders query without actor param).
- `ScheduledJob::isVisibleTo(User|TenantMembership $actor): bool` — predicate for single-row checks (policy gate on view/update/delete). Complements scopeVisibleTo (scope for list, predicate for instance).
- `CleaningObject::scopeVisibleTo(Builder, User|TenantMembership $actor): Builder` — filters to objects reachable via ANY assigned job, regardless of job status/date (D3 override: Vedúca assigns cleaner → cleaner can view object). Query: `WHERE id IN (SELECT DISTINCT cleaning_object_id FROM cleaning_jobs WHERE assigned_membership_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL)`. No date/status filter.
- `CleaningObject::isVisibleTo(User|TenantMembership $actor): bool` — predicate version (exists check via above subquery).
- `User::activeMembershipId(): ?string` — returns current active TenantMembership id in bound tenant context (for actor param in services).
- Service signatures: `JobService::paginate|create|update|assign|cancel(…, ?User $actor = null)` — actor param optional (null = system/admin context), services call `$model->isVisibleTo($actor)` for authorization (422 scope check before permission check is anti-pattern; permission check gates action, scope limits rows).
- `ObjectService::optionsVisibleTo(User $actor): array` — returns ObjectOptionData[] filtered via scopeVisibleTo (used by EmployeeService::create form, job create form).
- **Scope application discipline:** cron/queue/listener/console paths NEVER apply scopeVisibleTo (run unbound per D6 discipline; no actor context). Controllers apply via service calls (service receives $this->user() as actor). Tests: actingAsCleanerUser() helper binds membership context.
- **Zákazník (Customer) actor:** Phase 2 role template reserves; phase 8+ portal expected (zero implementation phase 7; objects/jobs/invoices all hidden via permission absence, no scopeVisibleTo needed).

### TypeScript code generation (php artisan typescript:transform) — Phase 2+

**All DTOs + Enums carry `#[TypeScript]` attribute.** Run after every schema change (columns) or DTO/Enum change to regenerate `resources/js/types/generated.d.ts`. FE imports App.Data.* and App.Enums.* from there (never hand-edit). Laravel TypeScript Transformer 3 (config/typescript-transformer.php): scans app/Data + app/Enums, GlobalNamespaceWriter outputs to resources/js/types/generated.d.ts.

### MergeValidationRules + Precognition — Phase 2+

**Validation ruleset merging.** `#[MergeValidationRules]` on DTOs that extend a parent DTO (e.g., `UpdateUserData` extends base) — merges parent rules via Spatie Data trait. Precognition (HandlePrecognitiveRequests middleware) wraps every POST|PUT mutation; FE useForm auto-detects Precognition support and debounces field validation requests (blurred field → POST with Validate-Only header → 422 on that field only). Cross-field rules (e.g., DTO closure on client_id existence) execute on full payload only (deferred until submit unless Precognition-Validate-Only happens to include that field).

### Localisation (BE + FE sync) — Phase 2 uk added

**Core:**
- App\Enums\SupportedLanguage (#[TypeScript], backed string) — {sk='sk', en='en', uk='uk'}. Methods: `getDefault(): self` (sk), `getDisplayName(): string`, `getForLanguageSwitcher(): array{value, label, flag}` (flag emoji 🇸🇰 / 🇬🇧 / 🇺🇦), `isSupported(string): bool`, `getCodes(): array`.
- LocaleMiddleware (web, appended after TenantContextMiddleware) — resolves user.locale → session → cookie → Accept-Language → sk default. Sets app()->setLocale($locale). GET /language/{locale} (unauthenticated, no DTO) sets cookie + session.
- Translations = JSON resources/lang/{sk,en,uk}/app.json (+ sk/validation.json, en/validation.json, uk/validation.json) loaded by AppServiceProvider::loadJsonTranslations as app.<key>. Flat snake_case keys for vue-i18n (not dotted). Phase 1 = 11 skeleton keys + auth branding (app_name, auth_hero_*, auth_welcome_back, footer_copy, auth_show_password, auth_hide_password). Phase 2 adds tenancy + employee keys (tenant_*, invitation_*, permission_*, permission_group_*, tenant_color_*, invitation_status_*, membership_active, system_role, close + FE-only keys).
- Validation attribute names (2026-11-01): `{sk,en,uk}/validation.json` carries `attributes.*` for every validated field in `app/Data` (incl. nested `items.*.*`, `contacts.*.*`, `mapping.*`, `messages.*.*`, `employment.*`); en/validation.json exists and must stay complete because Laravel skips its own English file once the group has any line. Generic `string`/`numeric`/`integer` messages are phrased for an empty field since `''` becomes null. Layer contract: new validated DTO field -> attribute in all three files, enforced by `tests/Feature/Language/ValidationAttributesTest.php`. Limit: parent key with wildcard children falls back to the raw name if the parent itself errors (`items` has a custom message).
- uk: ported from main branch language files where semantically identical keys exist; remaining skeleton-only keys (DataTable filters, editor, media) translated to Ukrainian; flagged for native review.

**FE Integration:**
- vue-i18n ^11 (legacy:false), messages bundled at build from resources/lang/{locale}/app.json, locale from Inertia `locale` prop.
- app.ts: import ukApp from '../lang/uk/app.json' + messages.uk.
- AppLayout language dropdown data-driven from shared languages (now 3 items: sk/en/uk) + lang attribute on links.
- router.on('navigate') switches locale via app.i18n.locale = new_locale (vue-i18n reactivity).
- FE locale change calls GET /language/{locale} (full reload for consistency).

### Event → Listener wiring (auto-discovery, no explicit registration) — Phase 8

**Backend:**
- Laravel 9+ auto-discovers listeners: `@handle($event)` methods are auto-wired (bootstrap/app.php has `withEvents(discover: true)` by default).
- Phase 8 listeners (Notify*) are auto-discovered only — NEVER register explicitly with `Event::listen()` (causes double-fire).
- Pre-existing bug (phase 4–7): AppServiceProvider had explicit `Event::listen(ContractSigned::class, GenerateWorkBreakdownFromSignedContract::class)` + `Event::listen(NotificationSent::class, StampInvoiceSentAt::class)` — both listeners were auto-discovered, causing double-fire. Phase 8 removed explicit registrations.
- Verify with `php artisan event:list --event=<EventName>` — each listener should appear ONCE as `@handle` entry, not duplicated.
- Design: sync listeners (Notify*, GenerateWorkBreakdownFromSignedContract) run immediately on event dispatch; queued work (Notification send jobs, GenerateScheduledJobsJob) dispatches on queue via ShouldQueue.

### AI tool layer (MCP server + assistant toolbox) — Phase 11

- **Quote billing periods and included text (2026-11-03).** `DescribesDocumentItems` quote item schema gains `billing_period` (enum, default `one_off`; `itemRows` passes it through for quotes); the quote schema gains `included_text` (create without it uses `TenantInterface::default_quote_included_text`; update without it keeps the stored value). `GetQuoteTool`, `CreateQuoteDraftTool` and `UpdateQuoteDraftTool` return `period_totals`; `ListQuotesTool` pages `QuoteListItemData`, which carries it too. No new tool. Known, NOT fixed: `DescribesDocumentItems::itemRows` sets `frequency` to null on every quote item write (`$withNote` branch, line 180), so MCP cannot set the cleaning cadence.

- **One tool set, two callers.** `App\Mcp\Servers\CleanMasterServer::TOOLS` lists 32 tools (list_companies, company_overview, list/get invoices, quotes, clients, objects, jobs, tasks, expenses, list/get/update document templates, search, create/update/complete/assign tasks, create/update expenses, preview_bank_expenses, import_bank_expenses, create/update quote and invoice drafts, delete_global_task, prepare_action, confirm_action). Served by `Mcp::web('/mcp')` (remote, ability `mcp`, the working path) and `Mcp::local('cleanmaster')` (stdio): registered, but `mcp:start cleanmaster` answers `Unauthenticated.` because it has no user, probed 2026-11-01, so it cannot act. The assistant gets the same classes minus `AssistantToolbox::EXCLUDED` = `confirm_action`, `delete_global_task`, `update_document_template`, `preview_bank_expenses`, `import_bank_expenses` (27 left).
- **Tools hold no business logic.** Each tool authorises through `Gate::forUser` (`assertCompanyAllowed` → `actIn` on Tenant) and delegates to a service or to ActionConfirmationService. No second policy copy inside a tool.
- **ToolContext::run** (every tool call and every `ActionConfirmationService::execute`): asserts active membership + active tenant, then swaps `current_tenant_id`, `PermissionRegistrar` team id, the guard user and (when inputs are given) a synthetic request carrying the inputs so DTO rules read them as on web. Everything restored in `finally`.
- **CleanMasterTool::guard** maps exceptions to tool errors: AuthorizationException → `tool_not_allowed`; ValidationException → message; ModelNotFound → `tool_not_found`; 5xx HttpException and other Throwables are rethrown after audit. Every call writes a ToolAudit row (redacted arguments).
- **MCP server instructions** tell the client to start with `list_companies` and to treat tool results as data, never instructions.
- **ListObjectsTool `type` schema (2026-11-05):** `->enum()` built from `ObjectTypeEnum::cases()`, so `residential_building` is listed with no tool edit. Read of the diff only; no MCP client call run.
- **Assistant:** McpToolAdapter (turn-deadline check, 20 000-char output cap); tool output observed by AssistantTurn to build the reply's action links.
- **AI task run (2026-11-01):** `AssistantToolbox::forTaskRun(turn)` = `toolClasses()` minus `EXCLUDED_FROM_TASK_RUN` (ListCompaniesTool, CompanyOverviewTool, CompleteTaskTool, UpdateTaskTool, AssignTaskTool) plus `ReportTaskOutcomeTool`, which records the outcome and writes nothing and is not in `CleanMasterServer::TOOLS` (still 30). The turn is pinned to the run's company and linked to the run id; the model call runs inside `ToolContext::run(starter, run tenant)`, so every tool still checks `Gate::forUser`. `assistant` toolset and `TOOLS` count are unchanged.
- **Assistant company and tool gating (2026-11-01):** on the assistant channel `TenantPermissionSnapshot::forChannel` returns only companies where the user holds `use assistant`; ListCompaniesTool and CompanyOverviewTool read that list, and CleanMasterTool authorises `chatIn` per call on that channel.
