MCP and the task_run channel are not gated by `use assistant` (they use the companies the token may act in, `actIn`).
- **Rate limits:** `throttle:mcp` keyed by MCP token id (`ai.mcp_throttle`, 120/min); `throttle:assistant` 30/min; `throttle:ai-analyze` 20/min.
- **Document template tools:** `list_document_templates`, `get_document_template`, `update_document_template`. An update of a global template always goes to the firm's own copy (`DocumentTemplateService::companyTarget` + `updateForCompany`, one transaction, so a refused edit leaves no copy); global rows are edited only in the web UI by a platform admin. `update_document_template` is MCP-only (`AssistantToolbox::EXCLUDED`).
- **Prompt-injection guard:** `App\Services\Ai\Guard\{PromptInjectionDetector,PromptGuard,InjectionVerdict,PromptInjectionCorpus}`, command `ai:guard-verify`, config `ai.guard.*`. `AssistantTurn::restrict()` / `refusalForWrite()` enforce the write block and caps; `McpToolAdapter` screens arguments and output and wraps output in `<tool_output_{nonce} tool="x" trust="untrusted">`; `LaravelAiAssistant` screens the request and filters the reply. `CleanMasterTool` screens MCP arguments/output (hooks `scannedArguments(array): array` narrows what `refusedByGuard` scans, `auditedArguments(array): array` narrows what `record` logs; both identity by default, overridden only by the bank expense tools), labels flagged output with `_security_notice` and sets the per-user cache restriction `ai-guard:mcp-restricted:{userId}`; every non-read-only tool is refused during it. Because `prepare_action` is refused too and a prepared token lives 10 minutes, no token outlives a 15-minute restriction. `PromptGuard::scanValues` scores each value separately; `scanOutput` decodes JSON first. `ExpenseService`, `ExpenseImportService`, `TaskParseService` drop flagged model input/output. Tools are classified read-only by the `IsReadOnly` attribute; a missing attribute counts as a write.

### Two-step confirmation — Phase 11

- Irreversible actions (issue/send invoice, send quote, sign contract, cancel invoice, delete invoice draft / quote / task / expense) are never executed by a tool directly. `prepare_action` → PendingAction (preview with company name, payload_hash, subject_hash fingerprint, TTL by channel: `ai.pending_action_ttl_minutes` = 10 for mcp and assistant, `ai.task_run.pending_action_ttl_minutes` = 1440 for task_run).
- **Fingerprint:** sha256 over key-sorted attributes (updated_at excluded), line items (invoice / quote) and the quote's resolved client name. A changed or deleted subject at confirm time cancels the row (`PendingActionSubjectChangedException`).
- **Payload hash** over the key-sorted payload (jsonb does not keep key order); mismatch → 422 on `status`.
- **Token:** MCP prepares return a one-time random token (only sha256 stored; `PendingActionPreparedData` carries it and is deliberately not a Spatie Data). Assistant-channel and task_run rows carry no token and are app-only.
- **Channels:** `ai.confirm_channels` per action. Chat (MCP `confirm_action`) is allowed only for issue_invoice, send_invoice, send_quote; every other action is app-only (web or API `POST …/confirm`).
- **Single-use:** status Executed on success; Expired by `cleanup:pending-actions` hourly; Cancelled by user or subject change.
- **Assistant:** may prepare, never confirm. Its reply carries the pending action with `confirm_in_app_only` when the channel list is app-only.

### Mobile API surface (/api and /mcp) — Phase 11

- **Quote billing periods (2026-11-03, additive only).** `QuoteListItemData` and `QuoteDetailData` keep `total` and gain `period_totals` (`[{billing_period, subtotal, vat_amount, total}]`, strings, present groups only, enum order); `QuoteItemData` gains `billing_period` (default `one_off`). No route and no permission change. The mobile app still shows the combined `total` until it reads `period_totals` (follow-up in `cleaning-software-app`, then `pnpm api-types:export`).

- **`/api` group order (routes/api.php):** `auth:sanctum` → `locale.api` → `user.active` → `ability:mobile` → `tenant.context` → `tenant.required`. Login (`POST /api/auth/login`) sits outside the ability group (HandlePrecognitiveRequests + locale.api); logout is `auth:sanctum` + `locale.api` only.
- **Routes in the ability group:** /me, /dashboard, /notifications (bell, list, read-all, read), /invoices (+stats, +pdf, +issue, +send-draft, +send with throttle `invoice-send`; 2026-11-06), /quotes (+pdf), /devices (throttle `devices` 10/min), /assistant/messages (throttle `assistant`, gate `chatIn`: mobile access + `use assistant` in the company), /tasks (+parse under `ai-analyze`; +attachments: GET/POST `/tasks/{task}/attachments`, GET/DELETE `/{attachment}`, POST throttled `task-attachments`; +comments and AI runs: GET `/tasks/{task}/thread`, POST `/tasks/{task}/comments` (201), DELETE `/tasks/{task}/comments/{comment}`, GET `/tasks/{task}/ai-run-status`, POST `/tasks/{task}/ai-runs` (202)), /expenses (analyze under `ai-analyze`; store, show, update, delete, confirm, receipt; no import routes, statement import is web only), /pending-actions (list, confirm, cancel), /profile, /users.
- **`/mcp` group order (routes/ai.php):** `auth:sanctum` → `locale.api` → `user.active` → `ability:mcp` → `throttle:mcp`. Tokens with only `mcp` ability; a `mobile` token is rejected.
- **Tenant context before route binding:** `bootstrap/app.php` prepends TenantContextMiddleware before `SubstituteBindings`, so scoped route bindings (for example `/tasks/{task}` via TaskScope, `/pending-actions/{pendingAction}`) resolve under the caller's tenant. Security fix `6f08aa9`; `main` lacks it.
- **Tenant selection:** `X-Tenant-Id` header (must be a membership the caller holds, else 403 `tenant_forbidden`) → session → first membership (filtered by mobile access for mobile tokens). Mobile token in a company without `access mobile app` → 403 `mobile_forbidden`.
- **Errors:** validation → 422 with field keys (`errors.ai` when the provider is unavailable, `errors.status` for pending-action state, `errors.token` for bad MCP tokens); no active membership → 403 `no_active_tenant`.
- Scribe docs cover the `Assistant` group and ResponseFromSpatieData shapes.

### Global task and per-person exceptions (rule 6) — Phase 11

Rule 6 (`BelongsToTenant` + `tenant_id NOT NULL` on every domain model) has these deliberate exceptions on this branch:
- **Global tasks.** `tasks.tenant_id` is nullable; a NULL row is owned by `created_by_user_id` and is visible only to its creator. Isolation comes from `TaskScope` (fail-closed) plus the Policy as second layer. `Task::unscopedForSystem()` is the single bypass; queue and console loaders go through `TaskService::findForSystem`.
- **Activity for global rows.** `Activity::$keepNullTenant` stops the creating hook from filling `tenant_id` while a tenant is bound, so audit rows of global tasks stay tenant-less (visible to their causer through `visibleInTenant`).
- **Notifications.** `notifications.tenant_id` is nullable (migration 2026_11_01_000300) for system messages without a company. NotificationPolicy and bell scopes filter by recipient.
- **DeviceToken** has no tenant_id: a device belongs to a person; tenant visibility is checked at send time (`hasMobileAccess`).
- **MobileSession** (2026-11-01) has no tenant_id: per person, like DeviceToken. No Policy and no LogsActivity (hourly rotation would flood the audit log); revocations are logged explicitly as `mobile_session_revoked`.
- **Task attachments.** `media.tenant_id` may be NULL only for Task owners (DB CHECK `media_tenant_required_unless_task`, migration 2026_11_02_000200). The creating hook copies the owner's tenant_id, so a personal task's file is not filed under the bound company. The Media viewer excludes Task media.
- **Task comments (2026-11-01).** `task_comments.tenant_id` is nullable and set by TaskCommentService from the task only (NULL on a personal task). `TaskCommentScope` is fail-closed (bound tenant's rows, or NULL-tenant rows of the authenticated user). `user_id` is NOT NULL and is the accountable person (the writer, or the run starter for AI text), so every comment on a personal task carries the creator's id. Activity never logs `body`; personal-task comments log with `keepNullTenant`.
- **Task runs (2026-11-01).** `task_runs.tenant_id` is NOT NULL (BelongsToTenant): the company the run acts in, chosen at start on a personal task and equal to the task's tenant on a firm task. The job loads the Task through `TaskService::findForSystem` (the existing single bypass, no new caller); the relation `Task::aiRuns()` drops TenantScope only on the relation. TaskRun has no LogsActivity.

### Sync AI calls (deviation) — Phase 11

- Plan intent was to keep long work off the request. The shipped AI calls (assistant reply, expense analyze, bank statement categorisation, task parse) run **synchronously inside the HTTP request**: no `ShouldQueue`, no `dispatch` in `app/Ai`, `app/Services/Ai`, `app/Services/Expenses` (grep-verified).
- Bounds: `ai.assistant_timeout` 30 s per provider step; `ai.assistant.turn_seconds` 45 s gates the start of each tool call; `#[MaxSteps(6)]` caps steps (worst case ~6 × 30 s when a model keeps requesting tools); `ai.timeout` 30 s on the other AI calls; assistant provider failures without effects surface as 422 `errors.ai`.
- Transport: `docker/nginx/default.conf` `fastcgi_read_timeout 300` (≥ 90 s required). A proxy in front of nginx must not cut earlier — TODO verify at deploy.
- **Queued exception (2026-11-01): the AI task run.** "Nech to spraví AI" runs as `App\Jobs\RunTaskWithAi` on queue `ai` (`config('ai.task_run.queue')`, env `AI_TASK_RUN_QUEUE`), `Tries(1)`, `Timeout(85)`, `FailOnTimeout`, dispatched afterCommit. Reason: production on Laravel Cloud sleeps, and Flex managed queues allow at most 90 s per job with at-least-once delivery, so a request-bound run would be cut off mid-task. Worker budgets: turn 45 s, step 25 s, job 85 s, below the 90 s limit (Laravel Cloud behaviour unverified). A run that runs out of time ends `could_not_finish` and leaves the task open; the person continues it with "Pokračovať". Queue `retry_after` stays `DB_QUEUE_RETRY_AFTER` 90. No retry by design (`Tries(1)`: a redelivered run would repeat writes); a lost run ends `failed` through `failed()` or the stale sweep.
- The rest of the AI calls stay synchronous, as above. Grep check: `ShouldQueue` appears in app/Jobs/RunTaskWithAi.php and the notifications/jobs listed in the Queue section, not in app/Ai or app/Services/Ai.

### Mobile contract export (pnpm api-types:export) — Phase 11

- `scripts/export-api-types.mjs` copies `resources/js/types/generated.d.ts` (produced by `php artisan typescript:transform` from `#[TypeScript]` DTOs and enums) to `../cleaning-software-app/src/types/api.generated.d.ts` by default, or to a path passed as argument; prepends a "do not edit" header. Fails when the mobile checkout is missing and no path is given.
- The mobile repo is `git@github.com:VladimirGostik/cleaning-software-app.git` (not in this worktree).
- DTOs that must not reach the mobile types (for example PendingActionPreparedData with the token) are plain classes, not `#[TypeScript]`.

## Layer contracts

### HTTP ↔ Service
Controller receives DTO + route-bound model, calls final readonly service, returns Inertia::render / redirect-with-flash (success|error|info|status) / response()->json(Data). Exceptions: RoleService signals via InvalidArgumentException; profile password change and auth attempts inline in controllers; list queries live in controllers (User, Role, AuditLog) except MediaService::index. On port: all business model services follow same pattern (create/update/delete in service, list in controller).

### Service ↔ Model
Services own DB::transaction; models hold casts, LogsActivity options, scopes. No observers/events besides framework auth. On port: add business model observers + event dispatchers (QuoteSent, InvoiceIssued, InvoiceOverdue, ContractExpired, etc.).

### Service ↔ Job / Queue
Queued (QUEUE_CONNECTION sync in tests): notifications on BaseTenantNotification and InvoiceIssued (`Timeout(120)`, see Queue), GenerateRecurringInvoiceJob, GenerateScheduledJobsJob, StoreSignedContractPdf, and RunTaskWithAi (queue `ai`, `Tries(1)`, `Timeout(85)`, dispatched afterCommit from TaskRunService::start, the only queued AI call). Schedule: PurgeTemporaryUploadsCommand daily; `cleanup:task-runs` daily 12:00 Europe/Bratislava (ExpireTaskRuns).

### BE ↔ FE shared props (HandleInertiaRequests::share) — Phase 2 tenancy collapse + PermissionEnum

**Typed & delivered:**
- `assistantAvailable: boolean` (2026-11-01) = `AiClientOptions::isConfigured()`; `can.useAssistant` comes from the generic `can` map (PermissionEnum `use assistant`, sharedKey `useAssistant`).
- flash: {success?, error?, info?, status?, string?}
- auth: {user: {id, name, email, locale: SupportedLanguage} | null}
- tenant: {active: TenantListItemData | null, available: TenantListItemData[]} (Phase 2 new; tenant.available ordered by name, all with is_active=true)
- tenantColors: {value: TenantColorEnum, label: string}[] (Phase 2 new; 8 colour options for AddTenantModal)
- canCreateTenant: boolean (2026-10-31; platform admin only; TenantSwitcher hides "add company" otherwise)
- can: Record<string, boolean> (derived from PermissionEnum::cases, keyed by sharedKey = Str::camel, 56 keys; e.g., 'viewEmployees', 'createClients')
- languages: {value: SupportedLanguage, label: string, flag?: string}[] (now sk/en/uk; phase 2 adds uk)
- locale: string (current user.locale, set by LocaleMiddleware)
- navigation: NavigationItemData[] (BE-driven discovery via #[NavItem] attributes, resolved per user's can() checks)

**TS location:**
- Single `SharedProps` interface in resources/js/types/index.d.ts (D1: collapsed from two declare blocks). `declare module '@inertiajs/core' { interface InertiaConfig { sharedPageProps: SharedProps } }`.
- Import-free global augmentation: removed types/inertia.d.ts (D1).
- FE reads via usePageProps() computed (D3: never destructure at setup, always read .value in computed/template).

**Phase 2 changes:**
- auth.user.locale added (user.locale consumer on login via LocaleMiddleware).
- Dropped SharedCan (hand-enumerated keys rotted; now derived from PermissionEnum::sharedKey, unidirectional per-permission).
- Dropped canResetPassword (skeleton's page-prop-only Auth/Login, not shared — defer to phase 3+ if needed).
- Removed duplicate types/inertia.d.ts (D1).

### Per-page props (Inertia render)
**Phase 1 (skeleton):**
- Auth/Login {canResetPassword: bool}
- Auth/ForgotPassword {status?: string}
- Auth/ResetPassword {email: string, token: string}
- Dashboard {}
- Profile/Show {}

**Phase 2 (tenancy + identity):**
- Invitations/Accept {invitation: InvitationAcceptPageData {state: InvitationAcceptStateEnum, token, email?, tenant_name?, role_name?, invited_email?}}
- Users/Index {users: Paginator<UserListItemData>, filters?: Record<string, unknown>, filterOptions: {roles: RoleListItemData[]}}
- Users/Form {user?: UserListItemData, roles: RoleListItemData[]}
- Roles/Index {roles: Paginator<RoleDetailData>}
- Roles/Form {role?: RoleDetailData, permissions: PermissionGroupData[]}
- AuditLogs/Index {activities: Paginator<ActivityLogListItemData>, filters?: Record<string, unknown>, filterOptions?}
- AuditLogs/Show {activity: ActivityLogDetailData}
- Media/Index {media: Paginator<MediaListItemData>, filters?: Record<string, unknown>, filterOptions?}
- Media/Show {media: MediaDetailData}

**2026-11-01 (task attachments):**
- Tasks/Show {task: TaskDetailData, attachments: TaskAttachmentListData, context: TaskFormContextData} (attachment names and URLs are in the `attachments` prop only, not in `task`).

**2026-10-31 (documents, contracts, employees):**
- DocumentTemplates/Index {templates: Paginator<DocumentTemplateListItemData>, filters, canCreateGlobal} · Create {type, employment_type, isGlobal, tokens: DocumentTokenCatalogData, letterhead, footer} · Edit {template: DocumentTemplateEditData} (read-only when `!can.update`).
- Contracts/Create {context: ContractFormContextData, prefill: ContractCreatePrefillData} · Contracts/Edit (+encryptHistory) · Contracts/Show (+encryptHistory; ContractDetailData with `body` null = no text access).
- Clients/Show, Objects/Show, Employees/Show add {contracts: ContractListItemData[]|null (null without ViewContracts; newest 50), canCreateContract: bool}.
- Employees/Show adds {personal: EmployeePersonalData|null (masked national id), documents: EmployeeDocumentData[]|null, documentConstraints: EmployeeDocumentConstraintsData|null}; `employee.can.manage_documents`. 2026-11-01: Employees/Create + Edit + Show props carry `employee_type`, `has_access`, `invitation_pending`, `company_ico`, `company_name`, `contact_email`, nullable `user_id`/`email` in list/detail DTOs; form context `can_manage_access`; Index filters `employee_type`, `has_access` (list items drop `employment_type`). Employees/Create + Edit add {personal: EmployeePersonalFormData|null (null hides the section; Edit sends the full national_id and logs `national_id_viewed`)}; Edit calls `Inertia::encryptHistory()`; logout calls `Inertia::clearHistory()`.
- Quotes/Show adds contractTemplates: DocumentTemplateOptionData[]; QuoteDetailData.text_document; Quotes/Create|Edit context adds templates/tokens/letterhead/footer.
- 2026-11-03 (quote billing periods; firm logo part removed 2026-11-05): Quotes/Show gets `period_totals` (in QuoteDetailData) and `included_text`; Quotes/Create|Edit context gets `default_included_text`; RecurringInvoices/Create gets `prefill` (RecurringInvoicePrefillData | null, built from the `quote_id` + `billing_period` query); Settings/Invoicing gets `logo` and `logo_constraints` (2026-11-03; removed 2026-11-05, no logo prop on the page).
- Settings/Invoicing: InvoiceSettingsData adds `representative_name`.
- Enum/type mirrors: ContractableTypeEnum gains `client`; new DocumentTypeEnum, DocumentTokenKindEnum, DocumentTokenGroupEnum, DocumentTemplateScopeEnum, DocumentTemplateStateEnum (all #[TypeScript], run `typescript:transform`).

### clients — Cleaning company customers (corporate + private) with contacts and address

**Core:**
- `App\Models\Client` — UUIDv7; traits BelongsToTenant, HasFactory, HasUuids, LogsActivity, SoftDeletes. Columns: tenant_id FK, `type` (ClientTypeEnum: corporate/private), name (255), nullable ico (32) + dic (32) + vat_number (32), bool is_vat_payer, address (street/city/postal_code/country), nullable note, **nullable peppol_participant_id (64, e-invoice recipient id; validated by `App\Rules\PeppolParticipantId` against config/efa.php participant_schemes; scheme 0245 needs a 10-digit value via the same `PeppolParticipantId::isValid` the UBL endpoint uses; copied to invoices.customer_peppol_participant_id at invoice creation)**. Relations: `contacts(): MorphMany<Contact>`, `primaryContact(): MorphOne<Contact>` (where is_primary=true), `objects(): HasMany<CleaningObject>`. Activity log on changes (logOnly same list as columns; logOnlyDirty). Partial unique index `(tenant_id, ico)` WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND ico IS NOT NULL.
- `App\Models\Contact` — table `contacts` (2026-11-04, replaces `client_contacts` + `object_contacts`); UUIDv7; `final`; traits BelongsToTenant, HasFactory, HasUuids, LogsActivity, SoftDeletes. Columns: tenant_id FK (restrictOnDelete), `contactable_type` (string, morph alias `client` or `cleaning_object`) + `contactable_id` (uuid), name (255), nullable position (255) + email (255) + phone (64), bool is_primary (default false), timestamps, soft_deletes. Index (contactable_type, contactable_id, is_primary); no DB FK to the parent (parents are only soft-deleted, cascade in services). Relation `contactable(): MorphTo`. Factory: ContactFactory (`->for($parent, 'contactable')`). Migration `2026_11_11_000100_create_contacts_table` (forward data move, ids kept; `activity_log.subject_type` rewritten to `App\Models\Contact`; `down()` restores the old tables).
- `App\Services\ClientService` (final readonly, ctor DatabaseManager) — `paginate(Request): LengthAwarePaginator` (QueryBuilder::for(Client) + AllowedFilter search/name/type/city/ico/created_at + defaultSort name + eager load contacts + primaryContact + counts → through ClientListItemData); `create(ClientUpsertData): Client` (transaction: Client::create + contact sync); `update(Client, ClientUpsertData): Client`; `delete(Client): void` — **D1 override user decision:** transaction: soft-delete all client's objects via `$client->objects->each->delete()` (SoftDeletes global scope excludes trashed from queries; per-model logging), soft-delete contacts, soft-delete client. CleaningObject keeps both SoftDeletes (for this cascade) and is_active (for direct deactivation via ObjectService::deactivate). **D8 note:** IČO uniqueness enforced by DTO rule (Precognition-visible) + DB partial index (race constraint accepted for internal tool). `contact sync via `ContactCollectionSynchronizer::sync($client->contacts(), $data->contacts->items(), 'app.client_contact_invalid')` — existing-id validation, soft-delete outgoing, primary auto-promotion (none → first becomes primary).
- `App\Enums\ClientTypeEnum` (#[TypeScript], backed string) — `Corporate='corporate'`, `Private='private'`. Method: `label(): string` → `__('app.client_type_'.$this->value)`.

**Satellites (BE):**
- DTOs: `ContactData` (id, name, ?position, ?email, ?phone, is_primary; shared with objects); `ClientUpsertData` (type enum required, name required, ?ico requiredIf type=corporate, ?dic, ?vat_number, is_vat_payer, address fields, ?note, ?peppol_participant_id, contacts: DataCollection<ContactData>). Rules: ico unique per tenant (D8), at most one primary contact. `ClientListItemData` (id, type, name, ?ico, ?city, contacts_count, objects_count, ?primary_contact_email, ?primary_contact_phone, created_at). `ClientDetailData` (extends ListItemData + dic, vat_number, street, postal_code, country, note, peppol_participant_id, contacts: DataCollection<ContactData>). `ClientOptionData` (id, name) — for pickers.
- Enums: ClientTypeEnum (see Core above).
- Factories: ClientFactory (with `private()`, `withContacts(int $count)` states).
- Seeders: ClientSeeder (demo tenant 12345678 with 6 corporate + 3 private clients, 2–3 contacts each).
- Policies: `ClientPolicy` — viewAny/view → ViewClients; create → CreateClients; update → EditClients; delete → DeleteClients. Tenant isolation via TenantScope (no extra record check).
- Controllers: `ClientController` (ctor ClientService $clients), actions: `#[Authorize('viewAny')] index(Request)` → Clients/Index `[clients, filters]`; `#[Authorize('view')] show(Client)` → Clients/Show `[client: ClientDetailData, objects: ObjectListItemData[], contracts: ContractListItemData[]|null, canCreateContract: bool]` (load contacts, eager-load client on objects; contracts = ContractService::listFor: contracts on the client and on its objects); `#[Authorize('create')] store(ClientUpsertData)` → show + flash (2026-10-31: redirects to clients.show so a contract can be started from the new client); `#[Authorize('update')] update(ClientUpsertData, Client)` → show + flash; `#[Authorize('delete')] destroy(Client)` → index + flash. Routes: GET /clients, GET /clients/{client}, POST /clients (Precognition), PUT /clients/{client} (Precognition), DELETE /clients/{client} (all `whereUuid`).
- Navigation: #[NavItem] on index → `clients` nav (icon UserGroupIcon, permission ViewClients, order 30).

**FE Satellites:**
- Composables: none (ClientForm owns its state via useForm).
- Components: `ClientTypeBadge` (badge + icon per type), `ClientDetailCard` (info card: type/VAT/address), `ClientContactsList` (primary badge, contact rows with links), `ClientObjectsTable` (unpaginated table), `ClientForm` (form with RadioGroup type, fields, ContactsListField for array editor), `ClientFormDrawer` (side drawer wrapper, title based on create/edit), `ContactsListField` (custom field-mode: rows, add/remove, set primary via radio, per-row TextInput/email/phone with Precognition).
- Pages: `Clients/Index` (DataTable: columns name/type/ico/city/primary_contact_email/objects_count; filters search/name/type/city/ico/created_at; sort name/type/city/ico/created_at; empty state "clients_empty"; buttons view/delete; create drawer from #actions). `Clients/Show` (header title/breadcrumbs + edit/add-object/delete buttons; grid: left ClientDetailCard+note+ContactsList, right ClientObjectsTable; edit drawer preloaded; delete confirm cascade copy; add-object preselects client).
- i18n: `client_add`, `client_edit`, `client_delete`, `client_delete_confirm`, `client_delete_cascade_hint`, `client_type`, `client_name`, `client_ico`, `client_dic`, `client_vat_number`, `client_is_vat_payer`, `client_contacts`, `client_contact_add`, `client_contact_remove`, `client_contact_is_primary`, `client_no_contacts`, `client_objects`, `client_no_objects`, `client_no_note`, `client_customer_since`, `clients_empty`, `clients_empty_hint`, `client_object_add` (per phase-3 FE plan step 10).

**Flow:**
- **List:** GET /clients → ClientController@index + AllowedFilter + sort/page → Clients/Index {clients: Paginator<ClientListItemData>, filters: Record<string, unknown>}. Table rows: view → Show page; delete → confirm modal (cascade copy). Create drawer from button → POST /clients (Precognition on blur) → ClientService::create → 302 index + flash success.
- **Show:** GET /clients/{client} (whereUuid) → RMB TenantScope → ClientController@show + eager load contacts + objects → Clients/Show {client: ClientDetailData, objects: ObjectListItemData[]}. Drawer forms: edit PUT /clients/{id} → 302 show + flash; add-object opens Objects form with client preselected.
- **Update:** PUT /clients/{client} (Precognition) → same service, 302 show + flash.
- **Delete:** DELETE /clients/{client} → ClientPolicy::delete gates → ClientService::delete (transaction: soft-delete all objects, soft-delete contacts, soft-delete client; activity logged per model).

**Depends on:** tenancy (tenant_id FK, BelongsToTenant, TenantScope), objects (Client::objects() relation, ClientService::delete soft-deletes children).

**Depended on by:** legacy-import (ClientService::create per imported client; matched by IČO, then name, soft-deleted rows included), audit-logs (revert of client rows: create/restored → `ClientService::delete`, deleted → restore), objects (client_id FK, ObjectUpsertData rule), quotes (client_id FK, withTrashed; QuoteService::convertToContract requires client_id), invoices (client_id FK + customer snapshot incl. peppol id), recurring-invoices (client_id FK), contracts (Client is a direct contract subject since 2026-10-31: `Client::contracts(): MorphMany<Contract>`, morph alias `client`, category service_agreement/nda/gdpr/other; a client contract has no Rozpis prác or jobs; object contracts still resolve `client.*` tokens through DocumentContextBuilder), dashboard (people metrics clients count).

**If you change Core, check:**
- ClientService::create|update|delete run inside `ActivityBatch::run` (one batch_uuid per action; the audit-log revert of a batch depends on it). A new write path must wrap too.
- ClientService::delete cascade: objects soft-delete logic (loop with `->delete()` per-model for activity logging), contact soft-delete, activity logging per model (3 types: Client, Contact, CleaningObject).
- IČO uniqueness: DTO rule builder (Rule::unique 'clients' with tenant_id + whereNull deleted_at), partial DB index, test reuse-after-delete scenario.
- Contact primary: ContactCollectionSynchronizer::sync logic (reset all → promote first if none), validation closure (at most one), test auto-promote.
- ClientTypeEnum.label() consumers: Badge (label t(clientTypeKey(type))), Form (type selector options), tests.
- ClientPolicy used in: ObjectController (clients list for picker), ObjectService (no direct policy call but objects depend on clients).

**Keywords (SK):** klient, podnikateľ, fyzická osoba, kontakt, IČO, DIČ, DPH, adresa, kontaktná osoba, primárny kontakt.

### objects — Cleaning locations per client with access info, contact persons, and deactivation

**Core:**
- `App\Models\CleaningObject` — table `objects`; UUIDv7; traits BelongsToTenant, HasFactory, HasUuids, LogsActivity, **SoftDeletes** (D1 override user decision: hybrid lifecycle — is_active for direct deactivation, deleted_at for client cascade). Columns: tenant_id FK, client_id FK, type (ObjectTypeEnum: office/apartment/house/common_areas), name (255), address (street/city/postal_code/country), nullable access_code (64) + key_box_code (64) + key_count (int) + special_instructions (text) + area_sqm (decimal:2) + floor (int) + gps_lat (10,7) + gps_lng (10,7) (GPS reserved, no UI), bool is_active (direct deactivation), timestamps + soft_deletes. Relations: `client(): BelongsTo` → `$this->belongsTo(Client::class)->withTrashed()` (keeps relation resolvable even if client is soft-deleted), `contacts(): MorphMany<Contact>` (ordered is_primary desc, name asc), `primaryContact(): MorphOne<Contact>` (where is_primary=true, convenience accessor). Scopes: `scopeVisibleTo(Builder, User $actor): Builder` — ViewAllObjects → unfiltered; else `$actor->activeMembershipId()` null → `whereRaw('1 = 0')` (fail-closed), else `whereHas('jobs', assigned_membership_id = membership)` (D3: any assigned job grants reachability, no status/date filter). `isVisibleTo(User): bool` — ViewAllObjects shortcut, else exists-check through the scope. Also `ObjectService::optionsVisibleTo(User $actor): array` (ObjectOptionData[] for pickers; used by ScheduledJobController). Activity log logOnly (no note column logged) + `billing_*` columns (2026-11-01). **Billing identity (2026-11-01):** nullable `billing_name` (255), `billing_ico` (32), `billing_dic` (32), `billing_vat_number` (32), `billing_peppol_participant_id` (64), `billing_street` (255), `billing_city` (255), `billing_postal_code` (16), `billing_email` (255, 2026-11-05, migration `2026_11_12_000100_add_billing_email_to_objects_table`, suggestion + save target only, never the buyer switch); no `billing_country` (buyer country = object `country`). `hasBillingIdentity(): bool` = `filled(billing_name)` (the only switch). Migrations `2026_11_07_000100_add_billing_identity_to_objects_table` (objects). Client = managing company; the house is the buyer on invoices only when it has billing identity.
- Contact persons: polymorphic `App\Models\Contact` (see clients Core), `contactable` = CleaningObject (morph alias `cleaning_object`). Soft-deleted on client cascade (ClientService::delete); untouched on deactivate / reactivate.
- `App\Services\ObjectService` (final readonly, ctor DatabaseManager, ContactCollectionSynchronizer) — `paginate(Request, User $actor): LengthAwarePaginator` (QueryBuilder::for(CleaningObject::visibleTo($actor)) + computed gate $includeContacts = $actor->can('viewContacts', CleaningObject::class), conditionally withCount('contacts')+with('primaryContact') when gate=true + through ObjectListItemData::fromModel($obj, $includeContacts)); `create(ObjectUpsertData $data, User $actor): CleaningObject` (transaction: create object, if gate=$actor->can('viewContacts', ...) then sync contacts via ContactCollectionSynchronizer), `update(CleaningObject $object, ObjectUpsertData $data, User $actor): CleaningObject` (transaction: update object, if gate then sync contacts else ignore $data->contacts), `deactivate(CleaningObject): void` (transaction: set is_active=false, log activity, leave contacts untouched), `reactivate(CleaningObject): void` (transaction: set is_active=true, log activity).
- `App\Enums\ObjectTypeEnum` (#[TypeScript], backed string) — `Office='office'`, `Apartment='apartment'`, `House='house'`, `ResidentialBuilding='residential_building'` (label "Bytový dom", added 2026-11-05; `objects.type` is a plain string column, no migration), `CommonAreas='common_areas'`. Method: `label(): string` → `__('app.object_type_'.$this->value)`.
- `App\Data\Contacts\ContactData` (#[TypeScript]) — the one contact DTO for clients and objects. Replaces `ClientContactData`, `ObjectContactData` and the `ContactPayload` interface (deleted 2026-11-04).
- `ObjectService::updatePostalCode(CleaningObject, string): void` (2026-11-05) — the only writer of an existing object's postal code (renamed from `fillBlankPostalCode`). The rule (fill blank, or correct a value equal to the client's postal code) lives in `LegacyImportService::correctPostalCode`. Callers: `grep -rn "updatePostalCode" app`.
- `App\Services\ContactCollectionSynchronizer` (final readonly) — `sync(MorphMany $relation, array<int, ContactData> $contacts, string $invalidMessageKey): void` (callers pass `$data->contacts->items()`). Idempotent synchronizer: loads existing by id, rejects foreign ids, soft-deletes outgoing, auto-promotes first to primary when none marked, upserts remainder. Shared by ClientService + ObjectService (one rule, two call sites).
- `App\Rules\AtMostOnePrimaryContact` — ValidationRule (message key in ctor), validates array of `is_primary` — rejects count > 1.

**Satellites (BE):**
- Billing DTOs (2026-11-01): `ObjectBillingData` (#[TypeScript], read-only: name? (nullable 2026-11-05), ico?, dic?, vat_number?, peppol_participant_id?, street?, city?, postal_code?, email?; `fromModel(CleaningObject): ?self` null when `!hasBillingIdentity() && blank(billing_email)`). `ObjectUpsertData` gains flat `billing_*` fields (`#[Nullable, Max(...)]` per field; `rules()`: `billing_name` `required_with` the other 7 billing fields (not `billing_email`, 2026-11-05), `billing_peppol_participant_id` `PeppolParticipantId` rule; `messages()` `billing_name.required_with` → `app.object_billing_name_required`; `public const array BILLING_FIELDS` = the 8 keys, used as the write gate; no `attributes()` label for `billing_name`). `ObjectDetailData` gains `billing: ?ObjectBillingData`, `fromModel(CleaningObject, bool $includeContacts, bool $includeBilling)`. `ObjectOptionData` gains `billing_name: ?string`, `fromModel(CleaningObject, bool $includeBilling)`: `ProvidesSubjectOptions::objectOptions` passes `Gate::allows('viewBilling', CleaningObject::class)`; `ObjectService::optionsVisibleTo` passes false (schedule pages are cleaner-reachable). `ObjectListItemData` gets no billing fields.
- DTOs: `ContactData` (shared with clients; id?, name required Max(255), position? Max(255), email? Email Max(255), phone? Max(64), is_primary=false). `ObjectUpsertData` (**changed:** is_active now required, not defaulted; added `DataCollection<ContactData> $contacts` field with `AtMostOnePrimaryContact('app.object_contacts_multiple_primary')` rule; mirrors ClientUpsertData). Rules: client_id Rule::exists scoped to current_tenant_id() + whereNull deleted_at (live clients only). `ObjectListItemData` (id, type, name, ?city, is_active, client_id, ?client_name, ?area_sqm, **?contacts_count** null if gated, **?primary_contact_email** null if gated, **?primary_contact_phone** null if gated, created_at; gate determined by caller's includeContacts param). `ObjectDetailData` (extends ListItemData + street, postal_code, country, access_code, key_box_code, key_count, special_instructions, floor, **?DataCollection $contacts** null if gated, gps columns omitted).
- Enums: ObjectTypeEnum (see Core).
- Factories: CleaningObjectFactory (default client_id via Client::factory(), `inactive()` state, `withContacts(int $count=1)` state via afterCreating hook, creates N, promotes first to primary). ContactFactory (name/position/email/phone, is_primary=false; state `primary()`; parent via `->for($parent, 'contactable')`).
- Seeders: ObjectSeeder (demo tenant, 1–3 per client with withContacts(random_int(1,2)), last client's inactive object gets 1 contact so deactivation losslessness is visible).
- Policies: `ObjectPolicy` — viewAny → ViewObjects; view → ViewObjects && isVisibleTo($user); create → CreateObjects; update → EditObjects && isVisibleTo; delete (gates deactivate) → DeleteObjects && isVisibleTo; **viewContacts(User $user): bool** → `$user->can(PermissionEnum::ViewAllObjects->value)` (field-level gate, no model param, no per-row query). **viewBilling(User $user): bool** (2026-11-01) → `$this->viewContacts($user)` (same breadth gate for billing identity). Tenant isolation + **own-only scoping** (D2) via isVisibleTo. Contacts gated separately via viewContacts class method.
- Controllers: `ObjectController` (ctor ObjectService), actions: `#[Authorize('viewAny')] index(Request, User $user)` → `$includeContacts = $user->can('viewContacts', CleaningObject::class)` + pass to service → Objects/Index `[objects, filters, filterOptions: {clients}]` (clients empty for own-only actors → filter hidden, button disabled); `#[Authorize('view')] show(CleaningObject, User $user)` → load contacts conditionally + ObjectDetailData::fromModel($object, $includeContacts) → Objects/Show `[object, clients: ClientOptionData[], contracts: ContractListItemData[]|null, canCreateContract: bool]` (clients empty if !can('update') → edit hidden); `#[Authorize('create')] store(ObjectUpsertData, User $user)` → pass $user to service → show + flash; `#[Authorize('update')] update(ObjectUpsertData, CleaningObject, User $user)` → pass $user to service → show + flash; `#[Authorize('delete')] deactivate(CleaningObject)` (POST to deactivate endpoint) → show + flash; `#[Authorize('update')] reactivate(CleaningObject)` (POST to reactivate endpoint) → show + flash. Routes: GET /objects, GET /objects/{object}, POST /objects (Precognition), PUT /objects/{object} (Precognition), POST /objects/{object}/deactivate, POST /objects/{object}/reactivate (all `whereUuid`). `private clientOptions(User $actor): array` — ViewAllObjects actors get all clients; own-only get empty (D2).
- Navigation: #[NavItem] on index → `objects` nav (icon BuildingOffice2Icon, permission ViewObjects, order 31).

**Contact visibility gate (field-level, fail-closed per Q1 decision):**
- **Signal on wire:** `contacts: null` means "not permitted to view"; `contacts: []` or `contacts_count: 0` means "permitted, none exist". Gate computed once per request, applied in DTO factory, conditional eager load is optimization only (gated actor query never touches the contacts table).
- **Write side:** ObjectService::create/update only syncs contacts when actor passes viewContacts gate; else $data->contacts ignored and existing rows left intact. Form payload always accepts (no client-side delete workaround; service owns the gate).
- **FE consequence:** Index: `contacts_count === null` is the only gate signal (two email/phone fields also nullable for ordinary reasons, never as gate). Column hidden entirely when gated. Detail: `contacts === null` → no card rendered; `contacts === []` → card shown with empty state.

**FE Satellites:**
- Composables: none new (shared ContactsListField accessed via Components/Contacts).
- Components: `ObjectTypeBadge` (badge + icon per type: office/apartment/house/residential_building/common_areas mapped; exhaustive `Record<App.Enums.ObjectTypeEnum, object>`, so a new case fails vue-tsc until added to different colours/icons), `ObjectStatusBadge` (active green / inactive ghost), `ObjectDetailCard` (type/status badges, ?client link, area/floor, address, created_at), `ObjectAccessCard` (warning-bordered card: access_code/key_box_code/key_count with sensitive warning + caption), **moved to Contacts/:** `ContactsList` (props contacts, title, emptyLabel; renders table of contact rows, primary badge, mailto/tel links), `ContactsListField` (props field; editor with add/remove rows, primary radio, Precognition re-validation on setPrimary), `contact.ts` (ContactRow type), `ObjectForm` (form: client SelectInput required, type/name, address fields, access section, key_count/special_instructions, **contacts section** v-if canSeeContacts computed = allows('view all objects'), is_active toggle on edit), `ObjectFormDrawer`, `objectPayload.ts` helper (ObjectFormData interface incl. contacts: ContactRow[], function to map DetailData → payload incl. string→null conversion + contacts ??: [] to handle gated actors).
- Pages: `Objects/Index` (DataTable: columns name/type/client_name→link/city/**primary_contact_email** (shows email/phone, hidden when gated), area_sqm/is_active; filters search/name/type/client_id/is_active/city/created_at; sort name/type/city/is_active/created_at; info hint when !ViewAllObjects; empty state; buttons view/deactivate when active; create drawer pre-scoped if single client; create hidden when no client list). `Objects/Show` (header + edit/deactivate buttons; inactive banner with reactivate button when is_active=false; grid: left ObjectAccessCard+instructions, right ObjectDetailCard + **ContactsList card** v-if object.contacts!==null; deactivate/reactivate modals; edit drawer).
- i18n: existing object_* keys + new `object_contacts` (sk: "Kontaktné osoby"), `object_no_contacts` (sk: "Žiadne kontaktné osoby."), `object_contact_invalid` (validation: "Kontaktná osoba nepatrí tomuto objektu."), `object_contacts_multiple_primary` (validation: "Iba jedna kontaktná osoba môže byť hlavná."); shared with clients: `contact_add` (sk: "Pridať kontaktnú osobu"), `contact_remove` (sk: "Odstrániť kontakt"), `contact_is_primary` (sk: "Hlavný kontakt"), `name`, `position`, `email`, `phone`, `empty_dash` (sk/en/uk parity, 1100 keys each).

**Flow:**
- **List:** GET /objects (actor) → ObjectController@index + compute gate + visibleTo($actor) + AllowedFilter → Objects/Index {objects[...ListItemData with contacts_count=null when gated], filters, filterOptions: {clients}}. Own-only actors: clients = [], info hint shown, button hidden, primary_contact_email column absent. Table rows: view → Show; deactivate button (active only) → deactivate confirm modal.
- **Show:** GET /objects/{object} → compute gate, conditional load contacts, RMB TenantScope + Policy::view isVisibleTo gate → Objects/Show {object: DetailData(contacts=null when gated), clients: empty if !can('update'), ContactsList when contacts!==null}. Edit drawer (clients list provided, contacts editor shown if gate=true and readonly placeholder if gate=false). Reactivate button (is_active=false) → PUT /objects/{id} with full payload + is_active=true.
- **Create:** POST /objects (Precognition) → ObjectService::create(data, $actor) inside transaction, sync contacts if gate, return object + load relations → 302 index + flash.
- **Update:** PUT /objects/{object} (Precognition) → ObjectService::update(object, data, $actor) inside transaction, sync contacts if gate (else ignore payload), return object + load relations → 302 show + flash.
- **Deactivate:** POST /objects/{object}/deactivate → ObjectPolicy::delete gate + isVisibleTo → ObjectService::deactivate (is_active=false, activity logged, contacts untouched).
- **Reactivate:** POST /objects/{object}/reactivate → ObjectPolicy::update gate + isVisibleTo → ObjectService::reactivate (is_active=true, activity logged, contacts untouched).

**Cascade:**
- **Client delete:** ClientService::delete loads objects.contacts (nested eager load), soft-deletes each contact in transaction, then soft-deletes object.
- **Object deactivate/reactivate:** is_active orthogonal to deleted_at; contacts untouched, lossless.
- **Contact removed from payload:** soft-deleted (not hard-deleted), parity with clients.

**Depends on:** tenancy (tenant_id FK, BelongsToTenant, TenantScope), clients (client_id FK + Rule::exists to live clients, client() withTrashed relation, ClientService::delete soft-deletes objects + object contacts as cascade).

**Depended on by:** legacy-import (ObjectService::create per imported building, client_id resolved from the import's client map; matched by name inside the firm, soft-deleted rows included), audit-logs (revert of object rows: `ObjectService::update` batch; restored CleaningObject → soft delete, deleted → restore), quotes (cleaning_object_id FK, ObjectBelongsToClient rule; convertToContract requires it), invoices + recurring-invoices (cleaning_object_id FK, object snapshot; `CustomerParty::forInvoice` reads `billing_*` when `hasBillingIdentity()` and makes the house the buyer; recurring list display name reads `billing_name`; `InvoiceRecipientService` reads `billing_email` and object contacts as e-mail suggestions, and writes `billing_email` on "save to object", 2026-11-05), contracts (service-agreement contractable via morph alias `cleaning_object`; `CleaningObject::contracts()` MorphMany; `object.*`/`client.*` document tokens; ContractController::formContext lists active objects), schedule (ScheduledJob.object_id FK, visibleTo reachability), ContractController::edit keeps the contract's current object in the picker even if inactive. ObjectPolicy::viewContacts gating pattern mirrors approach in dashboard + notifications phase 8+ (fail-closed field-level gates).

**If you change Core, check:**
- Contact model: SoftDeletes + LogsActivity; index (contactable_type, contactable_id, is_primary); no DB FK to the parent, so the soft cascade in services is the only cascade (no hard-delete path).
- ObjectService signatures: `paginate(Request, User $actor)`, `create(ObjectUpsertData, User $actor)`, `update(..., User $actor)` all require actor param for gate computation.
- ObjectPolicy::viewContacts: method signature (no model param), gate = ViewAllObjects exactly (no isVisibleTo re-check; validation-surface ownership: one owner per rule).
- ObjectDetailData::fromModel / ObjectListItemData::fromModel: both require `bool $includeContacts` param, not defaulted (fail-closed by making omission impossible to forget).
- ContactCollectionSynchronizer::sync callers: ObjectService::create/update (when gate) + ClientService (unchanged in phase-3, now shared). One rule (at most one primary, soft-delete outgoing), two call sites.
- ObjectService::deactivate/reactivate: no contact mutations (orthogonal).
- ObjectService::update runs inside `ActivityBatch::run` (2026-11-03, audit-log revert). A new write path on objects must wrap too, or its rows revert alone.
- ClientService::delete: loads nested objects.contacts before cascade; each contact soft-deleted in transaction.
- ObjectController store/update: new User $actor parameter passed through.
- `billing_email` is in `ObjectUpsertData::BILLING_FIELDS` (write gate), `ActivityRevertService::OBJECT_FIELDS` (revert allowlist) and the `billing_name` required_with exclusion; all three move together.
- ObjectTypeEnum case added (2026-11-05): `resources/js/utils/enums.ts` OBJECT_TYPES, ObjectTypeBadge `Record` (vue-tsc), `lang/{sk,en,uk}/app.json` `object_type_*`, `ListObjectsTool` `type` enum schema (from `cases()`), `generated.d.ts` (`typescript:transform`), mobile types (`pnpm api-types:export`). DTO validation, the QueryBuilder filter and ObjectForm select pick it up from `cases()`.

**Billing identity (2026-11-01) — satellite rules:**
- Write gate: `ObjectService::attributes` drops `ObjectUpsertData::BILLING_FIELDS` when the actor lacks `viewBilling`; existing values stay (same shape as the contacts gate).
- Read gate: `ObjectController::show` passes `$actor->can('viewBilling', CleaningObject::class)` into `ObjectDetailData::fromModel`; gated actors get `billing: null`. Cleaners with an assigned job never see billing on Show or in schedule options.
- FE: `ObjectForm.vue` billing section `v-if="canSeeBilling"` (= `allows('view all objects')`); `ObjectBillingCard.vue` mounted in `Pages/Objects/Show.vue` with `v-if="object.billing"`; `objectPayload.ts` maps `object.billing` (null → '').
- Factory: `CleaningObjectFactory::withBillingIdentity(array $overrides = [])`.
- **Billing e-mail (2026-11-05):** `billing_email` is a suggestion for invoice sending, never the buyer rule: `hasBillingIdentity()` stays `filled(billing_name)`, so an object with only an e-mail still has the client as buyer. Read by `InvoiceRecipientService::emailOptions` only under `viewBilling`; written by `InvoiceRecipientService::send` only on "save to object" (`saveTarget` requires `update` on the object and `viewBilling`).
- FE: `ObjectForm.vue` field `billing_email` (type email, hint `object_billing_email_hint`) in the billing section; `ObjectBillingCard.vue` shows the name line only when set plus the e-mail row; `objectPayload.ts` maps `billing_email` (`billing.name ?? ''`).
- FE deep link: `Pages/Objects/Show.vue` reads `?edit=billing` from `usePage().url`; opens the drawer (only when `canEdit`) with `focusSection='billing'`, so `billing_name` gets `autofocus`. Entry point: `RecurringCustomerCard` buyer link `/objects/{id}?edit=billing` (needs `edit objects` + `view all objects`).

**Keywords (SK):** objekt, lokalita, kancelária, byt, dom, spoločné priestory, prístupový kód, kľúč, deaktivácia, citlivé údaje, kontakty, správca, domovník, recepcia, kontakt na site, fail-closed gate, ViewAllObjects permission, fakturačné údaje, fakturovaný subjekt, v zastúpení, billing identity.

### quotes — Itemized + document quotes, clientless snapshot, status lifecycle, convertToInvoice, billing periods + per-group totals + recurring prefill (Phase 5, 2026-09-06; billing periods 2026-11-03)

**Core:**
- `App\Models\Quote` — UUIDv7; traits BelongsToTenant, HasFactory, HasPdfFilename, HasUuids, LogsActivity (logOnly status/kind/number/total/client_id/cleaning_object_id), SoftDeletes. Columns: tenant_id FK, nullable client_id FK → clients (withTrashed), nullable cleaning_object_id FK → objects (withTrashed), status (QuoteStatusEnum: draft/sent/accepted/rejected/expired), kind (QuoteKindEnum: itemized/document, immutable after create), nullable number (50 chars, partial unique index tenant_id + number WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND number IS NOT NULL), nullable subject (255), issue_date (date), valid_until (date), nullable sent_at/accepted_at/rejected_at (timestamp), bool is_vat_payer, nullable vat_rate (decimal:5,2), currency (CurrencyEnum, default EUR), money columns (subtotal/vat_amount/total default 0, all decimal:12,2), json vat_breakdown nullable, **clientless snapshot** (customer_name nullable, customer_email/customer_street/customer_city/customer_postal_code nullable, mutually exclusive with client_id via DTO `prohibits`), nullable note (text), timestamps, soft_deletes. Indexes (tenant_id,status), (tenant_id,kind), (tenant_id,client_id), (tenant_id,valid_until). Relations: `client()->withTrashed()`, `cleaningObject()->withTrashed()`, `items(): HasMany<QuoteItem>` ordered position, `invoices(): HasMany<Invoice>` (quote_id FK, re-conversion allowed → many invoices per quote). Methods: `isEditable(): bool` (Draft), `canBeConverted(): bool` (Accepted), `isDocument(): bool` (kind === document). No longer HasMedia (2026-10-31): `document(): MorphOne<Document>` holds the optional text (type quote) and the uploaded file (Document media collection `file`, disk config('documents.disk')); `contracts(): HasMany`. See domains documents + quotes (text documents). 2026-11-03: `period_totals` jsonb nullable (`[{billing_period, subtotal, vat_amount, total}]` as strings, enum order, present groups only, written only by `QuoteService::billingTotals`; `subtotal`/`vat_amount`/`total`/`vat_breakdown` stay the all-lines sum and are not printed); `included_text` text nullable (max 2000; `includedLines(): list<string>` splits, trims, drops blanks). Migration backfill: itemized quotes with items get one `one_off` group.
- `App\Models\QuoteItem` — UUIDv7; traits BelongsToTenant, HasFactory, HasUuids. Columns: tenant_id FK, quote_id FK (restrictOnDelete), description (255), frequency nullable (50, cleaning cadence for phase 7 work-breakdown generation, not printed on the quote PDF), billing_period string default `one_off` (QuoteBillingPeriodEnum, 2026-11-03; the price basis, independent of frequency; migration 2026_11_09_000100), quantity/unit_price (decimal:10,2), unit nullable (50), discount_percent/vat_rate (decimal:5,2), computed line_base/line_vat/line_total (decimal:12,2), position (smallInt). Index quote_id. Relation: `quote(): BelongsTo`.
- `App\Enums\QuoteStatusEnum` (#[TypeScript], backed string) — Draft|Sent|Accepted|Rejected|Expired; `canTransitionTo(self): bool` matrix; `label(): string`.
- `App\Enums\QuoteKindEnum` (#[TypeScript], backed string) — Itemized|Document; `label(): string`.
- `App\Enums\QuoteBillingPeriodEnum` (2026-11-03, #[TypeScript], backed string) — OneOff|Monthly|Quarterly|Yearly; `label()`, `adverb()`, `unitNoun()`, `shortSuffix()` (lang `app.quote_period_*`; null for one_off), `recurringFrequency()` (Monthly→RecurringFrequencyEnum::Monthly, Quarterly→Quarterly, Yearly→Annually, OneOff→null), `isRecurring()`. `App\Services\Documents\QuoteDisplay` holds the display rules shared by the quote PDF and the document blocks (`totalsLine`, `groupTotal`, `groups`, quantity / unit price / line cells). `App\Data\Quotes\QuotePeriodTotalsData::collectFrom(?array)` maps the stored JSON.
- `App\Services\QuoteService` (final readonly, ctor InvoiceService, TemporaryUploadService, DocumentItemService, DatabaseManager, QuoteTextService, DocumentTemplateRenderer, DocumentHtmlSanitizer, DocumentTemplateCatalogue) — `paginate(Request): LengthAwarePaginator<QuoteListItemData>` (QueryBuilder AllowedFilter search across number/subject/customer_name/client.name + dynamic filters status/kind/client_id/issue_date/valid_until/total/created_at, default -created_at, eager load client/object/media), `create(QuoteUpsertData, ?User, string $sessionId): Quote` (transaction: itemized → DocumentItemService::replacePriced + billingTotals; document + document_uuid → moveToModel), `update(Quote, QuoteUpsertData, ?User, string $sessionId): Quote` (guard isEditable), `send(Quote): Quote` (guard status transition + document kind; **422 on `status` while the text document has unresolved tokens**; transaction: resolves + sanitizes + freezes the text body, status Sent, dispatch QuoteSent), `accept/reject(Quote): Quote` (status guard + document guard; stamp accepted_at/rejected_at), `attachClient(Quote, string $clientId, ?string $objectId): Quote` (guard client_id IS NULL; nulls 5 snapshot columns; ObjectBelongsToClient rule), `duplicate(Quote): Quote` (transaction: `replicate(except: number, sent_at, accepted_at, rejected_at, deleted_at)->withoutRelations()->fill` Draft, issue_date=today, valid_until=today+config('quotes.default_validity_days'); items replicated 1:1; document kind → copy the file media onto the new Document; itemized → copyTextDocument: Draft source copies the body, otherwise reseeds from the template if it still exists, else no text document), `delete(Quote): void` (guard isEditable; soft-deletes the Quote and its Document), `convertToInvoice(Quote): Invoice` (guard document kind + Accepted status; build InvoiceUpsertData from one-off items only (422 `status` `quote_has_no_one_off_items` when none), snapshot clientless data if client_id IS NULL; transaction: create invoice via InvoiceService, update invoice.quote_id). 2026-11-03: `recurringInvoicePrefill(Quote, QuoteBillingPeriodEnum): RecurringInvoicePrefillData` (same guards as convertToInvoice plus one_off or an empty group → 422 `status` `quote_has_no_items_for_period`; writes nothing). `billingTotals(Quote)` writes `period_totals` per present group via `DocumentItemService::totals`; `duplicate` copies `billing_period`, `period_totals`, `included_text`; private `itemDescription()` = name + frequency label suffix + ` — note`, capped at 255 chars (254 + "…") and shared by convertToInvoice and the prefill.
- `App\Services\DocumentTotalsCalculator` (final readonly, shared with invoices) — `line(qty, unitPrice, discountPercent, vatRate, isVatPayer): array{line_base, line_vat, line_total}` (base = round2(qty×price×(1−d/100)); vat = round2(base×rate/100, 0 if !payer); total = round2(base+vat)); `totals(lines iterable, isVatPayer, roundingMode=None): array{subtotal, vat_amount, total, rounding_amount, vat_breakdown|null}` (byte-for-byte invoice math, breakdown sorted rate desc, null when non-payer or empty).
- Contracts: `RendersQuotePdf { public function render(Quote $quote): string; }`.
- `App\Services\Pdf\QuotePdfService implements RendersQuotePdf` — loadMissing(['items','client','cleaningObject']); supplier from Tenant::withoutGlobalScopes; render `pdf.quotes.default` via chrome driver (2026-11-05: no logo variable, the template inlines `kegos-logo.svg`; 2026-11-06: logo 45.63 mm wide, `overflow: visible`; footer `pdf.quotes.footer`, render wrapped in `DocumentLocale`).
- `App\Console\Commands\ExpireQuotes` (app:expire-quotes, routes/console.php daily) — withoutGlobalScope TenantScope, itemized only, Draft|Sent past valid_until → Expired + QuoteExpired event; foreach config('quotes.expiring_notice_days') → QuoteExpiring event with daysLeft.

**Satellites (BE):**
- Enums: QuoteStatusEnum, QuoteKindEnum (see Core).
- DTOs: `QuoteItemData` (id?, description required, frequency? max 50, billing_period (enum, default one_off; 2026-11-03), quantity≥0, unit?, unit_price≥0, discount_percent 0–100, vat_rate≥0, line_*?); `QuoteUpsertData` (#[MergeValidationRules], client_id XOR customer_name/email/street/city/postal_code via prohibits+required_without; cleaning_object_id; number unique-per-tenant; issue_date; valid_until≥issue_date; kind enum immutable on edit via Rule::in; subject?; note?; items required+min:1 for itemized, prohibited for document; document_uuid required for document kind on create, nullable on edit; currency enum; rules: ObjectBelongsToClient, OwnedTemporaryMedia, TemporaryMediaConstraints); `QuoteAttachClientData` (client_id required exists, object_id? exists + ObjectBelongsToClient); `QuoteListItemData` (id, number?, status, kind, subject?, customer_name = client?->name ?? $snapshot, client_id?, object_name?, currency, total formatted, period_totals (`QuotePeriodTotalsData[]`, 2026-11-03), issue_date, valid_until, has_document); `QuoteDetailData` (all scalars + client/object names + itemized QuoteItemData[] + VatBreakdownLineData[] + ?MediaFileData document + QuoteInvoiceLinkData[] invoices); `QuoteInvoiceLinkData` (id, number?, status InvoiceStatusEnum); `QuoteFormContextData` (default_included_text (firm default, 2026-11-03), ClientOptionData[] clients, ObjectOptionData[] objects, is_vat_payer, ?vat_rate, vat_rate_options from config('invoicing.vat_rates'), default_validity_days from config('quotes.default_validity_days')); `MediaFileData` (uuid, file_name, ?mime_type, size, download_url).
- Policies: QuotePolicy (viewAny/view/downloadPdf → ViewQuotes; create/duplicate → CreateQuotes; update/attachClient → EditQuotes; delete → DeleteQuotes; send → SendQuotes; accept/reject → ApproveQuotes; convertToInvoice → CreateInvoices — note Sekretárka lacks CreateInvoices by seed, hence cannot convert). Permission-only (state guards in service → 422).
- Controllers: QuoteController (use ProvidesSubjectOptions), #[NavItem] quotes index (DocumentDuplicateIcon, ViewQuotes, order 35). Routes: GET /quotes (index), GET /quotes/create, POST /quotes (Precognition), GET /quotes/{quote}, GET /quotes/{quote}/edit, PUT/PATCH /quotes/{quote} (Precognition), DELETE /quotes/{quote}, POST /quotes/{quote}/{send|accept|reject|duplicate} (action routes), POST /quotes/{quote}/attach-client (Precognition), POST /quotes/{quote}/convert-to-invoice, GET /quotes/{quote}/pdf (document: stream from media; itemized: render PDF). All routes whereUuid, inside auth+tenant.required.
- Events: `QuoteSent(string $tenantId, string $quoteId)`, `QuoteExpired(string $tenantId, string $quoteId)`, `QuoteExpiring(string $tenantId, string $quoteId, int $daysLeft)` — `implements ShouldDispatchAfterCommit` (zero listeners phase 5; phase 5 notifications subscribes).
- Factories: QuoteFactory (states numbered, sent, accepted, rejected, expired, vatPayer, withoutClient, document, forClient, forObject), QuoteItemFactory.
- Rules: `ObjectBelongsToClient` (DataAwareRule, reads client_id from payload; null passes; validates object belongs to client or 422 object_requires_client / object_not_of_client); `TemporaryMediaConstraints` (validates mime in whitelist + size ≤ KB from config('quotes.document.allowed_mimes'|max_size_kb), reads Media.mime_type which Spatie sniffed by content at upload; errors quote_document_invalid_type / quote_document_too_large).
- Config: `config/quotes.php` (document.disk default 'local'= storage/app/private, document.max_size_kb 10240, document.allowed_mimes, default_validity_days 30, expiring_notice_days [7,3,1]).

**FE Satellites:**
- Composables: none (form state via useForm).
- Components: `QuoteStatusBadge`, `QuoteKindBadge`, `QuoteRoughBadge` (badge for clientless), `QuoteDocumentUpload` (FileUploadInput + info; accepts pdf/image; v-model document_uuid), `QuoteAttachClientPanel` (appears only when client_id === null; form to attach + optional object; populated with ClientOptionData[] + ObjectOptionData[]), `QuoteForm` row-extra on the shared `InvoiceItemsEditor` (billing period select, frequency select, "Popis služby" textarea; `descriptionLabel` prop, 2026-11-03; `QuoteItemsEditor.vue` no longer exists), `QuoteSubjectPicker` (client/object/clientless radios; object options filtered by client), `QuoteFormSummary` (live per-group totals via `QuoteTotalsSplit`, 2026-11-03; no VAT recap), `QuoteTotalsSplit` (per-group block; VAT payer or not), `QuoteItemsTable` (Show items: name bold + note muted + frequency for the admin view; quantity / unit price / line cells from `utils/quoteBilling.ts`), `QuoteIncludedList` (bullets from `includedLines`), `QuotePeriodTotalsInline` (Index total cell), `QuoteRecurringInvoiceAction` (link for one recurring group, dropdown for several; gated by `create recurring_invoices`).
- Pages: `Quotes/{Index,Create,Edit,Show}` (Index = DataTable with filters/sort; Create = form; Edit = form + attach panel if clientless; Show = detail + attach panel if clientless + related invoices section).
- i18n: sk/en/uk keys (quotes, quote_status_*, quote_kind_*, quote_created/updated/sent/accepted/rejected/duplicated/client_attached/converted_to_invoice/deleted, quote_not_editable, quote_invalid_transition, quote_not_acceptable_for_conversion, quote_already_has_client, quote_document_*, object_requires_client, object_not_of_client).

**Flow:**
- **Create itemized:** POST /quotes (QuoteUpsertData: client or snapshot; itemized items + dates) → QuoteService::create (DocumentItemService::replacePriced → billingTotals) → 302 show + flash.
- **Create document:** POST /quotes (kind=document, document_uuid staged) → moveToModel onto the quote's Document (`file`) → documents disk → 302 show.
- **Send:** POST /quotes/{id}/send (Draft/Sent) → status validation → dispatch QuoteSent event → 302 show.
- **Accept/Reject:** POST /quotes/{id}/accept|reject (Draft/Sent) → stamp accepted_at / rejected_at.
- **Attach client:** POST /quotes/{id}/attach-client (while client_id IS NULL, any status) → null 5 snapshot columns → 302 show.
- **Duplicate:** POST /quotes/{id}/duplicate (Draft only) → copy as Draft, number=null, items copied → 302 edit.
- **Delete:** DELETE /quotes/{id} (Draft only) → soft-delete.
- **Convert to invoice:** POST /quotes/{id}/convert-to-invoice (Accepted, not document) → build InvoiceUpsertData from one-off items only (422 `status` `quote_has_no_one_off_items` when none), snapshot if clientless → InvoiceService::create → link quote_id → 302 invoices.show.
- **Recurring prefill (2026-11-03):** GET /recurring-invoices/create?quote_id={uuid}&billing_period=monthly|quarterly|yearly → `RecurringInvoiceController::create` → `QuoteService::recurringInvoicePrefill` (quote read tenant-scoped via findOrFail, `Gate::authorize('view')`; absent or invalid params → prefill null) → Create form prefilled with that group only → existing store (creates an Active template, starts nothing). No backlink column. Guard failures are 422 `status` and redirect back to Quotes/Show (StatusErrorAlert).
- **PDF:** GET /quotes/{id}/pdf (document: stream stored media; itemized: render via chrome driver) → Content-Disposition attachment.

**Depends on:** tenancy (tenant_id FK, BelongsToTenant, TenantScope), clients (client_id FK, withTrashed relation), objects (object_id FK + ObjectBelongsToClient rule, withTrashed relation), media + documents (Quote `document()` MorphOne to Document, private DOCUMENTS_DISK; temporary upload → moveToModel contract), invoices (convertToInvoice creates invoice, links quote_id), recurring-invoices (prefill target; `RecurringInvoiceController::create` reads `QuoteService::recurringInvoicePrefill`), settings-invoicing (signing place, default included text), documents (QuoteDisplay shared with the document blocks).

**Depended on by:** invoices (quote_id FK, source link in show), contracts phase 6 (convertToContract, links via quote_id deferred), recurring-invoices (prefill), document-templates (`quote.total*` tokens, `block.quote_items` / `block.quote_totals`), mcp (`DescribesDocumentItems`, `GetQuoteTool`, `CreateQuoteDraftTool`, `UpdateQuoteDraftTool`), mobile-api (additive `period_totals`, `billing_period`).

**If you change Core, check:**
- QuoteStatusEnum transitions: Draft→{Sent, Expired}; Sent→{Accepted, Rejected, Expired}; Accepted/Rejected/Expired terminal. Document kind has no lifecycle (all actions blocked except view/pdf/delete).
- QuoteService::convertToInvoice: one-off items only (422 `status` `quote_has_no_one_off_items` when none), item description via `itemDescription` (frequency label suffix + note, 255 cap), clientless snapshot carried, ItemizedOnly guard, AcceptedStatus guard.
- DocumentTotalsCalculator parity: invoice + quote math identical (line, total with VAT breakdown, rounding modes match).
- QuoteItem.frequency column: phase 7 consumer (work breakdown generation from accepted quotes).
- Media::move honours collection disk: staged file on public temp disk → moves to private DOCUMENTS_DISK on moveToModel.
- TemporaryMediaConstraints: validates against Media.mime_type (content-sniffed at upload, not filename).
- Precognition on cross-field rules (client_id + customer_name mutually exclusive, kind immutable): cross-field validation deferred to submit unless full payload sent.
- ExpireQuotes command: unbound iteration with SoftDelete scope exclusion (withoutGlobalScope), per-quote event dispatch.
- `period_totals` written only by `QuoteService::billingTotals` (per-group `DocumentItemService::totals`, invoice math parity, QuoteTotalsParityTest); a group is absent when it has no items; `QuoteBillingPeriodEnum` cases vs migration default `one_off` and the backfill SQL.
- Display rules: `QuoteBillingPeriodEnum` (label / adverb / unitNoun / shortSuffix) + `QuoteDisplay` drive the quote PDF, `block.quote_items` / `block.quote_totals` and the `quote.total*` tokens; change them together.
- `QuoteService::recurringInvoicePrefill` guards (document → 422 `quote_document_not_convertible`; not Accepted → 422 `quote_not_acceptable_for_conversion`; one_off or empty group → 422 `quote_has_no_items_for_period`) stay in step with `convertToInvoice`.

**Keywords (SK):** cenová ponuka, nástrel, položková, dokumentová, platná do, prijatá, konverzia, dobropis, súpis, fakturačné obdobie, jednorazovo, mesačne, štvrťročne, ročne, V cene je zahrnuté, logo, miesto podpisu.

### invoices — Draft / issue / pay / cancel + credit notes, SK VAT, Pay-by-Square QR (Phase 4, 2026-09-06; payments, bulk, EFA, Pohoda export added by 2026-10-31 reconcile)

**Core:**
- `App\Models\Invoice` — UUIDv7; traits BelongsToTenant, HasFactory, HasPdfFilename, HasUuids, LogsActivity (logOnlyDirty status/number/total/client_id/issued_at/paid_at/cancelled_at/sent_at), SoftDeletes. Columns: tenant_id FK, nullable client_id FK → clients (withTrashed), nullable cleaning_object_id FK → objects (withTrashed), nullable recurring_invoice_id FK → recurring_invoices (restrictOnDelete), nullable credited_invoice_id self-FK (restrictOnDelete), **nullable quote_id FK → quotes (nullOnDelete)** (audit backlink when generated from quote.convertToInvoice; invoice keeps snapshot so delete doesn't cascade; index quote_id), type (InvoiceTypeEnum: monthly/one_off/special), status (InvoiceStatusEnum: draft/issued/paid/overdue/cancelled), template (InvoiceTemplateEnum: classic/modern/minimal), nullable number (50 chars, partial unique index tenant_id + number WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND number IS NOT NULL), variable_symbol (10 chars, derived from number digits only), dates (period_from/period_to/issue_date/delivery_date/due_date nullable except issue_date), timestamps issued_at/sent_at/paid_at/cancelled_at nullable, **customer snapshot** (customer_name required, representative/ico/dic/vat_number/street/city/postal_code/country/email nullable; **`customer_represented_by` string 255 nullable** (2026-11-01) = client name when the house is the buyer, printed "v zastúpení"; `customer_representative` stays the standalone-mode contact person, a different fact), **object snapshot** (object_name/street/city/postal_code nullable), **supplier snapshot** (supplier_name required, ico/dic/vat_number/iban/swift/address_line/city/postal_code/country/contact_email/contact_phone/registration_info nullable, snapshot at issue from Tenant), **signature snapshot:** nullable `supplier_signature_media_id` (bigint FK → media, nullOnDelete) stamped at issue from `tenants.signature_media_id` (draft resolves tenant's current signature live; issued renders snapshot forever), **issuer snapshot:** nullable `issued_by_name` (string 255) stamped at issue from actor's TenantMembership.display_name or __('app.invoice_issued_by_system') for unattended jobs, bool is_vat_payer, nullable vat_rate (decimal), money columns (subtotal/vat_amount/total/deposit/rounding_amount default 0, all decimal:12,2), json vat_breakdown (nullable, frozen at issue), **SK fields** (constant_symbol/specific_symbol/payment_type/currency/rounding_mode/header_text/footer_text all nullable except currency EUR default), nullable note, **payments:** `paid_amount` decimal(12,2) default 0 (sum of invoice_payments, maintained by InvoiceService::recomputePaymentState), **e-invoice (EFA/Peppol):** `efa_status` (EfaStatusEnum none/ready/generated/submitted/accepted/rejected/failed, NOT NULL default 'none'), efa_id, efa_submitted_at, efa_last_error, customer_peppol_participant_id (snapshot at create from the buyer: the house when the object has billing identity, else the client; a client's Peppol ID is ignored in house mode), supplier_peppol_participant_id (snapshot from tenant), timestamps, soft_deletes. Indexes (tenant_id,status), (tenant_id,due_date), (tenant_id,issue_date), (tenant_id,client_id), recurring_invoice_id, credited_invoice_id, quote_id. Relations: `client()->withTrashed()`, `cleaningObject()->withTrashed()`, `items(): HasMany<InvoiceItem>` ordered position, `payments(): HasMany<InvoicePayment>`, `quote(): BelongsTo<Quote>` (nullable, audit trail), `creditedInvoice(): BelongsTo`, `creditNote(): HasOne credited_invoice_id`, `recurringInvoice(): BelongsTo`, `supplierSignatureMedia(): BelongsTo<Media>`. Attributes/methods: `balance_due` (total − deposit − paid_amount), `amount_payable` (total − deposit), `isEditable()` (Draft), `canBeCancelled()` (Issued | Overdue), `isCreditNote()`, `isPartiallyPaid()`, `isSettled()` (|balance_due| < 0.005). Implements HasMedia: collection `efa_xml` on config('invoicing.signature.disk'). LogsActivity also logs efa_status.
- `App\Models\InvoicePayment` — UUIDv7, BelongsToTenant, LogsActivity, SoftDeletes. Columns: tenant_id, invoice_id (restrictOnDelete), amount decimal(12,2) (negative for credit-note balances), paid_on date, payment_type (PaymentTypeEnum), source (PaymentSourceEnum manual|bank_import, default manual), external_reference (128 nullable), note, recorded_by_user_id (nullable FK users restrictOnDelete). Indexes (tenant_id, invoice_id), (tenant_id, paid_on). Relations invoice, recordedBy.
- `App\Models\InvoiceItem` — UUIDv7; traits BelongsToTenant, HasFactory, HasUuids. Columns: tenant_id FK, invoice_id FK (restrictOnDelete), description, qty/unit/unit_price (decimal:10,2), discount_percent (decimal:5,2), vat_rate (decimal:5,2), computed line_base/line_vat/line_total (decimal:12,2), position (smallInt). Index invoice_id. Relation: `invoice(): BelongsTo`.
- `App\Models\InvoiceNumberSequence` — UUIDv7; traits BelongsToTenant, HasUuids. Columns: tenant_id FK, year (smallInt), last_number (int), timestamps. Unique (tenant_id, year). No activity logging (operational).
- `App\Services\InvoiceNumberService` — `next(Tenant, DateTimeInterface): string` (firstOrCreate + lockForUpdate + collision loop honouring `{YYYY}/{YY}/{MM}/{X+}` format placeholders), `variableSymbol(string): ?string` (digits max 10 chars).
- `App\Support\CustomerParty::forInvoice(?CleaningObject, ?Client, CustomerParty $entered): self` (2026-11-01 rule; moved 2026-11-04 out of `InvoiceService::customerAttributes`; the single buyer resolver and the only writer of `customer_*` via `toInvoiceAttributes()`, called from `InvoiceService::buildAttributes`; `fromEntered` / `fromQuote` build the entered side). Three disjoint branches, values never mix: (1) house, when `$object?->hasBillingIdentity()`: `customer_name/ico/dic/vat_number/peppol_participant_id` = `billing_*`; address = `billing_street/city/postal_code` only when at least one of them is set, otherwise the object's `street/city/postal_code` (whole-address fallback, no field mixing); `customer_country` = `object.country`; `customer_represented_by` = `$client?->name`. (2) client, when `$client !== null`: today's client snapshot, `customer_represented_by` null. (3) standalone: form `customer_*` values, `customer_represented_by` null. Each branch also sets `CustomerParty::$source` (`App\Enums\CustomerPartySourceEnum`: object_billing | client | entered; default entered), read by `InvoiceBuyerData::fromParty` (2026-11-05). In branches 1 and 2 form values are ignored except `customer_representative` and `customer_email` (`$buyer`). `duplicate` and the credit note in `cancel` copy `customer_represented_by` like every other `customer_*` column. Draft `update` re-snapshots through the same resolver; issued/cancelled invoices never (`isEditable()`). `InvoiceService` private `supplierAttributes(Tenant)` (incl. `supplier_peppol_participant_id`) and `objectAttributes(?CleaningObject)` fill the rest (2026-11-04).
- `App\Services\InvoiceNumberService::continueSeries(Tenant, int $year, int $lastNumber): bool` (2026-11-05) — firstOrCreate + lockForUpdate; raises the year's `last_number` to the legacy last number, never lowers it; returns whether it raised. Caller: LegacyRecurringInvoiceImporter only. A later issue numbers `last + 1`.
- `App\Services\InvoiceService` (final readonly, ctor InvoiceNumberService, DatabaseManager, DocumentItemService) — `paginate(Request): LengthAwarePaginator<InvoiceListItemData>` (QueryBuilder AllowedFilter search/number/status/type/client_id/customer_name/issue_date/due_date/total/created_at, sorts default -created_at, eager load client), `stats(): InvoiceStatsData` (issued this month / overdue / pending / recurring monthly), `create(InvoiceUpsertData): Invoice` (transaction: snapshot customer/object/supplier + DocumentItemService::replacePriced → DocumentItemService::totals with VAT breakdown + rounding), `update(Invoice, InvoiceUpsertData): Invoice` (guard isEditable), `issue(Invoice, InvoiceIssueData, ?User $actor): Invoice` (guard Draft → guard supplier completeness: `$tenant->missingSupplierFields() !== []` → ValidationException key `supplier` + __('app.invoice_supplier_incomplete') before transaction, no sequence consumed; then tenant-scoped number exists check, set number/variable_symbol/status/issued_at/**supplier_signature_media_id from $tenant->signature_media_id**/**issued_by_name from actor's TenantMembership.display_name or __('app.invoice_issued_by_system')** via private `resolveIssuerName(string $tenantId, ?User $actor): string` helper), **`recordPayment(Invoice, InvoicePaymentData, ?User $actor): InvoicePayment` (replaces markPaid: guard canTransitionTo(Paid), lockForUpdate, amount > 0 and ≤ |balance_due| else 422 `amount`, create InvoicePayment source=manual, recomputePaymentState → paid_amount; settled → status Paid + paid_at)**, **`deletePayment(Invoice, InvoicePayment): void`** (422 when Cancelled; recompute; Paid reverts to Issued/Overdue by due_date when no longer settled), `cancel(Invoice, ?User $actor): Invoice` (transaction: mark Cancelled + create credit note with negated items/SK fields/**+ stamp both supplier_signature_media_id and issued_by_name on credit note** since credit note is born Issued with own number), `duplicate(Invoice): Invoice` (Draft copy, no number, **signature_media_id and issued_by_name both null** so draft resolves live), `delete(Invoice): void` (guard isEditable), `send(Invoice): void` (guard Issued + customer_email, dispatch InvoiceIssued queued notification).
- `App\Services\InvoiceBulkService` (final readonly; InvoiceService, RendersInvoicePdf, MergesPdfDocuments) — `pay(ids, paidOn, ?User)` (one full-balance recordPayment per invoice), `send(ids)`, `issue(ids, ?User)` (per-item ValidationException → BulkSkippedItemData with BulkSkipReasonEnum not_found/not_issued/no_email/not_draft/not_payable/supplier_incomplete/number_taken; returns BulkActionResultData), `mergedPdf(ids): string` (renders each via RendersInvoicePdf, single → as is, many → `MergesPdfDocuments::merge`). Limits config('invoicing.bulk'): max_ids 100, pdf_max_ids 10, merge_timeout_seconds 60, qpdf_binary 'qpdf' (exposed to FE as `bulkLimits` prop = InvoiceBulkLimitsData).
- `App\Services\Pdf\QpdfMerger implements MergesPdfDocuments` — shells out to the `qpdf` binary (installed in the app image, commit cdeed4e), temp files cleaned in finally, failure → PdfMergeFailedException. Bound in AppServiceProvider.
- `App\Services\Efa\EInvoiceService` (UblInvoiceXmlBuilder, SubmitsEInvoices, DatabaseManager) — `status(Invoice): EInvoiceStatusData` (readiness missing fields, canSubmit only when a non-null access point is bound), `xml(Invoice): string` (422 if Draft or not ready; flips efa_status none→ready), `submit(Invoice): Invoice` (lockForUpdate + efa_status→Submitted before provider call, restores on ValidationException, Failed + efa_last_error on Throwable, on success stores XML into media `efa_xml` and sets efa_id/efa_submitted_at). `SubmitsEInvoices` bound by `config('efa.driver')` (default `null` → NullEInvoiceAccessPoint; no live provider yet). `UblInvoiceXmlBuilder` builds Peppol BIS 3.0 UBL using config/efa.php maps (unit codes, payment means, participant schemes, mandatory_from 2027-01-01). Endpoint `endpoint(participantId, dic, country)`: stored `peppol_participant_id` when `PeppolParticipantId::isValid`, else for an SK party a 10-digit DIČ (whitespace and U+00A0 stripped) as `config('efa.dic_endpoint_scheme_id')` (0245):DIČ, else none; IČO is never an endpoint. `PartyLegalEntity/CompanyID` = IČO with schemeID `config('efa.ico_legal_scheme_id')` (0158) for SK parties only. `readiness(Invoice): list<string>` derives from the same endpoint(): missing `supplier_dic` / `customer_dic` when no endpoint (`customer_ico` no longer checked); a non-ready invoice gets no XML and no submit; PDF, issue and ordinary send unaffected. Inputs: invoices.customer_peppol_participant_id + customer_dic, supplier_peppol_participant_id + supplier_dic. TODO verify: 0158 scheme and the 10-digit DIČ endpoint rule against the official Slovak Peppol Authority spec (only vendor guides and OpenPeppol lists read). Open: no live access point (`efa.driver` null); per-firm access point credentials to design after provider choice, before mandate 2027-01-01.
