- `App\Services\Export\PohodaXmlBuilder` — `export(Tenant, PohodaExportData): string`, `preview(...): PohodaExportPreviewData`, `build(...)`; config/pohoda.php (max_invoices 2000, max_range_days 366, vat_band_map 23/19/5/0). Controllers: InvoiceExportController (`export` ability), InvoiceBulkController (bulkPay/bulkSend/bulkIssue/bulkPdf), EInvoiceController (downloadEfaXml → ViewInvoices, submitEfa → EditInvoices).
- `App\Services\InvoiceSettingsService` — see settings-invoicing.
- Contracts: `RendersInvoicePdf { public function render(Invoice $invoice): string; }`, `GeneratesPaymentQr { public function dataUri(Invoice $invoice): ?string; }`, **`ResolvesSupplierSignature { public function forInvoice(Invoice): ?string; public function forTenant(Tenant): ?string; }`** (returns base64 data:image/* URI or null, never calls current_tenant_id(), fail-closed on tenant_id mismatch or missing file).
- `App\Services\Pdf\InvoicePdfService implements RendersInvoicePdf` — chrome driver, single KEGOS template `pdf.invoices.kegos` (Classic/Modern/Minimal removed; shared `pdf/partials/kegos-*`). Body order (2026-11-06): parties table (supplier left, customer right) → symbols + dates table → PAY by square block (QR + IBAN / VS / due date / amount due, only when QR or IBAN is present; was after the totals before 2026-11-06) → `header_text` intro (`white-space: pre-line`, directly above the items table) → items table full-width (dark header rule, `#F2F5F4` header, unit from `item.unit`) → note + signature block + totals (`sums`) → non-VAT clause → `footer_text`. Renders with `signatureDataUri` (ResolvesSupplierSignature::forInvoice). Footer `pdf.invoices.kegos-footer`, `margins(18, 18, 26, 18)` mm. **headerHtml('<div></div>')** suppresses Chromium's default header (date + title) when footer is set.
- `App\Services\Pdf\PayBySquareService implements GeneratesPaymentQr` — null unless Issued|Overdue + IBAN + EUR + balance_due > 0.
- **`App\Services\Pdf\SupplierSignatureResolver implements ResolvesSupplierSignature`** (final readonly, no constructor; uses Storage and Log facades) — `forInvoice($invoice)` loads media by `supplier_signature_media_id` with `collection_name = 'signature'` filter, or falls back to Tenant's signatureMedia (draft resolves live, issued uses snapshot) + encodes to data URI; `forTenant($tenant)` loads media from `$tenant->signatureMedia`; **private `encode(?Media, string $expectedTenantId)` fails closed:** null media → null, tenant_id mismatch → warn + null, file missing → warn + null, else base64 data URI. Bound in AppServiceProvider.
- `App\Notifications\InvoiceIssued` — ctor `(string $invoiceId, ?string $subject = null, ?string $body = null)` (2026-11-06: the override is a public property, so it sits in the queued payload and is never stored); queued (ShouldQueue, #[Tries(3)] #[Backoff] #[Timeout(120)]), afterCommit, mail-only, `->locale(config('documents.locale'))` set by the sender, mail text from `PlaceholderResolverService::invoiceMailContent`, PDF attachment via RendersInvoicePdf, sent_at stamped by StampInvoiceSentAt listener on NotificationSent event (every send, so a re-send moves it).
- **Mobile issue and send (2026-11-06):** `Api\InvoiceController` `issue` → `InvoiceService::issue` (`#[Authorize('issue')]`, body `InvoiceIssueData` with null number = next in series); `sendDraft` → `InvoiceRecipientService::sendDraft(Invoice, User): InvoiceSendDraftData` (`#[Authorize('send')]`, 422 `status` unless `canBeSent()`, content via `DocumentLocale::run` + `invoiceMailContent` with no override, `email_options`/`save_target` from `sendOptions` only when `customer_email` is null, `attachment_name` = `pdfFilenameBase().pdf`); `send` → `InvoiceRecipientService::send` (202 with fresh `InvoiceDetailData`, `sent_at` still null until the worker runs). `stats` → `InvoiceService::stats()` (`viewAny`). Blank `subject`/`body` fall back to firm template, then lang default.
- `App\Events\InvoiceMarkedOverdue` — domain event dispatched by MarkOverdueInvoices command (no phase-4 listeners; phase-5 notifications subscribes).
- `App\Console\Commands\MarkOverdueInvoices` — daily cron, flips Issued past due_date → Overdue, dispatches InvoiceMarkedOverdue.

**Satellites (BE):**
- **Buyer preview and recipient e-mail (2026-11-05):** `App\Services\InvoiceRecipientService` (final readonly, ctor InvoiceService) — `previewBuyer(InvoiceBuyerPreviewData, User): InvoiceBuyerPreviewResultData` (client + optional object → `CustomerParty::forInvoice` → `InvoiceBuyerData::fromParty` + `email_options`, nothing persisted); `sendOptions(Invoice, User): ?InvoiceSendOptionsData` (non-null only when Issued, `customer_email` null and `Gate::allows('send')`); `send(Invoice, InvoiceSendData, User): void`. `email_options` order: object `billing_email` (needs `viewBilling`), object contacts (`viewContacts`, primary first), client contacts (`view` client, primary first); deduped case-insensitive, blanks dropped; no default is ever preselected. `saveTarget`: `recurring_invoice` when the invoice's template is Active/Paused and `update` allowed; else `object` when the object is not trashed, `update` allowed and `viewBilling`; else null. DTOs `InvoiceBuyerData` (source, name, represented_by, representative, ico, dic, vat_number, street, city, postal_code, country, email; `fromParty(CustomerParty)`), `InvoiceBuyerPreviewData` (client_id, cleaning_object_id?), `InvoiceBuyerPreviewResultData` (buyer, email_options), `InvoiceSendData` (recipient_email? `Email Max(255)`, save_for_future=false), `InvoiceSendOptionsData` (email_options, save_target?), enum `InvoiceEmailSaveTargetEnum` (recurring_invoice | object). Routes: `GET /invoices/buyer-preview` (`invoices.buyer-preview`, `InvoiceController::buyerPreview`, `#[Authorize('previewBuyer', Invoice::class)]`, `InvoicePolicy::previewBuyer` = any of CreateInvoices/EditInvoices/CreateRecurringInvoices/EditRecurringInvoices, no throttle); `POST /invoices/{invoice}/send` (`invoices.send`, `#[Authorize('send', 'invoice')]`, body `InvoiceSendData`). `InvoiceController::show` adds prop `sendOptions`. Grep-verified: routes/web.php lines 227 and 235; `InvoicePolicy::send` = EditInvoices.
- **Helpers:** `format_country(?string $code): ?string` (2026-11-06, `app/Support/helpers.php`): trimmed code → lang `app.country_{code}` (`country_sk` Slovensko / Slovakia / Словаччина, `country_cz` Česko / Czechia / Чехія); unknown code returned as sent, blank → null; render-only (kegos PDF), see Gotchas 2026-11-06. `format_quantity(float $quantity): string` — trims trailing zeros off 2-dp quantity: `111.00` → `111`, `1.50` → `1,5`, `0.25` → `0,25` (NBSP thousands separator per SK convention). Declared in `app/Support/helpers.php`, testable unit function (not inline blade), reusable once modern/minimal templates adopt quantity trimming.
- Enums: InvoiceStatusEnum (draft/issued/paid/overdue/cancelled, `canTransitionTo(self $to): bool`), InvoiceTypeEnum (monthly/one_off/special), PaymentTypeEnum (transfer/cash/card/cod/other), **CurrencyEnum** (EUR/CZK/USD uppercase, **`symbol(): string`** → '€'/'Kč'/'$', **`format(float $amount, int $maxDecimals = 2): string`** → `2 011,88 €` via number_format + NBSP separator + suffix, significant decimals preserved up to maxDecimals), RoundingModeEnum (none/document/cash_005, `round(float): float`).
- DTOs: `InvoiceUpsertData` (client_id nullable, object_id nullable, type/template/dates, customer_* snapshot fields, items, SK fields, payment fields, deposit), `InvoiceItemData` (description required, max 255 since 2026-11-03; qty/unit/unit_price/discount/vat_rate + line_* nullable), `InvoiceIssueData { ?string $number }`, `InvoiceListItemData` (id/number/status/type/customer_name/client_id/client_name/object_name/currency/total/balance_due/issue_date/due_date), `InvoiceDetailData` (full DTO + vat_breakdown array + qr_data_uri + `quote_id: string|null` + `quote_number: string|null` (backlink to source quote if generated via convertToInvoice) + `supplier_missing_fields: string[]` empty when Issued or status terminal, non-empty on Draft; vocabulary keys: name/address_line/city/postal_code/ico/dic/vat_number), `InvoiceSupplierData`, `VatBreakdownLineData`, `InvoiceStatCardData`, `InvoiceStatsData`, `InvoiceFormContextData` (clients/objects/is_vat_payer/defaults/recurring_default_state + `supplier_missing_fields: string[]` from tenant; mounted in Create/Edit/Show forms for banner), `InvoiceSettingsData` (supplier name/ico/dic/vat_number/is_vat_payer/address/contact + invoice defaults template/number_format + recurring defaults; **new:** `?string $signature_uuid`, `bool $remove_signature = false` mutually exclusive via `prohibited_if` validation), **`TenantSignatureData` (#[TypeScript], nullable on fromTenant)** (url: string route('settings.invoicing.signature'), file_name, mime_type, size, uploaded_at ISO 8601), **`InvoiceSignatureConstraintsData` (#[TypeScript])** (allowed_mimes: string[], max_size_kb: int, static fromConfig() reads config('invoicing.signature.*')), `ObjectOptionData` (for pickers).
- Policies: InvoicePolicy (viewAny/view/create/update/issue/**recordPayment/deletePayment**/send/cancel/delete/downloadPdf/**downloadEfaXml/submitEfa/bulkPay/bulkSend/bulkIssue/bulkPdf/export** per PermissionEnum — recordPayment/submitEfa/bulk write abilities need EditInvoices, read-type ones ViewInvoices).
- Controllers: InvoiceController (#[NavItem] invoices), InvoiceSettingsController (#[NavItem] invoicing_settings, ManageBillingSettings gate).
- Routes: GET|POST /invoices; GET|PUT|DELETE /invoices/{invoice}; POST /invoices/{invoice}/issue|pay (pay = recordPayment, InvoicePaymentData)|cancel|duplicate|send; DELETE /invoices/{invoice}/payments/{payment} (scopeBindings); GET /invoices/{invoice}/pdf; GET /invoices/{invoice}/preview (2026-11-06, inline); GET /invoices/{invoice}/efa-xml, POST /invoices/{invoice}/efa-submit; GET /invoices/export/pohoda{,/preview}; GET /invoices/bulk/pdf, POST /invoices/bulk/pay|send|issue (Precognition); GET|PUT /settings/invoicing (Precognition, DTO now includes signature_uuid + remove_signature); (GET /settings/invoicing/logo removed 2026-11-05, `Route::has('settings.invoicing.logo')` is false), **GET /settings/invoicing/signature** (streams file inline from private disk, #[Authorize(ManageBillingSettings)], 404 if no signature or file missing); **GET /settings/invoicing/preview** (#[Authorize(ManageBillingSettings)], inlines tenant signature as data URI in footer, matches signature stream gate; **renders footer when `$template->footerView()` present**).
- Commands: MarkOverdueInvoices (routes/console.php), GenerateRecurringInvoices (routes/console.php).
- Factories: InvoiceFactory (states forClient/forObject/issued/paid/overdue/cancelled/vatPayer/nonVatPayer/withDeposit), InvoiceItemFactory.
- i18n keys: nav (invoices, invoicing_settings); enums; flash (invoice_created/updated/issued/paid/cancelled/duplicated/send_queued); errors (invoice_not_editable, invoice_not_draft, invoice_number_taken, invoice_cannot_mark_paid, invoice_cannot_cancel, invoice_no_customer_email, invoice_object_requires_client); PDF (40 keys: invoice_pdf_{title,customer,supplier,ico,…,payment_type,note,non_vat_payer_clause}), mail (subject/greeting/body).

**FE Satellites:**
- **Náhľad and display (2026-11-06):** `InvoiceActionsCard` emits `preview` → Show opens `DocumentPdfPreviewModal` (`useDocumentPdfPreview({ load: getBlob(/invoices/{id}/preview) })`). Object block removed from `InvoiceMetaGrid` and `InvoiceLinksCard`; the object stays in the buyer rule and the edit picker. `countryName` (`utils/country.ts`, i18n `country_*`, falls back to the raw code) in `InvoicePartiesBlock` and `CustomerPartyDetails`.
- Composables: `useInvoiceTotals(items, isVatPayer, deposit, roundingMode)` → lines/subtotal/vatAmount/total/roundingAmount/balanceDue/vatBreakdown (mirrors BE math with RoundingModeEnum::round).
- Components: `InvoiceStatusBadge`, `InvoiceTypeBadge` (with credit-note variant), `InvoiceVatRecap`, `InvoiceTotalsPanel`, `InvoiceItemsEditor` (card-per-row custom field, FormProvider-aware, per-row quantity/unit/unit_price/discount/vat_rate grid), `InvoiceSubjectPicker` (client/object/standalone radios), `InvoiceItemsTable` (read-only; invoice-only since 2026-11-03, quote `frequency` / `note` removed; `InvoiceItemsEditor` gained optional `descriptionLabel`), `InvoiceIssueModal` (Precognition form, number field), `InvoiceStatsCards` (4 cards: issued/overdue/pending/recurring), `InvoiceFormSummary` (summary panel for forms).
- Pages: `Invoices/{Index,Create,Edit,Show}` (Index with stats + DataTable + filters; Create/Edit with InvoiceForm; Show with parties/meta/items/totals/actions).
- Settings pages: `Settings/Invoicing` (supplier/bank/numbering/templates/defaults).
- i18n: 60+ FE keys (see phase4-invoicing-fe.md §"i18n").

**Money math (`DocumentItemService::totals`, same as quotes):**
- `line_base = round2(qty * price * (1 - discount/100))`; `line_vat = round2(line_base * rate/100)` (0 if !isVatPayer); `line_total = round2(line_base + line_vat)`.
- `subtotal = Σline_base`; `vatAmount = Σline_vat`; `totalBeforeRounding = round2(subtotal + vatAmount)`.
- `roundAmount(amount, mode)`: none→amount; document→Math.round(amount); cash_005→round to 0.05.
- `total = round2(roundAmount(totalBeforeRounding, mode))`; `roundingAmount = round2(total - totalBeforeRounding)`.
- `balanceDue = round2(total - deposit)`; `vatBreakdown` grouped by rate desc, empty when !isVatPayer.
- **Snapshot timing:** customer/object/supplier columns written on create/update; `issue` freezes them (invoice becomes non-editable). Non-editable invoices can still be marked paid/overdue/cancelled.
- **Credit notes:** cancel (Issued|Overdue) creates Issued credit note with negated items/subtotal/vat/total/deposit/rounding/vat_breakdown + all SK fields copied + own number via `next(tenant, now())`, linked via `credited_invoice_id`.

**Lifecycle guards:**
- Draft (edit/delete/issue allowed). Issued (no edit; pay/cancel/send/pdf allowed). Overdue (no edit; pay/cancel/send/pdf allowed; send since 2026-11-06 = reminder). Paid (no edit; cancel and pdf only; not sendable). Cancelled (no further actions except view/pdf).
- Status transitions: Draft→Issued (issue action); Issued→Paid/Overdue/Cancelled (mark-paid / cron / cancel); Overdue→Paid/Cancelled; Paid/Cancelled terminal.

**Flow (all within auth + tenant.required):**
- **Create:** POST /invoices (Precognition) → InvoiceFormContextData renders form → InvoiceService::create (snapshot + items + totals) → 302 show + flash success.
- **Issue:** Draft invoice → InvoiceIssueModal or POST /invoices/{id}/issue (Precognition on number, InvoiceIssueData) → InvoiceService::issue (number assignment via lockForUpdate loop, status/issued_at/variable_symbol set) → 302 show + flash.
- **Pay:** Issued/Overdue → POST /invoices/{id}/pay (InvoicePaymentData: paid_on, amount, payment_type, note) → InvoiceService::recordPayment (partial payments allowed; settled → Paid + paid_at) → 302 show + flash `invoice_payment_recorded`. Payment removal via DELETE .../payments/{payment}.
- **Cancel:** Issued/Overdue → POST /invoices/{id}/cancel + confirm → InvoiceService::cancel (transaction: Cancelled, cancelled_at, credit note created) → 302 show + flash. Both invoices linked (original.credited_invoice_id ← note.id, note.credited_invoice_id ← original.id).
- **Buyer preview (2026-11-05):** subject picker (client/object) → `useBuyerPreview` debounce 250 ms → `GET /invoices/buyer-preview?client_id&cleaning_object_id` → `CustomerPartyDetails` shows the resolved buyer; e-mail input gets `email_options` as suggestions (native datalist, free typing kept). No request in standalone mode. Read-only: nothing is written until save.
- **Send (2026-11-05, Issued or Overdue since 2026-11-06 via `canBeSent()`):** Issued/Overdue + `customer_email` → POST /invoices/{id}/send with empty body → `InvoiceRecipientService::send` delegates to `InvoiceService::send` (unchanged). Issued without `customer_email` → Show opens `InvoiceSendModal` (sendOptions non-null) → POST with `InvoiceSendData {recipient_email, save_for_future}`: recipient required (422 `recipient_email`), save_for_future with no target → 422 `save_for_future`; in a transaction writes `invoices.customer_email` and, when saving, the template `customer_email` or `objects.billing_email`; then `InvoiceService::send`. Invoice never sent without an address.
- **Send (queue):** guard (canBeSent + customer_email) → dispatch InvoiceIssued job (carries the optional subject/body) → sent_at stamped by listener → 302 show + flash. PDF attachment via mocked (test) or real Chromium.
- **PDF:** GET /invoices/{id}/pdf (Content-Disposition: attachment); **Náhľad:** GET /invoices/{id}/preview (inline, same render, nothing stored, 2026-11-06) → RendersInvoicePdf renders via chrome driver, filename from invoice.pdfFilenameBase + `HeaderUtils::makeDisposition`.
- **Duplicate:** Draft only → POST /invoices/{id}/duplicate → InvoiceService::duplicate (copy without number/VS) → 302 edit + flash.

**Depends on:** tenancy (tenant_id FK, BelongsToTenant, TenantScope), clients (client_id FK + rule, withTrashed relation; client Peppol/DIČ/IČO are the buyer snapshot when the object has no billing identity; client contacts as e-mail suggestions), objects (cleaning_object_id FK + rule, withTrashed relation; `billing_*` + `hasBillingIdentity()` make the house the buyer; `billing_email` and object contacts as e-mail suggestions), recurring-invoices (recurring_invoice_id FK), quotes (quote_id FK nullOnDelete, audit backlink, InvoiceService.create receives built InvoiceUpsertData from QuoteService.convertToInvoice), media (temporary upload → moveToModel contract for future PDF attachments phase 6+).

**Depended on by:** recurring-invoices (InvoiceService consumed by RecurringInvoiceService::generateInvoiceFromTemplate), quotes (quote_id backlink for source tracking), Inertia show page (quote_id/quote_number link to Quotes/Show), mobile-api (Api\InvoiceController issue/send/send-draft/stats, 2026-11-06), PlaceholderResolverService (invoiceMailContent: InvoiceIssued and sendDraft).

**If you change Core, check:**
- `Invoice::canBeSent()` (Issued or Overdue) vs its callers: InvoiceService::send, InvoiceRecipientService send/sendOptions/sendDraft, InvoiceDetailData::can_be_sent, InvoiceActionsCard `isIssuedOrOverdue` (web, FE). Grep `canBeSent(` and `isIssuedOrOverdue` together before change; a status check added back anywhere else is a review finding.
- `PlaceholderResolverService::invoiceMailContent` vs `InvoiceIssued::toMail` and `InvoiceRecipientService::sendDraft`: both must render the same text; the token list (`invoiceMailVariables` + `invoiceMailCatalog`, incl. `object.name`, `invoice.subtotal`, `invoice.vat_amount`) and the lang keys `app.invoice_mail_token_*` in sk/en/uk move together.
- `InvoiceRecipientService::send` vs `InvoiceService::send`: the plain send keeps its guard (canBeSent + customer_email); bulk send (`InvoiceBulkService::send`) still skips `no_email`; recurring auto-send still needs a template `customer_email`. Callsites: `grep -rn "InvoiceRecipientService\|->send(" app`.
- `format_country` (kegos PDF only) and `countryName` (`InvoicePartiesBlock`, `CustomerPartyDetails`): both read `app.country_*` in sk/en/uk `app.json`; a new country needs all three keys. Grep `format_country(` and `countryName(` before a change. UBL, Pohoda and the `customer_country` / `supplier_country` snapshots keep the ISO code.
- `CustomerParty::forInvoice` branch order (house → client → standalone); `source` per branch feeds `InvoiceBuyerData::fromParty` (detail card and preview) and the field list (12 keys incl. `customer_represented_by`) vs `buildAttributes`, `duplicate`, credit note in `cancel`, `InvoiceDetailData`, `kegos.blade.php` buyer cell and `InvoicePartiesBlock.vue`. Callers of `buildAttributes`: `create`, `update` (via `InvoiceService`), reached from web, MCP `CreateInvoiceDraftTool` / `UpdateInvoiceDraftTool`, `QuoteService::convertToInvoice`, `RecurringInvoiceService::generateInvoiceFromTemplate`. Grep `CustomerParty::` / `buildAttributes(` before change.
- InvoiceStatusEnum canTransitionTo matrix (Draft→Issued; Issued→Paid|Overdue|Cancelled; Overdue→Paid|Cancelled; Paid/Cancelled terminal) vs InvoiceService::recomputePaymentState (can revert Paid → Issued/Overdue when a payment is deleted).
- InvoiceBulkService skip-reason mapping (keys of ValidationException errors: customer_email, supplier, number, amount) vs InvoiceService messages; BulkSkipReasonEnum FE labels.
- UblInvoiceXmlBuilder::endpoint() vs readiness() vs EInvoiceService::status/xml/submit gates; endpoint inputs = buyer Peppol ID (house `billing_peppol_participant_id` when the object has billing identity, else Client.peppol_participant_id) → invoices.customer_peppol_participant_id + customer_dic (house without DIČ and Peppol → not ready, same readiness message as a client without them; normal issue, PDF and send unaffected), Tenant → supplier_peppol_participant_id + supplier_dic. Callsites: `PeppolParticipantId::isValid` (App\Rules\PeppolParticipantId::validate + endpoint()), config `efa.dic_endpoint_scheme_id` (rule + endpoint()), `efa.ico_legal_scheme_id` (writeParty CompanyID), `efa.participant_schemes` (rule only). config/efa.php assumptions flagged in-file as unconfirmed with accountant.
- InvoiceService::issue lockForUpdate loop + collision detection vs manual number override (InvoiceIssueData).
- InvoiceService::cancel credit-note generation (negated items + all SK fields copied, own number, two-way linked, **BOTH supplier_signature_media_id + issued_by_name stamped**).
- InvoiceService::issue and cancel **?User $actor parameter signature** (issue called from controller + GenerateRecurringInvoiceJob; cancel from controller).
- InvoiceService::duplicate **must NOT stamp signature_media_id or issued_by_name** (fresh draft resolves live).
- SupplierSignatureResolver: **never calls `current_tenant_id()`** (queue path unbound), fail-closed on tenant_id mismatch + missing file.
- InvoicePdfService **passes signatureDataUri to the single `pdf.invoices.kegos` template** (resolved via ResolvesSupplierSignature::forInvoice), footer via `pdf.invoices.kegos-footer`.
- InvoicePdfService chrome driver setup (config/laravel-pdf.php CHROMIUM_PATH, env LARAVEL_PDF_DRIVER, docker apk packages).
- PayBySquareService null conditions (Issued|Overdue, IBAN, EUR, balance_due > 0).
- InvoiceIssued notification queued discipline (afterCommit, retries, timeout), attachment via contract interface (testable via mock), **renders PDF with signature via SupplierSignatureResolver**.
- DocumentItemService::totals money math (line_base formula, vat_breakdown grouping, rounding modes, balanceDue logic).
- Snapshot timing (create/update write, issue freezes, non-editable invoice pay/cancel unaffected).
- MarkOverdueInvoices cron (withoutGlobalScope TenantScope, per-invoice event dispatch) + test idempotency.
- config/invoicing.php signature block (disk, max_size_kb, allowed_mimes) wired to TemporaryMediaConstraints + settings upload.

**Keywords (SK):** náhľad PDF, krajina, mes., odoslať znova, predmet a text e-mailu, náhľad e-mailu, mobilná faktúra, e-mail príjemcu, náhľad odberateľa, uložiť e-mail, faktúra, návrh, vydaná, splatnosť, overdue, zaplatená, stornovaná, dobropis, číslovanie, DPH, jednotková sadzba, záloha, Pay-by-Square QR, zokrúhlenie, podpis, pečiatka, "vystavil".

### recurring-invoices — Template-driven auto-generation on schedule (Phase 4, 2026-09-06)

**Core:**
- `App\Models\RecurringInvoice` — UUIDv7; traits BelongsToTenant, HasFactory, HasUuids, LogsActivity (logOnlyDirty status/frequency/next_run_at/start_date/occurrences_generated), SoftDeletes. Columns: tenant_id FK, `billed_month` (RecurringBilledMonthEnum none/current/previous, NOT NULL default `none`, 2026-11-05, migration `2026_11_11_000100`), nullable client_id FK (withTrashed), nullable cleaning_object_id FK → objects (withTrashed), name (255), type (InvoiceTypeEnum, immutable after create), template nullable (InvoiceTemplateEnum), frequency (RecurringFrequencyEnum: monthly/every_2_months/quarterly/semi_annually/annually), day_of_month (1–28), status (RecurringInvoiceStatusEnum: active/paused/completed/cancelled), bool auto_issue, **bool auto_send (default false; DTO rule requires auto_issue)**, dates (start_date, end_date nullable), occurrences_limit nullable, occurrences_generated default 0, next_run_at/last_generated_at nullable, **customer snapshot** (customer_name/representative/ico/dic/vat_number/street/city/postal_code/country/email nullable, same as Invoice), **SK fields** (constant_symbol/payment_type/currency/rounding_mode/header_text/footer_text/deposit), due_days (14 default), nullable note, timestamps, soft_deletes. Relations: `client()->withTrashed()`, `cleaningObject()->withTrashed()`, `items(): HasMany<RecurringInvoiceItem>`, `generatedInvoices(): HasMany<Invoice>` (`recurring_invoice_id` FK). Methods: `isRunnable(): bool` (active + today ≤ next_run_at), `hasReachedLimit(): bool`, `hasReachedEndDate(Carbon): bool`.
- `App\Models\RecurringInvoiceItem` — UUIDv7; traits BelongsToTenant, HasFactory, HasUuids. Columns: tenant_id FK, recurring_invoice_id FK (restrictOnDelete), description, qty/unit/unit_price (decimal:10,2), discount_percent (decimal:5,2), vat_rate (decimal:5,2), position. Index recurring_invoice_id.
- `App\Services\RecurringInvoiceService` (final readonly, ctor InvoiceService, DatabaseManager) — `paginate(Request): LengthAwarePaginator<RecurringInvoiceListItemData>` (QueryBuilder AllowedFilter search/name/customer_name/status/frequency/client_id/next_run_at/created_at, default -created_at), `create/update/delete`, `pause/resume/cancel` (state transitions with 422 guards), `generateInvoiceFromTemplate(RecurringInvoice, ?CarbonImmutable $billedMonth = null): ?Invoice` (2026-11-05: billed month = argument, else `billed_month->billedMonth(next_run_at)`; `null` billed month → null periods as before; returns `null` when `(recurring_invoice_id, period_from)` exists incl. soft-deleted; `{mesiac/rok}` → `n/Y` in header, footer, note, item descriptions; build InvoiceUpsertData with per-item vat_rate/discount + SK fields + deposit, call InvoiceService::create, link via recurring_invoice_id, optionally auto-issue per flag or tenant default), `resolveTenantDefaultState(tenantId): RecurringDefaultStateEnum`.
- `App\Jobs\GenerateRecurringInvoiceJob` (ShouldQueue, ShouldBeUnique) — queued from GenerateRecurringInvoices command, `uniqueId() = recurring_id`, `uniqueFor() = 3600`, idempotency guard (isRunnable && next_run_at ≤ today), binds `app()->instance('current_tenant_id')`, transaction: generate via service, (D2b) check `$tenant->missingSupplierFields()` before auto-issue; after a successful auto-issue, **if `auto_send` and customer_email present → `InvoiceService::send` (else Log::warning `recurring_invoice.auto_send.skipped_no_email`)**; — if supplier incomplete + auto_issue=true, skip issue (leave Draft + Log::warning) and advance schedule anyway; else if supplier complete, **call `InvoiceService::issue($invoice, new InvoiceIssueData(number: null), null)` with explicit null actor** (unattended path → issued_by_name = __('app.invoice_issued_by_system'), supplier_signature_media_id = tenant.signature_media_id), advance next_run_at/occurrences_generated/last_generated_at, mark Completed on limit/end-date.
- `App\Console\Commands\GenerateRecurringInvoices` — daily cron, finds Active rows with next_run_at ≤ today, dispatches job per row.

**Satellites (BE):**
- Enums: RecurringFrequencyEnum (monthly/every_2_months/quarterly/semi_annually/annually, `monthsInterval(): int`, `nextRunDate(Carbon $from, int $dayOfMonth): Carbon` — clamps to daysInMonth, startOfDay), RecurringInvoiceStatusEnum (active/paused/completed/cancelled, `isRunnable(): bool`), RecurringDefaultStateEnum (draft/issued), RecurringBilledMonthEnum (2026-11-05, `#[TypeScript]`: none/current/previous; `billedMonth(runDate)` = the month billed, `runDate(billedMonth, day)` = run date of the following month clamped to its length, `TOKEN` `{mesiac/rok}`, `formatMonth` `n/Y`). Form and schedule card show the label (FE RecurringInvoiceForm, RecurringScheduleCard).
- DTOs: `RecurringInvoiceUpsertData` (2026-11-05 `customer_email` rule `Nullable, Email, Max(255)`; name/type/frequency/day_of_month/auto_issue/start_date/end_date/occurrences_limit, customer_* snapshot, SK fields, items, due_days; NO period_from/period_to; `billed_month` (2026-11-05) is the period input, generated invoices get the billed month's period when it is not `none`, null periods otherwise), `RecurringInvoiceItemData` (description/qty/unit/unit_price/discount/vat_rate), `RecurringInvoiceListItemData`, `RecurringInvoiceDetailData` (full + lastGenerated invoice timestamp + generated invoices list). 2026-11-03: `RecurringInvoicePrefillData` (quote_id, client_id, cleaning_object_id, name, frequency, customer_* snapshot, currency, note, items of one billing group only); `RecurringInvoiceItemData.description` max 255.
- Policies: RecurringInvoicePolicy (per PermissionEnum cases).
- Controllers: RecurringInvoiceController (#[NavItem] recurring_invoices; `create()` adds the `prefill` prop from the `quote_id` + `billing_period` query params, 2026-11-03).
- Factories: RecurringInvoiceFactory (states active/paused/completed/cancelled/dueToday), RecurringInvoiceItemFactory.
- i18n keys: nav recurring_invoices; enum labels (frequency, status); flash; errors (termination validation: both end_date + limit, can't pause completed/cancelled, can't resume non-paused).

**FE Satellites:**
- Components: `RecurringStatusBadge`, `RecurringFrequencyBadge`, `RecurringInvoiceForm` (name, subject picker shared with invoices, frequency radio + day-of-month + 3-way termination radio, template, items editor shared, SK fields, deposit), form renders `InvoiceFormSummary` (dates set to start_date + due_days). 2026-11-03: `prefill` prop (`RecurringInvoicePrefillData` from a quote) on `Pages/RecurringInvoices/Create`, passed to the form, which overlays the create defaults, sets `initialMode` (object / client / standalone) and shows an info alert linking back to the quote.
- Pages: `RecurringInvoices/{Index,Create,Edit,Show}`.
- i18n: recurring_* keys (§phase4-invoicing-fe.md).

**Frequency logic:**
- `RecurringFrequencyEnum::monthsInterval()` returns interval (1, 2, 3, 6, 12).
- `RecurringFrequencyEnum::nextRunDate(from, dayOfMonth)` anchors on startOfMonth (month-end clamp via `daysInMonth`, e.g., day 28 in Feb → last day of Feb).

**Lifecycle:**
- Active (scheduled, next_run_at populated, job dispatches at due time).
- Paused (manual pause, next_run_at frozen, resume restarts from last next_run_at).
- Completed (auto on termination limit reached or end_date passed, or manual complete via cancel if reached limit first).
- Cancelled (manual cancel terminal).

**Flow:**
- Create (form) → RecurringInvoiceService::create → Active with computed next_run_at.
- Create from a quote (2026-11-03): GET /recurring-invoices/create?quote_id={uuid}&billing_period=monthly|quarterly|yearly → `RecurringInvoiceController::create` → `QuoteService::recurringInvoicePrefill` (quote read tenant-scoped via findOrFail, `Gate::authorize('view')`; absent or invalid params → prefill null) → form prefilled with that group only → existing store (starts nothing by itself). No backlink column. Guard failures are 422 `status` and redirect back to the quote.
- Daily `app:generate-recurring-invoices` command → finds Active + due → dispatches GenerateRecurringInvoiceJob per row.
- Job (ShouldBeUnique 3600s window) → generateInvoiceFromTemplate (calls InvoiceService::create) → optionally auto-issue per flag or tenant default → links via recurring_invoice_id → advance next_run_at → mark Completed if limit/end-date reached.

- Job duplicate path (2026-11-05): `generateInvoiceFromTemplate` returns null for an existing period → job skips issue and send, still advances `next_run_at`, logs `recurring_invoice.generate.skipped_existing_period`.
- Issue reminder (2026-11-05): daily 12:00 `app:remind-invoice-issue` → `RecurringInvoiceService::sendIssueReminders(today)` → acts only when today = `min(config('invoicing.issue_reminder.day') = 30, daysInMonth)` → per firm with an Active template whose `billed_month` ≠ none → `Notification::send` to users with `edit invoices` (`InvoiceIssueReminder`). Creates and issues nothing.

**Depends on:** invoices (InvoiceService::create/issue; `CustomerParty::forInvoice` for the detail buyer), objects (`cleaningObject.billing_name` for the list display name), quotes (`QuoteService::recurringInvoicePrefill` builds the Create prefill, 2026-11-03), tenancy (tenant_id FK, BelongsToTenant).

**Buyer detail card (2026-11-05):** `RecurringInvoiceDetailData.buyer` (`InvoiceBuyerData`) = `InvoiceBuyerData::fromParty(CustomerParty::forInvoice($ri->cleaningObject, $ri->client, CustomerParty::fromEntered($ri)))`, the same chain as `InvoiceService::buildAttributes` at generation, so the card shows the buyer the next invoice gets. Also `cleaning_object_name`. `RecurringCustomerCard.vue` renders it: source note, missing-e-mail warning (`buyer.email` null; stronger when `auto_send`), edit link by `source` (object billing → `/objects/{id}?edit=billing`, client → `/clients/{id}`, entered → `/recurring-invoices/{id}/edit` when active/paused). `buyer.email` is the template's typed `customer_email` only.

**Buyer (2026-11-01):** the template never snapshots client or house data. Each generated invoice gets its buyer from `InvoiceService::create` at generation time, so an object with billing identity makes the house the buyer with `customer_represented_by` = client name, and Peppol routing follows that buyer. A client-linked template keeps the client's Peppol ID. `customer_display_name` (`RecurringInvoiceListItemData` only, 2026-11-05; removed from `RecurringInvoiceDetailData`, which carries `buyer` instead) = `cleaningObject.billing_name` → `client.name` → `cleaningObject.client.name` → `customer_name`.

**If you change Core, check:**
- RecurringFrequencyEnum nextRunDate month-end clamp (Feb day 28 → last Feb).
- GenerateRecurringInvoiceJob uniqueness window (3600s, idempotency guard on isRunnable + next_run_at ≤ today).
- RecurringInvoiceService generateInvoiceFromTemplate: per-item vat_rate/discount + SK fields snapshot + billed-month periods (null when `none`) + `{mesiac/rok}` replacement + duplicate guard (`periodAlreadyGenerated`) + auto-issue logic. `billed_month` change → `RecurringInvoiceUpsertData`, `RecurringInvoiceDetailData`, `RecurringScheduleCard` label and the import (`LegacyRecurringInvoiceImporter::data`, always previous) (flag vs tenant default).
- Termination logic: end_date vs occurrences_limit (both provided = 422), Completed state, can't pause completed/cancelled.
- `customer_display_name` order in `RecurringInvoiceListItemData` vs the object billing rule; detail buyer resolver is `CustomerParty::forInvoice` with `fromEntered($ri)`, keep it identical to generation (`InvoiceService::buildAttributes`).
- `RecurringInvoiceDetailData` shape: FE reads `buyer.name` (not `customer_display_name`); `RecurringCustomerCard.vue` and `RecurringInvoiceDetailData` tests move together.
- Quote prefill: `RecurringInvoicePrefillData` fields vs the Create form overlay (`RecurringInvoiceForm` prefill prop) and `QuoteService::recurringInvoicePrefill` item mapping (one group only, same description helper as `convertToInvoice`).

**Keywords (SK):** odberateľ pravidelnej faktúry, úprava odberateľa, opakovaná faktúra, harmonogram, frekvencia, výtvorenosť, podmienky ukončenia, automatické vydanie, fakturovaný mesiac, upomienka na vydanie.

### settings-invoicing — Tenant supplier data + invoice defaults (Phase 4, 2026-09-06)

**Core:**
- `App\Models\TenantInterface` (existing, extended in Phase 4) — adds columns to phase 2 baseline: `recurring_default_state` (RecurringDefaultStateEnum, default 'draft'), `default_constant_symbol` (10 chars nullable), `default_payment_type` (PaymentTypeEnum, default 'transfer'), `default_currency` (CurrencyEnum, default 'EUR'), `default_rounding_mode` (RoundingModeEnum, default 'none'). Extended logOnly with these fields. `invoice_template` was dropped by migration `2026_11_03_000100_drop_invoice_template_columns` (forward `php artisan migrate`, never `migrate:fresh`). `default_quote_included_text` (text nullable, max 2000; migration `2026_11_09_000200_add_quote_settings_to_tenants`; prefills new quotes). Later additions: `default_header_text`/`default_footer_text` (text nullable, 2026-09-20), **`invoice_mail_subject` (255) / `invoice_mail_body` (text) nullable templates with `{{invoice.*}}` tokens (2026-10-12), consumed by `Notifications\InvoiceIssued` through PlaceholderResolverService::invoiceMailVariables; blank → built-in lang subject/view**.
- `App\Models\Tenant` (extended) — adds supplier columns: `dic` (32 nullable), `vat_number` (32 nullable), `address_line` (255 nullable), `city` (255 nullable), `postal_code` (16 nullable), `country` (2 nullable), `contact_email` (255 nullable), `contact_phone` (30 nullable), `swift_bic` (11 nullable, SWIFT-BIC format), **`peppol_participant_id` (64 nullable; supplier Peppol id, snapshot onto invoices.supplier_peppol_participant_id)**. `signing_place` (varchar 100 nullable, 2026-11-03; full phrase such as "V Bratislave" or "Vo Zvolene", printed as "{signing_place}, dňa {date}"; empty → "{city}, dňa {date}"; no city → "Dňa {date}"). Extended logOnly + @property docblocks. Existing: `name` (255), `ico` (20 nullable), `is_vat_payer` (bool), `iban` (34 nullable), `invoice_number_format` (255, default 'FA-{YYYY}-{XXXX}'), `registration_info` (text nullable), `vat_rate` (decimal:5,2).
- `App\Services\InvoiceSettingsService` (final readonly, ctor TemporaryUploadService) — 2026-11-05 `fillBlanks(Tenant $target, TenantSupplierProfileData $profile, ?Tenant $source): void` (fills only blank supplier columns address/city/PSČ/DIČ/IBAN and, from the source, contact e-mail/phone, representative, signing place and interface texts + mail templates; writes via `update`, so activity logs) and `copyMissingMedia(Tenant $source, Tenant $target): void` (copies the signature via Spatie Media copy when the target has none; sets `signature_media_id`; the `logo` copy was removed 2026-11-05). `update(Tenant, InvoiceSettingsData, ?User $actor, string $sessionId): void` (transaction: write Tenant supplier + invoicing fields, write TenantInterface invoice defaults + recurring defaults + default header/footer + invoice_mail_subject/body (blank → null) + peppol_participant_id; **if $data->signature_uuid: moveToModel($tenant, 'signature', $uuid, $actor, $sessionId) then update signature_media_id; else if $data->remove_signature: update signature_media_id = null**).

**Satellites (BE):**
- `InvoiceSettingsService::adoptNumberFormat(Tenant, string): bool` (2026-11-05) — sets `invoice_number_format` only while it still equals the default; writes through `update` (activity log). Caller: LegacyRecurringInvoiceImporter. A custom format is kept and reported (`number_format kept`).
- DTOs: `InvoiceSettingsData` (supplier: name/ico/dic/vat_number/is_vat_payer/address_line/city/postal_code/country/contact_email/contact_phone; invoicing: invoice_number_format/iban/swift_bic/vat_rate/registration_info; recurring: recurring_default_state; defaults: default_constant_symbol/default_payment_type/default_currency/default_rounding_mode, **default_header_text/default_footer_text (nullable text, phase 10)**; **signature: ?string $signature_uuid, bool $remove_signature = false** mutually exclusive `prohibited_if` validation). Rules: country 2 chars, IBAN format, SWIFT-BIC format, number format with {X+} placeholder validation, constant symbol digits only, vat_rate 0–100, **signature constraints (mime/size) injected from config/invoicing.php**, default text fields max 5000 chars. 2026-11-03 quote settings: `default_quote_included_text` (nullable, max 2000), `signing_place` (nullable, max 100). 2026-11-05: firm logo fields and DTOs removed (`logo_uuid`, `remove_logo`, `TenantLogoData`, `TenantLogoConstraintsData`).
- Policies: no policy (owner-only gate via ManageBillingSettings permission).
- Controllers: `InvoiceSettingsController` (final readonly, ctor InvoiceSettingsService) — `#[Authorize(ManageBillingSettings)]` on show/update/signature, `#[NavItem(invoicing_settings, ManageBillingSettings, settings group)]`. Routes: GET /settings/invoicing → InvoiceSettingsData::fromTenant(current_tenant) + **TenantSignatureData::fromTenant($tenant) if present** + **InvoiceSignatureConstraintsData::fromConfig()**; PUT /settings/invoicing (Precognition) → InvoiceSettingsService::update($tenant, $data, $request->user(), $request->session()->getId()); **GET /settings/invoicing/signature** (streams file inline, #[Authorize(ManageBillingSettings)], 404 if absent or file missing).

**FE Satellites:**
- Pages: `Settings/Invoicing` (supplier section, bank section, number format preset/custom, template picker, defaults, recurring state).
- Components: `InvoiceSettingsSupplierCard`, `InvoiceSettingsBankCard`, `InvoiceNumberFormatField` (presets + custom with live example), `InvoiceSettingsPreviewCard` + `InvoicePreviewFrame` (preview iframe; picker/thumbnail removed), `InvoiceSettingsDefaultsCard`, **`InvoiceSettingsSignatureCard` (delegates to `SettingsImageField`, 2026-11-03) + `SettingsImagePreview` (renamed from `InvoiceSignaturePreview`; plain img, not iframe)**, `SettingsImageField` (shared 4-state image body: empty / saved / staged upload / staged removal; props image, constraints, intent, error, labels; emits update:intent), **`InvoiceSettingsQuoteCard`** (page only: default included text, signing place; logo block removed 2026-11-05). `signatureIntent.ts` renamed `imageIntent.ts` (`ImageIntent`, `imageIntentToFields`, `fieldsToImageIntent`).
- i18n: settings keys (§phase4-invoicing-fe.md).

**Defaults propagation:**
- New Invoice form: `InvoiceFormContextData::fromTenant` loads interface.default_* → `context.defaults` → form initializes SK fields + deposit from defaults.
- New RecurringInvoice form: same propagation + `context.recurring_default_state` used in hint.
- Settings page: GET shows current values (fromTenant), PUT persists changes. Next invoice forms receive updated defaults.

**Number format:**
- Presets: `FA-{YYYY}-{XXXX}`, `{YYYY}{XXXX}`, `{YYYY}/{XXX}`, `{YY}{MM}{XXX}`.
- Custom: regex `{X+}` placeholder (required, caught by validation).
- Placeholder substitution in `InvoiceNumberService::next`: `{YYYY}` → year, `{YY}` → last 2 digits, `{MM}` → month zero-padded, `{X+}` → sequence number zero-padded to length of X run.
- InvoiceTemplatePicker removed; live preview is InvoiceSettingsPreviewCard (GET /settings/invoicing/preview, no `{template}`).

**Depends on:** tenancy (Tenant/TenantInterface models), invoices (defaults used in forms).

**If you change Core, check:**
- `fillBlanks` / `copyMissingMedia` (2026-11-05): fill rule = write only null or blank target values (`onlyBlankTargets`); a filled target field must never change. Callers: LegacyImportService only. `copyMissingMedia` must run inside `ToolContext::run` for the target (Media takes `tenant_id` from `current_tenant_id()`) and outside any transaction.
- TenantInterface logOnly: fields added (recurring_default_state, default_*).
- Tenant logOnly: 11 new supplier fields + existing (name, ico, is_vat_payer, iban, invoice_number_format, registration_info, vat_rate, **signature_media_id**).
- InvoiceSettingsService::update: transaction scope, Tenant + TenantInterface both updated, **TemporaryUploadService::moveToModel called when signature_uuid present**. 2026-11-03: `signing_place` and `default_quote_included_text` are plain writes (Tenant / TenantInterface). The logo write path was removed 2026-11-05.
- InvoiceSettingsController::show: returns **TenantSignatureData + InvoiceSignatureConstraintsData** in props (FE card setup); no logo props since 2026-11-05 (were `logo`, `logo_constraints` from 2026-11-03).
- InvoiceSettingsController::signature: reads current tenant's signatureMedia directly, streams the file inline for GET.
- `InvoiceSettingsService::adoptNumberFormat` (2026-11-05): "default" = `config('invoicing.default_number_format')`. The importer's target `LegacyRecurringInvoiceImporter::LEGACY_NUMBER_FORMAT` (`{YYYY}{XXXX}`) is a separate constant; change both if the target format changes.
- Number format validation regex (must include {X+}).
- Defaults propagation in InvoiceFormContextData::fromTenant (stale defaults if not re-fetched on page load).
- config/invoicing.php signature block (disk, max_size_kb, allowed_mimes) consistency with TemporaryMediaConstraints validation rule.

**Keywords (SK):** nastavenia, dodávateľ, číslovanie, šablóna, SK polia, záloha, podpis, pečiatka, "nahradiť", "odstrániť".

### BE ↔ FE API (Sanctum, routes/api.php) — Phase 2 tenancy + /api/me

**Web SPA (Inertia) note:**
- No Sanctum on web SPA — routes/web.php uses session auth only.
- API routes (routes/api.php) reserved for mobile/partner integrations (bearer tokens).

**API endpoints (Sanctum Bearer tokens, auth:sanctum):**
- POST /api/auth/login (guest, throttle mobile-login) → LoginData {email, password, remember} → AuthTokenData {token, refresh_token, user: UserListItemData}.
- POST /api/auth/refresh (guest, throttle mobile-token) → RefreshTokenData {refresh_token} → MobileTokenPairData {token, refresh_token} | 401 {message}. Only 401 means the session is dead; 403, 422, 429, 5xx and network keep the tokens.
- POST /api/auth/revoke (guest, throttle mobile-token) → RefreshTokenData → 204.
- POST /api/auth/logout (auth:sanctum) → 204. Bearer callers only (MCP); mobile does not call it.
- **GET /api/me (auth:sanctum + tenant.context + tenant.required)** — returns MeData {userId, activeTenantId, permissions: PermissionEnum[]}} (Phase 2 new; mobile app capabilities polling path).
- GET|PUT /api/profile (auth:sanctum + tenant.context + tenant.required) → UserListItemData / 204.
- PUT /api/profile/password (auth:sanctum + tenant.context + tenant.required) → 204; logs out the other mobile sessions, the calling phone stays.
- GET /api/users (auth:sanctum + tenant.context + tenant.required) → Paginator<UserListItemData> (member-only, tenant-scoped).
- GET|PUT|DELETE /api/users/{user} (auth:sanctum + tenant.context + tenant.required) → UserListItemData / 204.
- Precognition on all POST|PUT mutations (HandlePrecognitiveRequests middleware).

**On port:**
- Phase 5: POST /api/notifications/bell (auth:sanctum + tenant.required) for 60s polling (notifications).
- Phase 3+: /api/clients*, /api/objects*, /api/quotes*, /api/invoices*, /api/contracts* mirrors web routes.

### BE ↔ FE Enums — Phase 2 PermissionEnum + tenancy enums

**Generated from #[TypeScript] → App.Enums.* union in resources/js/types/generated.d.ts:**
- PermissionEnum (67 backed string cases, grouped by resource: employees/roles/audit_logs/api_docs/media/billing + 9 groups phase 3+) — methods: label(), group(), groupLabel(), sharedKey() (Str::camel), values(). Labels NOT shipped in generated.d.ts (FE uses t() for localized labels from app.json; BE Spatie Permission stores value).
- TenantColorEnum (8 backed hex cases: a16207, ea580c, ca8a04, eab308, ca8a04, 16a34a, 0891b2, 0284c7) — methods: label(), options(). Labels translated via t('app.tenant_color_<hex>').
- InvitationStatusEnum (pending/accepted/revoked/expired) — label().
- InvitationAcceptStateEnum (expired/wrong_user/existing_user/new_user) — FE-only states for Accept page rendering (not persisted; 4-way dispatch from backend hints).
- SupportedLanguage (sk/en/uk) — methods: getDefault(), getDisplayName(), getForLanguageSwitcher(), isSupported(), getCodes(). Labels and flags bundled.

**Phase 3+ on port:**
- JobStatusEnum, JobTypeEnum, TaskFrequencyEnum (schedule).
- ObjectTypeEnum, ClientTypeEnum (clients/objects).
- QuoteKindEnum, ContractCategoryEnum, ContractTermTypeEnum, PaymentTypeEnum, RoundingModeEnum, EmploymentContractTypeEnum, InvoiceStatusEnum, RecurringInvoiceFrequencyEnum, etc.

**FE pattern:**
- Never destructure enum values; always use App.Enums.PermissionEnum for type-safe checks.
- useAuthorization().allows(PermissionEnum.ViewClients) → read page.props.can[permissionKey(value)].

### FE Authorization — Phase 2 useAuthorization + Can component

**Derived from PermissionEnum:**
- BE: HandleInertiaRequests.share builds can: `collect(PermissionEnum::cases())->mapWithKeys(fn ($p) => [$p->sharedKey() => $user->can($p->value)])`.
- FE: useAuthorization().allows(App.Enums.PermissionEnum.ViewClients) → permissionKey('view clients') → 'viewClients' → page.props.can['viewClients'] === true.

**Guard component:**
- Components/Can.vue (required permission: App.Enums.PermissionEnum prop) — `<slot v-if="allows(...)" /><slot v-else name="fallback" />`.
- Never use $page.props.can.viewUsers directly (string keys, no type safety). Always use useAuthorization().allows(enum) or <Can> component.

**BE-filtered navigation:**
- NavigationRegistry discovers #[NavItem] on controllers with permission: PermissionEnum::*->value.
- Visible in nav only if user->can(permission).
- FE renders from shared navigation (filtered by BE, no client-side re-filtering needed).

**No role checks (mandate):**
- Neither RBAC check ($user->hasRole) nor role-based rendering.
- Only permission-based authorization (single axis: user/permission).

### BE ↔ FE Notifications bell (web vs. API) — Phase 8

**Web route (session auth, browser poll):**
- `GET /notifications/bell` (web route, auth + tenant.required middleware) → `NotificationController::bell()` → `NotificationService::bell(User, tenantId): NotificationBellData`
- Returns JSON `{ unread_count: number, recent: NotificationListItemData[] }` (5 latest, including read).
- FE `useNotificationBell` composable polls every 60s, ref-counted subscribers (only one poller per tab), stops on 401/403/419 (auth loss), pauses when tab hidden (battery optimization).

**API route (Sanctum, header-based tenant):**
- `GET /api/notifications/bell` (Sanctum Bearer token, no session; X-Tenant-Id header required) → `Api\NotificationBellController` (invokable) → same service method.
- Returns same NotificationBellData JSON.
- Reserved for mobile phase 9 (Capacitor app polling via Bearer token + tenant header).
- `/api/*` NOT stateful: browser cookies do NOT authenticate (no `statefulApi()` in bootstrap/app.php). Reasons: (a) SPA uses session for web routes only, (b) mobile uses Bearer tokens. Dual routes eliminate need for statefulApi() CSRF overhead.

**DTO contract:**
- `NotificationBellData (unread_count, recent: NotificationListItemData[])`
- `NotificationListItemData (id, type: NotificationTypeEnum, title, body, url, read_at, created_at)` — timestamps ISO-8601, url can be null.

### BE ↔ mobile API (JSON, Sanctum) — Phase 11
- 2026-11-03 quotes (additive): `QuoteListItemData.period_totals`, `QuoteDetailData.period_totals`, `QuoteItemData.billing_period` (see Mobile API surface).
- 2026-11-06 invoices (mobile issue and send; additive, no migration, no new permission): `GET /api/invoices/stats` → `InvoiceStatsData` (active company). `POST /api/invoices/{id}/issue` body `{}` or `{number?}` → 200 `InvoiceDetailData`; 422 `status` / `supplier` / `number`. `GET /api/invoices/{id}/send-draft` → 200 `InvoiceSendDraftData` {recipient_email, email_options, save_target, subject, body (plain text, `\n` breaks), attachment_name}; 422 `status`. `POST /api/invoices/{id}/send` body `InvoiceSendData` {recipient_email?, save_for_future, subject? (max 255, one line), body? (max 5000)} → 202 `InvoiceDetailData`; 422 `status` / `recipient_email` / `save_for_future` / `subject` / `body`; 429 on the 11th call per minute. `InvoiceDetailData` gains `tenant_id`, `can_be_issued` (Draft and complete supplier), `can_be_sent` (`canBeSent()`); `InvoiceListItemData.sent_at` (ISO 8601 or null). Permissions: issue / send / send-draft need `edit invoices`; stats / list / detail `view invoices`. Scribe: `Api\InvoiceController` attributes.

- Responses built from Spatie Data (`toArray`); `MeData` carries `companies: list<MobileCompanyData>` (tenant_id, name, color, mobile_access, permissions).
- Assistant reply: `AssistantReplyData` {reply, actions: AssistantActionData[] {type, label, url}, pending_action: PendingActionData|null}. Pending action: `PendingActionData` {id, action, status, preview, tenant_id, tenant_name, subject_label, customer, total, email, expires_at, confirm_in_app_only}. Confirm result: `ActionResultData` {action, subject_label, url, message}.
- Push deep links: `PushRoute` allowlist (`/tasks/{uuid}`, `/invoices/{uuid}`, `/quotes/{uuid}`, `/expenses/{uuid}`, `/pending-actions`), else `/notifications`.
- `GET /api/dashboard` (Api\DashboardController, `#[ResponseFromSpatieData(DashboardData::class)]`) returns the same `DashboardData` that DashboardService builds for the web dashboard.
- Task attachments: `TaskAttachmentListData` {attachments: TaskAttachmentData[], can_add, constraints: TaskAttachmentConstraintsData}; `TaskAttachmentData` {id (media uuid), name, mime_type, size, previewable, uploaded_by_name, created_at, download_url (relative path, route `api.tasks.attachments.show`), can_delete}. POST 201 returns `TaskAttachmentData`; DELETE 204; download is a stream, not JSON. Names and URLs are never sent to MCP or the assistant (only `attachments_count` in TaskDetailData).

### BE ↔ FCM payload — Phase 11

- `PushMessage` {title, body, route, tenantId, notificationId, badge}. Sent as multicast per user's device tokens; tokens reported unusable are deleted from `device_tokens`.

### Task thread (comments and AI runs) — web Inertia + mobile JSON (2026-11-01)

- `TaskThreadData` {comments: TaskCommentData[] (oldest first, ≤ 200, filtered per viewer), latest_run: TaskRunData | null, question: TaskQuestionData | null, pending_actions: PendingActionData[] (viewer's own, pending, unexpired, linked to runs of this task), ai_available, can_comment, can_start_run, run_companies: TaskRunCompanyData[] (personal task only), max_body_chars}. Built only by TaskThreadService::for. Web `Tasks/Show` receives it as prop `thread`; the same JSON is returned by GET /tasks/{task}/thread for polling.
- `TaskRunData` {id, status: TaskRunStatusEnum, is_active, closed_task, started_by_name, tenant_id, tenant_name, finished_at, actions: AssistantActionData[]}. `TaskRunStatusData` {latest_run_id, status, is_active} is the light poll response of GET /tasks/{task}/ai-run-status.
- `TaskCommentData` {id, author_kind: TaskCommentAuthorEnum, author_name (for `ai`: the starter's name), body (plain text, never HTML), created_at, is_mine, can_delete, answers_comment_id (null when the viewer cannot see the question)}. No `task_run_id` in the DTO.
- `TaskQuestionData` {comment_id, answered, answer_comment_id, can_answer}.
- `TaskListItemData` gains `ai_run_status: TaskRunStatusEnum | null` (null outside the audience).
- Inputs: TaskCommentStoreData {body, answers_comment_id?}, TaskRunStartData {company_id?} (required on a personal task, ignored on a firm task).
- Errors: 422 keys `status` (second active run, closed task, question no longer open, live pending action of the starter), `ai` (no key, `errors.ai`), `company_id`, `answers_comment_id`, `body`; 403 no run permission or company not allowed; 404 for a comment the viewer cannot see.
- Mobile types: `src/types/api.generated.d.ts` from `pnpm api-types:export`; the web `resources/js/types/generated.d.ts` by `php artisan typescript:transform`. Both carry these DTOs once the export runs.

### Task board and statuses — BE ↔ web and mobile (2026-11-06)

- Status write: `POST /api/tasks/{task}/status` and web `POST /tasks/{task}/status` take `TaskStatusChangeData` {status: TaskStatusEnum} (5 values; `cancelled` is gone). API returns `TaskDetailData`; web redirects back with flash `task_status_changed`. Same status is a no-op (no activity row).
- Board: `GET /api/tasks/board?scope=` returns `TaskBoardData` {columns: `TaskBoardColumnData`[]} with exactly 5 columns in `TaskStatusEnum::boardOrder()` order; each column {status, total (all matching rows), tasks (≤ 50, `TaskListItemData`), has_more}. Web `GET /tasks?view=board` passes the same shape as prop `board` (`tasks` is then null); `view=table` passes `tasks` (paginator) and `board` null. The board ignores `filter[status]`, `sort`, `page`, `per_page`.
- Card fields on `TaskListItemData` (list, board, MCP `list_tasks`): `comments_count` (human comments only), `attachments_count`, `can_update` (use it for the status menu; never recompute it client-side), `is_active` (the unfinished set is the server's; no client copy). `TaskDetailData.is_active` likewise.
- Create: `POST /api/tasks` and web store accept optional `status` (In activeValues). A `done` value → 422 `status`. The create drawer / sheet sends it only on create.
- Filters (list and board, same names): `filter[status]` comma list (table only), `filter[assignee]` = `me|none|<membership uuid>`, `filter[due]` = `overdue|today|week|none`, `sort=priority`. `assigned_membership_id` remains for MCP and old clients. Unknown filter values are ignored (`me|none|uuid` only).
- Errors: 422 `status` for complete on a done task (`task_not_open`) and reopen on an unfinished one (`task_not_closed`), and for an AI run / answer on a done task (`task_not_open_for_run` / `task_not_open_for_answer`, texts reworded to "unfinished"). 403 without `update` (status) or `view` (board); board across companies needs `viewAnyAcrossCompanies`.
- Old endpoints (`complete`, `reopen`, `filter[status]=open`) keep their shape. `filter[status]=cancelled` returns an empty list, not an error. No shim for old mobile builds: the app ships with the server.

### Task reminder slots — BE ↔ web and mobile (2026-11-04)

- Write: `TaskUpsertData` {`remind_on`: `Y-m-d` | null, `remind_slot`: `morning|noon|evening` | null} as a pair; `remind_at` (ISO, legacy) only without a slot (`prohibited` with a slot). A pair with one side missing → 422 on the missing field. Null pair = no reminder.
- Read: `TaskDetailData` {`remind_at` (display, UTC ISO), `remind_on`, `remind_slot`}; the pair is derived from the stored `remind_at` (`dateAndSlotFor`), so the client never computes timezones.
- Form context (web Inertia prop `context` and `GET /api/tasks/form-context`): `reminder_slots` = list of {`slot`, `hour`} in case order (7, 12, 19); `default_remind_on`, `default_remind_slot` = first slot strictly after now.
- Web and mobile send the pair with `remind_at` null or absent; an unchanged web reminder sends `{remind_at: stored}` instead (same-minute rule keeps it).

### audit-log revert — BE ↔ FE props (2026-11-03)

- `ActivityLogListItemData` (Index rows) = `{id, description, log_name, causer_name, causer_email, subject_type, subject_id, subject_label, event, created_at, revert: ActivityRevertData}`. `revert` on the list is the quick check: no conflict, later-change or batch-member checks. Those surface as 422 on POST.
- `ActivityLogDetailData` (Show) = list fields + `properties`, `attribute_changes` (raw, collapsed in the UI) + `revert` (full check) + `changes: ActivityRevertChangeData[]`.
- `ActivityRevertData` = `{status: available|unavailable|reverted, reason: ActivityRevertBlockReasonEnum | null (null iff available), changes_count, batch_size, blocking_activity_id, reverted_at, reverted_by_name, reverted_by_activity_id, reverts_activity_id}`. `reverted` wins over every other status.
- `ActivityRevertChangeData` = `{field, label, old, new, current, conflict, masked}`. `old` null for created / restored rows, `new` null for deleted rows, `current` null when the subject is missing or masked, `masked` → old / new / current all null.
- POST `/audit-logs/{activity}/revert` (no body) → 302 back with flash `app.audit_log_reverted`. 422 on key `status` with the reason label. 403 on the policy / record ability failure. 422 `unique_conflict` on a unique index collision.
- Props: `AuditLogs/Index {activities: Paginator<ActivityLogListItemData>, filters}`; `AuditLogs/Show {activity: ActivityLogDetailData}`.
- FE props: `ActivityRevertStatus {revert}`, no emits. `ActivityChangesDiff {changes, event}`, no emits. `ActivityRevertCard {revert, changes}`, emits `revert: []`, uses `useAuthorization().allows('revert audit logs')`.
- FE confirm: `useActivityRevertConfirm()` wraps `useDeleteConfirm` (POST `/audit-logs/{id}/revert`); the description uses `revert.changes_count` and `batch_size`. Index and Show share it.
- Generated TS: `resources/js/types/generated.d.ts` (ActivityRevertData, ActivityRevertChangeData, both enums, `PermissionEnum` `'revert audit logs'`). Mobile types via `pnpm api-types:export` carry the same DTOs once the export runs.

### legacy payload ↔ importer (encrypted seeder, 2026-11-05)

- Input: decrypted JSON object. Required arrays: `firms`, `clients`, `firm_clients_2026`, `monthly_templates`. Optional: `manual_recurring` (list; absent = none), `last_invoice_number_2026` (`{"<legacy firm id>": "YYYYNNNN"}`), `object_postal_codes` (`{"<object name>": "NNN NN"}`).
- `monthly_templates[*].last_billed_month`: `"YYYY-MM"`, the last month legacy invoiced for that template. Missing or invalid → recurring row `skipped` (`no last_billed_month`); the object is still imported.
- `manual_recurring[*]`: `id` (legacy invoice id, report reference), `firm`, `client`, `name` (= object name = recurring name), `object_type` (ObjectTypeEnum value), `header1..header4`, `above`, `services`, `frequency` (`monthly` | `every_2_months`), `last_billed_month`, `items[]` (`name`, `quantity`, `price`). `client` must be a client imported for that firm.
- Invariant: header lines never provide a postal code or city.
- Output (2026-11-06): the importer sets recurring item `unit` to `mes.` for every row (constant, not read from the payload).

### buyer preview, recurring buyer card and invoice send — BE ↔ FE (2026-11-05)

- 2026-11-06: `InvoiceSendData` gains optional `subject` / `body`; the web send modal still posts only `recipient_email` + `save_for_future`, so a web send uses the firm template (no web editor). `InvoiceActionsCard` shows the send block for overdue too.

- `GET /invoices/buyer-preview?client_id=&cleaning_object_id=` (empty string = no object) → `InvoiceBuyerPreviewResultData {buyer: InvoiceBuyerData, email_options: string[]}`. 422 on `client_id` / `cleaning_object_id` (company-scoped, live rows, object belongs to client); 403 without create/edit invoices and create/edit recurring invoices. FE: `useBuyerPreview` (pattern of `useDocumentTemplatePreview`, AbortController, 250 ms debounce).
- `RecurringInvoiceDetailData {buyer: InvoiceBuyerData, cleaning_object_name}`; `customer_display_name` absent from detail. Consumer: `RecurringCustomerCard.vue`.
- `Invoices/Show` props: `sendOptions: InvoiceSendOptionsData | null` (null unless Issued, no customer_email, actor may send). `InvoiceActionsCard` prop `recipientChoosable` = `sendOptions !== null`.
- `POST /invoices/{invoice}/send` body `InvoiceSendData {recipient_email: string | null, save_for_future: boolean}`; the modal `InvoiceSendModal` uses `useForm('post', …)`, transforms `recipient_email || null`; 422 keys `recipient_email`, `save_for_future`, `status`.
- `TextInput` prop `suggestions?: readonly string[]` → native `<datalist>`; free typing kept; no preselected value.
- Object side: `ObjectBillingData.email`, `ObjectUpsertData.billing_email`, `Pages/Objects/Show` query `?edit=billing`.

### invoice preview — BE ↔ FE (2026-11-06)

- `GET /invoices/{invoice}/preview` (`invoices.preview`, `#[Authorize('downloadPdf', 'invoice')]`, throttle `document-preview`) → `application/pdf`, `Content-Disposition: inline`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, filename `pdfFilenameBase()`. The download route `GET /invoices/{invoice}/pdf` shares the same response helper (attachment disposition).
- Web consumer: `utils/http.ts` `getBlob` (throws `HttpError` with the server message on a JSON error, and on a 200 that is not `application/pdf`) → `useDocumentPdfPreview({ load })` → `DocumentPdfPreviewModal`.
- Mobile consumer: `GET /api/invoices/{id}/pdf` (existing download endpoint, blob with bearer); the API has no inline preview route.

## Gotchas

### Backend (updated Phase 2: 8 resolved, 18 active/new)

1. ~~Spatie Permission teams OFF~~ — **FIXED 2026-09-06:** teams=true, team_foreign_key='tenant_id' (uuid columns). RoleTemplatesSeeder::seedForTenant(Tenant) creates role bundles per tenant. setPermissionsTeamId discipline enforced in TenantContextMiddleware, RegistrationService, InvitationAcceptService, UserService, tests/TestCase::actingAsTenantUser, every job/command. `setPermissionsTeamId` NOT called before role/permission checks in D6 console contexts = intentional (commands/seeders run unbound on purpose; bind explicitly when needed).

2. ~~Sanctum personal_access_tokens.tokenable_id is bigint (morphs) vs users.id uuid~~ — **FIXED 2026-09-06:** uuidMorphs('tokenable') applied. Tests run on Postgres.

3. ~~activity_log morph columns uuid~~ — **FIXED 2026-09-06:** D1 decision: TenantInterface UUID (uuid PK, LogsActivity on it). App\Models\Activity (D2) custom hook, tenant_id nullable. uuid morphs stay (nullableUuidMorphs). Vendor Media (bigint id) must never be logged (gotcha 3-old: moot now — Media has no LogsActivity, only on domain models).

4. ~~No PermissionEnum~~ — **FIXED 2026-09-06:** App\Enums\PermissionEnum (53 backed string cases, #[TypeScript], methods label/group/groupLabel/sharedKey/values). PermissionSeeder seeds global catalogue (not per-tenant; roles are). RoleTemplatesSeeder uses enum values. HandleInertiaRequests builds can from enum. Policies use enum values. FE useAuthorization().allows(enum) + permissionKey(value) → Str::camel mapping. Tests (CreatesUsers, etc.) call setPermissionsTeamId before role checks.

5. ~~PermissionMiddleware vs #[Authorize]~~ — **CLARIFIED 2026-09-06:** keep permission: middleware ONLY for /docs* (non-model route); all model CRUD use #[Authorize] attribute (RBAC-full profile). Routes middleware group: POST /logout, then tenant.required gates all app/* except logout; api/* gates auth:sanctum + tenant.context + tenant.required.

6. ~~is_active not enforced at login~~ — **FIXED 2026-09-06:** D4 guards: AuthController/Api/AuthController login includes `is_active => true` in Auth::attempt (failure = bad credentials + fires Failed audit) + `hasActiveMembership()` check (none = ValidationException 'no_active_tenant' indistinguishable from membership loss). RequireActiveTenant middleware ensures bound tenant on all authenticated HTTP requests (web logout + redirect / API 403). Mid-session membership deactivation = next request logs out (transparent).

7. ~~config/media-urls.php:5,16 references non-existent App\Models\EmailTemplate~~ — Still open (not a tenancy blocker; phase 3+ will address).

8. ~~Auth-event audit covers 3 of 7 events; request()->ip() used directly~~ — **FIXED 2026-09-06:** LogAuthenticationActivity covers login, logout, login_failed, password_reset, password_reset_link_sent, login_lockout and password_changed since 2026-11-04; `attempt` and `verified` from the 7-event plan are still not logged; get_client_ip() helper in app/Support/helpers.php (reachable IP-proxy aware per laravel-13-conventions/references/rate-limiting.md). 7-event full audit (attempt, throttled, verified, failed, login, logout, logout, other) deferred post-bootstrap (gotcha-noted for clarity).

9. ~~No rate limiters on login / forgot / reset / api login~~ — **FIXED 2026-11-04:** web login, forgot-password and reset-password have named limiters (see auth Core); API login keeps `throttle:mobile-login`. Invitation accept: `throttle:invitation-accept` (5/min per IP).

10. ~~UserSeeder seeds admin@example.com/password + 5 faker users~~ — **FIXED 2026-09-06:** D3: UserSeeder calls RegistrationService::createOwner('Admin', 'admin@example.com', 'password', 'Demo Cleaning s.r.o.', '12345678'), creating canonical User → Tenant → TenantMembership → Admin role assignment. No 5 faker users (spec chose "no demo accounts"). Bootstrap path = `php artisan app:create-owner` (interactive or flag-based).

11. ~~Dead code: DTOs UserIndexFilterData, RoleIndexFilterData, ActivityLogIndexFilterData, LanguageSwitchData~~ — **FIXED 2026-09-06:** OwnedTemporaryMedia has no callers yet (phase 3+ will consume via Quote document upload, Invoice PDF, etc.; by design, noted). AllowedFilter.php fixed (2026-09-06 prior). Dead DTOs removed from phase 2 codebase.

12. ~~Roles/Form permissions prop is a bare grouped array (TS any)~~ — **FIXED 2026-09-06:** PermissionGroupData (#[TypeScript], id/group/group_label/permissions: PermissionItemData[]) types form prop.

13. ~~Temporary upload response is a bare array~~ — Still a bare array (not a DTO; API response minimalism rule). FileUploadInput.vue:41 hard-codes POST /uploads (intentional single endpoint).

14. ~~SupportedLanguage only sk/en~~ — **FIXED 2026-09-06:** Added uk (Ukrainian). Translations JSON resources/lang/{sk,en,uk}/app.json + {sk,en,uk}/validation.json. Flat snake_case keys (vue-i18n legacy:false compatible). FE app.ts imports all three; AppLayout language dropdown renders all three.

15. db:table artisan fails in container (intl missing) — use `docker compose exec postgres psql` directly.

16. ~~No morph map~~ — **FIXED 2026-09-06:** AppServiceProvider.php: `Relation::morphMap(['tenant_membership' => TenantMembership::class])` (current only consumer; enforceMorphMap deferred when all models mapped).

17. ~~No preventLazyLoading, no SoftDeletes~~ — **FIXED 2026-09-06:** Domain models on phase 2 port (Tenant, TenantMembership, TenantInterface, TenantInvitation + existing User, Role, Activity, Media, TemporaryUpload) have: SoftDeletes where appropriate (all except User/TenantMembership which use is_active flag or are pivots), BelongsToTenant where appropriate (all except User, Tenant, TenantMembership, TenantInterface, Role, Permission, Activity), TenantScope global scope. Phase 3+ models inherit same pattern. preventLazyLoading deferred (no prod user data yet; enable when customers live).

18. PHPStan level max against `phpstan-baseline.neon` (188 entries, regenerated 2026-09-06 after tenancy fixes). Run with `--memory-limit=1G` to avoid OOM. Baseline burn-down deferred Phase 3+ (targets: test closures, AllowedFilter generics, QueryBuilder chains, Scribe strategies).

19. `compose.yml` injects `DB_CONNECTION=pgsql`, `DB_DATABASE=cleanmaster_admin` into container. PHPUnit `<env>` blocks need `force="true"` to override compose env (phpdotenv reads `$_SERVER`). Testing DB `cleanmaster_admin_testing` auto-created by docker/postgres/init-testing-db.sql on first volume init.

20. **NEW (Phase 2):** D8 FK restrictOnDelete (no cascades). Tenants are soft-deleted, so cascades never fire anyway. Owners/tenants with historical data cannot be deleted (deactivate instead). Explicit guard: UserService::delete asserts not owner; TenantMembership::delete keeps User row.

21. **NEW (Phase 2):** D10 invitation token 64-char random, plaintext unique index, 7d expiry, single-use. Hashing deferred (low-risk internal tool, noted for future hardening).

22. **NEW (Phase 2):** Inertia shared props typing (D1): single `SharedProps` interface in types/index.d.ts (collapsed from two blocks). Import-free global augmentation — no types/inertia.d.ts file. FE reads via usePageProps() ComputedRef, never destructure at setup (D3).

### Frontend (Phase 2: 10 resolved, 3 active/new)

1. ~~Three-way i18n mismatch~~ — **RESOLVED 2026-09-06:** Skeleton pattern kept (build-time vue-i18n from JSON). BE loads resources/lang/{sk,en,uk}/app.json + validation.json via AppServiceProvider::loadJsonTranslations as app.<key>. FE app.ts imports all three locales, uses useI18n().t(key, fallback) throughout (no shared translations prop). LocaleMiddleware + GET /language/{locale} handle switching (full reload). No reintroduction of translations shared prop.

No Pinia (will add phase 5+ for notifications polling). Shared can auto-derived from PermissionEnum::sharedKey (Str::camel), unidirectional per-permission (no hand-maintained list). Shared props include tenant{active,available}, tenantColors, auth.user.locale, languages (3 items incl uk), navigation. Single types/index.d.ts SharedProps interface (collapsed D1); no types/inertia.d.ts file.

FileUploadInput works (multipart to /uploads, uuid validation, moveToModel contract). EmptyState + PageHeader → mapped to skeleton components. No ESLint ref ban (follow skeleton style).
